```
   131→def triage_phase_banner(plan: PlanModel) -> str:
   132→    """Return a banner string when triage stage IDs are in the queue."""
   133→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   134→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   135→    has_triage = any(sid in order for sid in TRIAGE_IDS)
   136→    if not has_triage:
   137→        return ""
   138→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   139→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   140→    completed = [s for s in ("observe", "reflect", "organize") if s in stages]
   141→    if completed:
   142→        return (
   143→            f"TRIAGE MODE ({len(completed)}/4 stages complete) — "
   144→            "complete all stages to exit. Run: desloppify plan triage"
   145→        )
   146→    return (
   147→        "TRIAGE MODE — review issues need analysis before fixing. "
   148→        "Run: desloppify plan triage"
   149→    )
   150→
   151→
```

```
     2→

     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
    10→from desloppify.engine.plan import (
    11→    append_log_entry,
    12→    build_triage_prompt,

    18→)
    19→
    20→
    21→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    22→class TriageServices:
    23→    """Callables shared across triage handler modules."""
    24→
    25→    command_runtime: Callable[[argparse.Namespace], Any]
    26→    load_plan: Callable[..., dict]
    27→    save_plan: Callable[..., None]
    28→    collect_triage_input: Callable[[dict, dict], Any]
    29→    detect_recurring_patterns: Callable[[dict, dict], dict]
    30→    append_log_entry: Callable[..., Any]
    31→    extract_issue_citations: Callable[[str, set[str]], set[str]]
    32→    build_triage_prompt: Callable[[Any], str]
    33→
    34→
    35→def default_triage_services() -> TriageServices:
    36→    """Return the default runtime triage service bundle."""
    37→    return TriageServices(
    38→        command_runtime=command_runtime,
    39→        load_plan=load_plan,
    40→        save_plan=save_plan,
    41→        collect_triage_input=collect_triage_input,

    46→    )
```

```
1→"""State mutation helpers for triage stage records."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.state import utc_now
     6→
     7→from .helpers import cascade_clear_later_confirmations
     8→
     9→
    10→def _resolve_reusable_report(
    11→    report: str | None,
    12→    existing_stage: dict | None,
    13→) -> tuple[str | None, bool]:
    14→    if report:
    15→        return report, False
    16→    if existing_stage and existing_stage.get("report"):
    17→        return existing_stage["report"], True
    18→    return None, False
    19→
    20→
    21→def _record_observe_stage(
    22→    stages: dict,
    23→    *,
    24→    report: str,
    25→    issue_count: int,
    26→    cited_ids: list[str],
    27→    existing_stage: dict | None,
    28→    is_reuse: bool,
    29→) -> list[str]:
    30→    stages["observe"] = {
    31→        "stage": "observe",
    32→        "report": report,
    33→        "cited_ids": cited_ids,
    34→        "timestamp": utc_now(),
    35→        "issue_count": issue_count,
    36→    }
    37→    if is_reuse and existing_stage and existing_stage.get("confirmed_at"):
    38→        stages["observe"]["confirmed_at"] = existing_stage["confirmed_at"]
    39→        stages["observe"]["confirmed_text"] = existing_stage.get("confirmed_text", "")
    40→    cleared = cascade_clear_later_confirmations(stages, "observe")
    41→    if not is_reuse:
    42→        stages["observe"].pop("confirmed_at", None)
    43→        stages["observe"].pop("confirmed_text", None)
    44→    return cleared
    45→
    46→
    47→def _record_organize_stage(
    48→    stages: dict,
    49→    *,
    50→    report: str,
    51→    issue_count: int,
    52→    existing_stage: dict | None,
    53→    is_reuse: bool,
    54→) -> list[str]:
    55→    stages["organize"] = {
    56→        "stage": "organize",
    57→        "report": report,
    58→        "cited_ids": [],
    59→        "timestamp": utc_now(),
    60→        "issue_count": issue_count,
    61→    }
    62→    if is_reuse and existing_stage and existing_stage.get("confirmed_at"):
    63→        stages["organize"]["confirmed_at"] = existing_stage["confirmed_at"]
    64→        stages["organize"]["confirmed_text"] = existing_stage.get("confirmed_text", "")
    65→    return cascade_clear_later_confirmations(stages, "organize")
    66→
    67→
    68→def _record_enrich_stage(
    69→    stages: dict,
    70→    *,
    71→    report: str,
    72→    shallow_count: int,
    73→    existing_stage: dict | None,
    74→    is_reuse: bool,
    75→) -> list[str]:
    76→    stages["enrich"] = {
    77→        "stage": "enrich",
    78→        "report": report,
    79→        "timestamp": utc_now(),
    80→        "shallow_count": shallow_count,
    81→    }
    82→    if is_reuse and existing_stage and existing_stage.get("confirmed_at"):
    83→        stages["enrich"]["confirmed_at"] = existing_stage["confirmed_at"]
    84→        stages["enrich"]["confirmed_text"] = existing_stage.get("confirmed_text", "")
    85→    return cascade_clear_later_confirmations(stages, "enrich")
    86→
    87→
    88→def _record_confirm_existing_completion(
    89→    *,
    90→    stages: dict,
    91→    note: str,
    92→    issue_count: int,
    93→    confirmed_text: str,
    94→) -> None:
    95→    stages["organize"] = {
    96→        "stage": "organize",
    97→        "report": f"[confirmed-existing] {note}",
    98→        "cited_ids": [],
    99→        "timestamp": utc_now(),
   100→        "issue_count": issue_count,
   101→        "confirmed_at": utc_now(),
   102→        "confirmed_text": confirmed_text,
   103→    }
   104→
   105→
   106→__all__ = [
   107→    "_record_confirm_existing_completion",
   108→    "_record_enrich_stage",
   109→    "_record_observe_stage",
   110→    "_record_organize_stage",
   111→    "_resolve_reusable_report",
   112→]
   113→
```

```
1→"""Subprocess-oriented runner helpers for review batch execution."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import os
     6→import sys
     7→from pathlib import Path
     8→
     9→from ._runner_process_attempts import (
    10→    _handle_early_attempt_return,
    11→    _handle_failed_attempt,
    12→    _handle_successful_attempt,
    13→    _handle_timeout_or_stall,
    14→    _resolve_retry_config,
    15→    _run_batch_attempt,
    16→)
    17→from ._runner_process_io import _extract_payload_from_log  # noqa: F401 (runner_parallel import)
    18→from ._runner_process_types import (
    19→    CodexBatchRunnerDeps,
    20→    FollowupScanDeps,
    21→)
    22→
    23→
    24→def codex_batch_command(*, prompt: str, repo_root: Path, output_file: Path) -> list[str]:
    25→    """Build one codex exec command line for a batch prompt."""
    26→    effort = os.environ.get("DESLOPPIFY_CODEX_REASONING_EFFORT", "low").strip().lower()
    27→    if effort not in {"low", "medium", "high", "xhigh"}:
    28→        effort = "low"
    29→    return [
    30→        "codex",
    31→        "exec",
    32→        "--ephemeral",
    33→        "-C",
    34→        str(repo_root),
    35→        "-s",
    36→        "workspace-write",
    37→        "-c",
    38→        'approval_policy="never"',
    39→        "-c",
    40→        f'model_reasoning_effort="{effort}"',
    41→        "-o",
    42→        str(output_file),
    43→        prompt,
    44→    ]
    45→
    46→
    47→def run_codex_batch(
    48→    *,
    49→    prompt: str,
    50→    repo_root: Path,
    51→    output_file: Path,
    52→    log_file: Path,
    53→    deps: CodexBatchRunnerDeps,
    54→    codex_batch_command_fn=None,
    55→) -> int:
    56→    """Execute one codex batch and return a stable CLI-style status code."""
    57→    if codex_batch_command_fn is None:
    58→        codex_batch_command_fn = codex_batch_command
    59→    cmd = codex_batch_command_fn(
    60→        prompt=prompt,
    61→        repo_root=repo_root,
    62→        output_file=output_file,
    63→    )
    64→    config = _resolve_retry_config(deps)
    65→    log_sections: list[str] = []
    66→
    67→    for attempt in range(1, config.max_attempts + 1):
    68→        header, result = _run_batch_attempt(
    69→            cmd=cmd,
    70→            deps=deps,
    71→            output_file=output_file,
    72→            log_file=log_file,
    73→            log_sections=log_sections,
    74→            attempt=attempt,
    75→            max_attempts=config.max_attempts,
    76→            use_popen=config.use_popen,
    77→            live_log_interval=config.live_log_interval,
    78→            stall_seconds=config.stall_seconds,
    79→        )
    80→        early_return = _handle_early_attempt_return(result)
    81→        if early_return is not None:
    82→            return early_return
    83→        timeout_or_stall = _handle_timeout_or_stall(
    84→            header=header,
    85→            result=result,
    86→            deps=deps,
    87→            output_file=output_file,
    88→            log_file=log_file,
    89→            log_sections=log_sections,
    90→            stall_seconds=config.stall_seconds,
    91→        )
    92→        if timeout_or_stall is not None:
    93→            return timeout_or_stall
    94→
    95→        log_sections.append(
    96→            f"{header}\n\nSTDOUT:\n{result.stdout_text}\n\nSTDERR:\n{result.stderr_text}\n"
    97→        )
    98→
    99→        success_code = _handle_successful_attempt(
   100→            result=result,
   101→            output_file=output_file,
   102→            log_file=log_file,
   103→            deps=deps,
   104→            log_sections=log_sections,
   105→        )
   106→        if success_code is not None:
   107→            return success_code
   108→        failure_code = _handle_failed_attempt(
   109→            result=result,
   110→            deps=deps,
   111→            attempt=attempt,
   112→            max_attempts=config.max_attempts,
   113→            retry_backoff_seconds=config.retry_backoff_seconds,
   114→            log_file=log_file,
   115→            log_sections=log_sections,
   116→        )
   117→        if failure_code is not None:
   118→            return failure_code
   119→
   120→    deps.safe_write_text_fn(log_file, "\n\n".join(log_sections))
   121→    return 1
   122→
   123→
   124→def run_followup_scan(
   125→    *,
   126→    lang_name: str,
   127→    scan_path: str,
   128→    deps: FollowupScanDeps,
   129→    force_queue_bypass: bool = False,
   130→) -> int:
   131→    """Run a follow-up scan and return a non-zero status when it fails."""
   132→    scan_cmd = [
   133→        deps.python_executable,
   134→        "-m",
   135→        "desloppify",
   136→        "--lang",
   137→        lang_name,
   138→        "scan",
   139→        "--path",
   140→        scan_path,
   141→    ]
   142→    if force_queue_bypass:
   143→        followup_attest = (
   144→            "I understand this is not the intended workflow and "
   145→            "I am intentionally skipping queue completion"
   146→        )
   147→        scan_cmd.extend(["--force-rescan", "--attest", followup_attest])
   148→        print(
   149→            deps.colorize_fn(
   150→                "  Follow-up scan queue bypass enabled (--force-followup-scan).",
   151→                "yellow",
   152→            )
   153→        )
   154→    print(deps.colorize_fn("\n  Running follow-up scan...", "bold"))
   155→    try:
   156→        result = deps.subprocess_run(
   157→            scan_cmd,
   158→            cwd=str(deps.project_root),
   159→            timeout=deps.timeout_seconds,
   160→        )
   161→    except deps.timeout_error:
   162→        print(
   163→            deps.colorize_fn(
   164→                f"  Follow-up scan timed out after {deps.timeout_seconds}s.",
   165→                "yellow",
   166→            ),
   167→            file=sys.stderr,
   168→        )
   169→        return 124
   170→    except OSError as exc:
   171→        print(
   172→            deps.colorize_fn(f"  Follow-up scan failed: {exc}", "red"),
   173→            file=sys.stderr,
   174→        )
   175→        return 1
   176→    return int(getattr(result, "returncode", 0) or 0)
   177→
   178→
   179→__all__ = [
   180→    "CodexBatchRunnerDeps",
   181→    "FollowupScanDeps",
   182→    "_extract_payload_from_log",
   183→    "codex_batch_command",
   184→    "run_codex_batch",
   185→    "run_followup_scan",
   186→]
   187→
```

```
    24→def _validate_attestation(
    25→    attestation: str,
    26→    stage: str,
    27→    *,
    28→    dimensions: list[str] | None = None,
    29→    cluster_names: list[str] | None = None,
    30→) -> str | None:
    31→    """Return error message if attestation doesn't reference required data, else None."""
    32→    text = attestation.lower()
    33→
    34→    if stage == "observe":
    35→        if dimensions:
    36→            found = [d for d in dimensions if d.lower().replace("_", " ") in text or d.lower() in text]
    37→            if not found:
    38→                dim_list = ", ".join(dimensions[:6])
    39→                return f"Attestation must reference at least one dimension from the summary. Mention one of: {dim_list}"
    40→
    41→    elif stage == "reflect":
    42→        refs: list[str] = []
    43→        if dimensions:
    44→            refs.extend(d for d in dimensions if d.lower().replace("_", " ") in text or d.lower() in text)
    45→        if cluster_names:
    46→            refs.extend(n for n in cluster_names if n.lower() in text)
    47→        if not refs and (dimensions or cluster_names):
    48→            return (
    49→                f"Attestation must reference at least one dimension or cluster name.\n"
    50→                f"  Valid dimensions: {', '.join((dimensions or [])[:6])}\n"
    51→                f"  Valid clusters: {', '.join((cluster_names or [])[:6]) if cluster_names else '(none yet)'}"
    52→            )
    53→
    54→    elif stage == "organize":
    55→        if cluster_names:
    56→            found = [n for n in cluster_names if n.lower() in text]
    57→            if not found:
    58→                names = ", ".join(cluster_names[:6])
    59→                return f"Attestation must reference at least one cluster from the plan. Mention one of: {names}"
    60→
    61→    elif stage == "enrich":
    62→        if cluster_names:
    63→            found = [n for n in cluster_names if n.lower() in text]
    64→            if not found:
    65→                names = ", ".join(cluster_names[:6])
    66→                return f"Attestation must reference at least one cluster you enriched. Mention one of: {names}"
    67→
    68→    return None
    69→

   427→def _cmd_confirm_stage(
   428→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   429→    *,
   430→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   431→) -> None:
   432→    """Router for ``--confirm observe/reflect/organize/enrich``."""
   433→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
   434→    confirm_stage = getattr(args, "confirm", None)
   435→    attestation = getattr(args, "attestation", None)
   436→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   437→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   438→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   439→
   440→    if confirm_stage == "observe":
   441→        _confirm_observe(args, plan, stages, attestation, services=resolved_services)
   442→    elif confirm_stage == "reflect":
   443→        _confirm_reflect(args, plan, stages, attestation, services=resolved_services)
   444→    elif confirm_stage == "organize":
   445→        _confirm_organize(args, plan, stages, attestation, services=resolved_services)
   446→    elif confirm_stage == "enrich":
   447→        _confirm_enrich(args, plan, stages, attestation, services=resolved_services)
   448→
   449→
   450→MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN = _MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN
   451→validate_attestation = _validate_attestation
   452→
   453→
   454→def cmd_confirm_stage(
   455→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   456→    *,
   457→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   458→) -> None:
   459→    """Public triage confirmation entrypoint."""
   460→    _cmd_confirm_stage(args, services=services)
   461→
   462→__all__ = [
   463→    "MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN",
   464→    "cmd_confirm_stage",
   465→    "validate_attestation",
   466→    "_MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN",

   473→]
```

```
    48→        else:

    68→def print_progress(plan: dict, open_issues: dict) -> None:

    98→

   100→    for c in clusters.values():

   363→def _print_prior_stage_reports(stages: dict) -> None:
   364→    """Print prior stage reports (observe/reflect) as context for current action."""
   365→    if "observe" in stages:
   366→        obs_report = stages["observe"].get("report", "")
   367→        if obs_report:
   368→            print(colorize("\n  Your observe analysis:", "dim"))
   369→            for line in obs_report.strip().splitlines()[:8]:
   370→                print(colorize(f"    {line}", "dim"))
   371→            if len(obs_report.strip().splitlines()) > 8:
   372→                print(colorize("    ...", "dim"))
   373→    if "reflect" in stages:
   374→        ref_report = stages["reflect"].get("report", "")
   375→        if ref_report:

   383→def _print_issues_by_dimension(open_issues: dict) -> None:
   384→    """Print issues grouped by dimension with suggestions to surface contradictions."""
   385→    by_dim: dict[str, list[tuple[str, dict]]] = defaultdict(list)
   386→    for fid, f in open_issues.items():
   387→        detail = f.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(f.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   388→        dim = detail.get("dimension", "unknown")
   389→        by_dim[dim].append((fid, f))
   390→
   391→    print(colorize("\n  Review issues by dimension:", "cyan"))
   392→    print(colorize("  (Look for contradictions: issues in the same dimension that", "dim"))
   393→    print(colorize("  recommend opposite changes. These must be resolved before clustering.)", "dim"))
   394→    max_per_dim = 5
   395→    for dim in sorted(by_dim, key=lambda d: (-len(by_dim[d]), d)):
   396→        items = by_dim[dim]
   397→        print(colorize(f"\n    {dim} ({len(items)}):", "bold"))
   398→        for fid, f in items[:max_per_dim]:
   399→            summary = f.get("summary", "")
   400→            short = short_issue_id(fid)
   401→            detail = f.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(f.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   402→            suggestion = (detail.get("suggestion") or "")[:120]
   403→            print(f"      [{short}] {summary}")
   404→            if suggestion:
   405→                print(colorize(f"        \u2192 {suggestion}", "dim"))
   406→        if len(items) > max_per_dim:
   407→            print(colorize(f"      ... and {len(items) - max_per_dim} more", "dim"))
   408→    print(colorize("\n  Use hash in commands: desloppify plan skip <hash>  |  desloppify show <hash>", "dim"))
   409→

   411→def cmd_triage_dashboard(
   412→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   413→    *,
   414→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   415→) -> None:
   416→    """Default view: show issues, stage progress, and next command."""
   417→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
   418→    runtime = resolved_services.command_runtime(args)
   419→    state = runtime.state
   420→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   421→    si = resolved_services.collect_triage_input(plan, state)
   422→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   423→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   424→
   425→    _print_dashboard_header(si, stages, meta, plan)
   426→    _print_action_guidance(stages, meta, si, plan)
   427→    _print_prior_stage_reports(stages)
   428→    _print_issues_by_dimension(si.open_issues)
   429→
   430→    # Show reflect dashboard when observe done, reflect not done
   431→    if "observe" in stages and "reflect" not in stages:
   432→        print_reflect_dashboard(si, plan, services=resolved_services)
   433→
   434→    # Show current cluster progress
   435→    print_progress(plan, si.open_issues)
   436→
   437→def show_plan_summary(plan: dict, state: dict) -> None:
   438→    """Print a compact plan rendering: clusters + queue order + coverage."""
   439→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   440→    active = {
   441→        n: c for n, c in clusters.items()
   442→        if c.get("issue_ids") and not c.get("auto")
   443→    }
   444→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
   445→
   446→    if active:
   447→        print(colorize(f"\n  Clusters ({len(active)}):", "bold"))
   448→        for name, cluster in active.items():
   449→            count = len(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   450→            steps = len(cluster.get("action_steps", []))
   451→            desc = (cluster.get("description") or "")[:60]
   452→            print(f"    {name}: {count} items, {steps} steps — {desc}")
   453→
   454→    queue_order = [
   455→        fid for fid in plan.get("queue_order", [])
   456→        if not fid.startswith("triage::") and not fid.startswith("workflow::")
   457→    ]
   458→    if queue_order:
   459→        show = min(15, len(queue_order))
   460→        print(colorize(f"\n  Queue order (first {show} of {len(queue_order)}):", "bold"))
   461→        for i, fid in enumerate(queue_order[:show]):
   462→            f = issues.get(fid, {})
   463→            summary = (f.get("summary") or fid)[:60]
   464→            detector = f.get("detector", "?")
   465→            cn = find_cluster_for(fid, active)
   466→            print(f"    {i+1}. [{detector}] {summary}{f' ({cn})' if cn else ''}")
   467→
   468→    organized, total, _ = triage_coverage(
   469→        plan, open_review_ids=open_review_ids_from_state(state),
   470→    )
   471→    pct = int(organized / total * 100) if total else 0
   472→    print(colorize(f"\n  Coverage: {organized}/{total} in clusters ({pct}%)", "bold"))
   473→
   474→__all__ = [
   475→    "cmd_triage_dashboard",
   476→    "print_organize_result",
   477→    "print_progress",
   478→    "print_reflect_dashboard",
   479→    "print_reflect_result",
   480→    "print_stage_progress",
   481→    "show_plan_summary",
   482→]
   483→
```

```
   111→def parse_batch_selection(raw: str | None, batch_count: int) -> list[int]:
   112→    """Parse optional 1-based CSV list of batches."""
   113→    if not raw:
   114→        return list(range(batch_count))
   115→
   116→    selected: list[int] = []
   117→    seen: set[int] = set()
   118→    for token in raw.split(","):
   119→        text = token.strip()
   120→        if not text:
   121→            continue
   122→        idx_1 = int(text)
   123→        if idx_1 < 1 or idx_1 > batch_count:
   124→            raise ValueError(f"batch index {idx_1} out of range 1..{batch_count}")
   125→        idx_0 = idx_1 - 1
   126→        if idx_0 in seen:
   127→            continue
   128→        seen.add(idx_0)
   129→        selected.append(idx_0)
   130→    return selected
   131→
   132→
   133→def extract_json_payload(raw: str, *, log_fn) -> dict[str, object] | None:
   134→    """Best-effort extraction of first JSON object from agent output text."""
   135→    text = raw.strip()
   136→    if not text:
   137→        return None
   138→
   139→    decoder = json.JSONDecoder()
   140→    last_decode_error: json.JSONDecodeError | None = None
   141→    for start, ch in enumerate(text):
   142→        if ch not in "{[":
   143→            continue
   144→        try:
   145→            obj, _ = decoder.raw_decode(text[start:])
   146→        except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
   147→            last_decode_error = exc
   148→            continue
   149→        if (
   150→            isinstance(obj, dict)
   151→            and isinstance(obj.get("assessments"), dict)
   152→            and isinstance(obj.get("issues"), list)
   153→        ):
   154→            return obj
   155→    if last_decode_error is not None:
   156→        log_fn(f"  batch output JSON parse failed: {last_decode_error.msg}")
   157→    else:
   158→        log_fn("  batch output JSON parse failed: no valid payload found")
   159→    return None
   160→
   161→
   162→def _validate_dimension_note(
   163→    key: str,
   164→    note_raw: object,
   165→) -> tuple[list[object], str, str, str, str]:
   166→    """Validate a single dimension_notes entry and return parsed fields.
   167→
   168→    Returns (evidence, impact_scope, fix_scope, confidence, issues_preventing_higher_score).
   169→    Raises ValueError on invalid structure.
   170→    """
   171→    if not isinstance(note_raw, dict):
   172→        raise ValueError(
   173→            f"dimension_notes missing object for assessed dimension: {key}"
   174→        )
   175→    evidence = note_raw.get("evidence")
   176→    impact_scope = note_raw.get("impact_scope")
   177→    fix_scope = note_raw.get("fix_scope")
   178→    if not isinstance(evidence, list) or not evidence:
   179→        raise ValueError(
   180→            f"dimension_notes.{key}.evidence must be a non-empty array"
   181→        )
   182→    if not isinstance(impact_scope, str) or not impact_scope.strip():
   183→        raise ValueError(
   184→            f"dimension_notes.{key}.impact_scope must be a non-empty string"
   185→        )
   186→    if not isinstance(fix_scope, str) or not fix_scope.strip():
   187→        raise ValueError(
   188→            f"dimension_notes.{key}.fix_scope must be a non-empty string"
   189→        )
   190→
   191→    confidence_raw = str(note_raw.get("confidence", "medium")).strip().lower()
   192→    confidence = (
   193→        confidence_raw if confidence_raw in {"high", "medium", "low"} else "medium"
   194→    )
   195→    issues_note = str(note_raw.get(DIMENSION_NOTE_ISSUES_KEY, "")).strip()
   196→    if not issues_note:
   197→        issues_note = str(note_raw.get(LEGACY_DIMENSION_NOTE_ISSUES_KEY, "")).strip()
   198→    return evidence, impact_scope, fix_scope, confidence, issues_note
   199→
   200→
```

```
1→"""Epic triage orchestrator and public API."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→import logging
     7→from dataclasses import dataclass
     8→from typing import Any
     9→
    10→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    11→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    12→
    13→from .epic_triage_apply import TriageMutationResult, apply_triage_to_plan
    14→from .epic_triage_parsing import (
    15→    ISSUE_ID_RE,
    16→    extract_issue_citations,
    17→    parse_triage_result,
    18→)
    19→from .epic_triage_prompt import (
    20→    _TRIAGE_SYSTEM_PROMPT,
    21→    ContradictionNote,
    22→    DismissedIssue,
    23→    TriageInput,
    24→    TriageResult,
    25→    build_triage_prompt,
    26→    collect_triage_input,
    27→)
    28→
    29→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    30→
    31→
    32→def last_real_review_timestamp(state: dict) -> str | None:
    33→    """ISO timestamp of most recent genuine review import (not manual override/scan reset)."""
    34→    REAL_MODES = {"holistic", "per_file", "trusted_internal", "attested_external"}
    35→    audit = state.get("assessment_import_audit", [])
    36→    if isinstance(audit, list):
    37→        for entry in reversed(audit):
    38→            if isinstance(entry, dict) and entry.get("mode") in REAL_MODES:
    39→                ts = entry.get("timestamp")
    40→                if ts:
    41→                    return str(ts)
    42→    holistic = (state.get("review_cache") or {}).get("holistic")
    43→    if isinstance(holistic, dict):
    44→        return holistic.get("reviewed_at")
    45→    return None
    46→
    47→def detect_recurring_patterns(
    48→    open_issues: dict[str, dict],
    49→    resolved_issues: dict[str, dict],
    50→) -> dict[str, dict]:
    51→    """Detect dimensions with both resolved AND current open issues.
    52→
    53→    Returns ``{dimension: {"open": [ids], "resolved": [ids]}}``.
    54→    A dimension with both resolved and open issues signals a potential
    55→    loop — similar issues recur after previous fixes.
    56→    """
    57→    def _dimension(f: dict) -> str:
    58→        detail = f.get("detail", {})
    59→        if isinstance(detail, dict):
    60→            return detail.get("dimension", "")
    61→        return ""
    62→
    63→    open_by_dim: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    64→    for fid, f in open_issues.items():
    65→        dim = _dimension(f)
    66→        if dim:
    67→            open_by_dim.setdefault(dim, []).append(fid)
    68→
    69→    resolved_by_dim: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    70→    for fid, f in resolved_issues.items():
    71→        dim = _dimension(f)
    72→        if dim:
    73→            resolved_by_dim.setdefault(dim, []).append(fid)
    74→
    75→    recurring: dict[str, dict] = {}
    76→    for dim in set(open_by_dim) & set(resolved_by_dim):
    77→        recurring[dim] = {
    78→            "open": open_by_dim[dim],
    79→            "resolved": resolved_by_dim[dim],
    80→        }
    81→    return recurring
    82→
    83→@dataclass
    84→class TriageDeps:
    85→    """Injectable dependencies for the triage engine."""
    86→
    87→    llm_call: Any = None  # Callable[[str, str], str] — (system, user) -> response
    88→
    89→def triage_epics(
    90→    plan: PlanModel,
    91→    state: StateModel,
    92→    *,
    93→    deps: TriageDeps | None = None,
    94→    dry_run: bool = False,
    95→    trigger: str = "manual",
    96→) -> TriageMutationResult:
    97→    """Run epic triage: collect input, call LLM, apply results.
    98→
    99→    If ``dry_run`` is True, collects input and builds prompt but does not
   100→    call the LLM or mutate the plan.
   101→
   102→    If ``deps.llm_call`` is None, returns a dry-run result with the prompt.
   103→    """
   104→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   105→    si = collect_triage_input(plan, state)
   106→
   107→    prompt = build_triage_prompt(si)
   108→    valid_ids = set(si.open_issues.keys())
   109→
   110→    if dry_run or deps is None or deps.llm_call is None:
   111→        result = TriageMutationResult(dry_run=True)
   112→        result.strategy_summary = f"[dry-run] Prompt built with {len(si.open_issues)} issues"
   113→        return result
   114→
   115→    # Call LLM
   116→    try:
   117→        raw_response = deps.llm_call(_TRIAGE_SYSTEM_PROMPT, prompt)
   118→        raw_json = json.loads(raw_response)
   119→    except (json.JSONDecodeError, TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
   120→        logger.error("Epic triage LLM response parse error: %s", exc)
   121→        result = TriageMutationResult()
   122→        result.strategy_summary = f"Triage failed: {exc}"
   123→        return result
   124→
   125→    triage = parse_triage_result(raw_json, valid_ids)
   126→    return apply_triage_to_plan(plan, state, triage, trigger=trigger)
   127→
   128→__all__ = [
   129→    "ContradictionNote",
   130→    "DismissedIssue",
   131→    "ISSUE_ID_RE",
   132→    "TriageDeps",
   133→    "TriageInput",
```

```
   157→def _cmd_stage_reflect(
   158→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   159→    *,
   160→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   161→) -> None:
   162→    """Record the REFLECT stage: compare current issues against completed work.
   163→
   164→    Forces the agent to consider what was previously resolved and whether
   165→    similar issues are recurring. Requires a 100-char report (50 if ≤3 issues).
   166→    If recurring patterns are detected, the report must mention at least one
   167→    recurring dimension name.
   168→    """
   169→    report: str | None = getattr(args, "report", None)
   170→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attestation", None)
   171→
   172→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
   173→    runtime = resolved_services.command_runtime(args)
   174→    state = runtime.state
   175→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   176→
   177→    if not has_triage_in_queue(plan):
   178→        print(colorize("  No planning stages in the queue — nothing to reflect on.", "yellow"))
   179→        return
   180→
   181→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   182→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   183→
   184→    # Jump-back: reuse existing report if no --report provided
   185→    existing_stage = stages.get("reflect")
   186→    is_reuse = False
   187→    if not report and existing_stage and existing_stage.get("report"):
   188→        report = existing_stage["report"]
   189→        is_reuse = True
   190→    elif not report:
   191→        _print_reflect_report_requirement()
   192→        return
   193→
   194→    if "observe" not in stages:
   195→        print(colorize("  Cannot reflect: observe stage not complete.", "red"))
   196→        print(colorize('  Run: desloppify plan triage --stage observe --report "..."', "dim"))
   197→        return
   198→
   199→    si = resolved_services.collect_triage_input(plan, state)
   200→
   201→    # Fold-confirm: auto-confirm observe if attestation provided
   202→    if not _auto_confirm_observe_if_attested(
   203→        plan=plan,
   204→        stages=stages,
   205→        attestation=attestation,
   206→        triage_input=si,
   207→    ):
   208→        return
   209→
   210→    issue_count = len(si.open_issues)
   211→
   212→    # Validation: report length
   213→    min_chars = 50 if issue_count <= 3 else 100
   214→    if len(report) < min_chars:
   215→        print(colorize(f"  Report too short: {len(report)} chars (minimum {min_chars}).", "red"))
   216→        print(colorize("  Describe how current issues relate to previously completed work.", "dim"))
   217→        return
   218→
   219→    # Detect recurring patterns
   220→    recurring = resolved_services.detect_recurring_patterns(
   221→        si.open_issues,
   222→        si.resolved_issues,
   223→    )
   224→    recurring_dims = sorted(recurring.keys())
   225→
   226→    # If recurring patterns exist, report must mention at least one dimension
   227→    if not _validate_recurring_dimension_mentions(
   228→        report=report,
   229→        recurring_dims=recurring_dims,
   230→        recurring=recurring,
   231→    ):
   232→        return
   233→
   234→    # Save stage
   235→    stages = meta.setdefault("triage_stages", {})
   236→    reflect_stage = {
   237→        "stage": "reflect",
   238→        "report": report,
   239→        "cited_ids": [],
   240→        "timestamp": utc_now(),
   241→        "issue_count": issue_count,
   242→    }
   243→    reflect_stage["recurring_dims"] = recurring_dims
   244→    stages["reflect"] = reflect_stage
   245→
   246→    # Jump-back: preserve or clear confirmation
   247→    if is_reuse and existing_stage and existing_stage.get("confirmed_at"):
   248→        stages["reflect"]["confirmed_at"] = existing_stage["confirmed_at"]
   249→        stages["reflect"]["confirmed_text"] = existing_stage.get("confirmed_text", "")
   250→    cleared = cascade_clear_later_confirmations(stages, "reflect")
   251→
   252→    resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
   253→
   254→    reflect_detail = {
   255→        "issue_count": issue_count,
   256→        "reuse": is_reuse,
   257→    }
   258→    reflect_detail["recurring_dims"] = recurring_dims
   259→    resolved_services.append_log_entry(
   260→        plan,
   261→        "triage_reflect",
   262→        actor="user",
   263→        detail=reflect_detail,
   264→    )
   265→    resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
   266→
   267→    print_reflect_result(
   268→        issue_count=issue_count,
   269→        recurring_dims=recurring_dims,
   270→        recurring=recurring,
   271→        report=report,
   272→        is_reuse=is_reuse,

   278→def _cmd_stage_organize(
   279→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   280→    *,
   281→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   282→) -> None:
   283→    """Record the ORGANIZE stage: validates cluster enrichment.
   284→
   285→    Instead of gating on a text report, validates that the plan data
   286→    itself has been enriched: each manual cluster needs description +
   287→    action_steps. This forces the agent to actually think about each
   288→    cluster's execution plan.
   289→    """
   290→    report: str | None = getattr(args, "report", None)
   291→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attestation", None)
   292→
   293→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
   294→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   295→
   296→    if not has_triage_in_queue(plan):
   297→        print(colorize("  No planning stages in the queue — nothing to organize.", "yellow"))
   298→        return
   299→
   300→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   301→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   302→
   303→    # Jump-back: reuse existing report if no --report provided
   304→    existing_stage = stages.get("organize")
   305→    is_reuse = False
   306→    if not report and existing_stage and existing_stage.get("report"):
   307→        report = existing_stage["report"]
   308→        is_reuse = True
   309→
   310→    if not _require_reflect_stage_for_organize(stages):
   311→        return
   312→
   313→    # Fold-confirm: auto-confirm reflect if attestation provided
   314→    if not _auto_confirm_reflect_for_organize(
   315→        args=args,
   316→        plan=plan,
   317→        stages=stages,
   318→        attestation=attestation,
   319→    ):
   320→        return
   321→
   322→    # Validate: at least 1 manual cluster with issues
   323→    manual_clusters = _manual_clusters_or_error(plan)
   324→    if manual_clusters is None:
   325→        return
   326→
   327→    # Validate: all manual clusters are enriched
   328→    if not _clusters_enriched_or_error(plan):
   329→        return
   330→
   331→    # Validate: all review issues are in a manual cluster (or wontfixed)
   332→    state = resolved_services.command_runtime(args).state
   333→    if not _unclustered_review_issues_or_error(plan, state):
   334→        return
   335→
   336→    # Warn if few/no cluster operations since reflect
   337→    reflect_ts = stages.get("reflect", {}).get("timestamp", "")
   338→    if reflect_ts and not is_reuse:
   339→        activity = count_log_activity_since(plan, reflect_ts)
   340→        cluster_ops = sum(
   341→            activity.get(k, 0)
   342→            for k in ("cluster_create", "cluster_add", "cluster_update", "cluster_remove")
   343→        )
   344→        if cluster_ops == 0:
   345→            print(colorize("  Warning: no cluster operations logged since reflect.", "yellow"))
   346→            print(colorize("  Did you create clusters, add issues, and enrich them?", "yellow"))
   347→            print(colorize("  The organize stage should reflect real work — not just recording a report.", "dim"))
   348→            print()
   349→        elif cluster_ops < 3:
   350→            print(colorize(f"  Note: only {cluster_ops} cluster operation(s) logged since reflect.", "yellow"))
   351→            print(colorize("  Make sure all clusters are properly set up before proceeding.", "dim"))
   352→            print()
   353→
   354→    report = _organize_report_or_error(report)
   355→    if report is None:
   356→        return
   357→
   358→    stages = meta.setdefault("triage_stages", {})
   359→    cleared = _record_organize_stage(
   360→        stages,
   361→        report=report,
   362→        issue_count=len(manual_clusters),
   363→        existing_stage=existing_stage,
   364→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   365→    )
   366→
   367→    resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
   368→
   369→    resolved_services.append_log_entry(
   370→        plan,
   371→        "triage_organize",
   372→        actor="user",
   373→        detail={"cluster_count": len(manual_clusters), "reuse": is_reuse},
   374→    )
   375→    resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
   376→
   377→    print_organize_result(
   378→        manual_clusters=manual_clusters,
   379→        plan=plan,
   380→        report=report,
   381→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   382→        cleared=cleared,
   383→        stages=stages,
   384→    )

   386→
   387→def _cmd_stage_enrich(
   388→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   389→    *,
   390→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   391→) -> None:
   392→    """Record the ENRICH stage: validate steps have detail/refs for executor readiness.
   393→
   394→    This stage sits between organize and commit. It checks whether action steps
   395→    are detailed enough for an executor with zero context. Steps should have
   396→    ``detail`` (sub-points) and/or ``issue_refs`` (for auto-completion).
   397→
   398→    The agent can still go back and organize (add/remove clusters, reorder)
   399→    at this point — the stage just validates step quality.
   400→    """
   401→    report: str | None = getattr(args, "report", None)
   402→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attestation", None)
   403→
   404→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
   405→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   406→
   407→    if not has_triage_in_queue(plan):
   408→        print(colorize("  No planning stages in the queue — nothing to enrich.", "yellow"))
   409→        return
   410→
   411→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   412→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   413→
   414→    # Jump-back: reuse existing report if no --report provided
   415→    existing_stage = stages.get("enrich")
   416→    report, is_reuse = _resolve_reusable_report(report, existing_stage)
   417→
   418→    if not _require_organize_stage_for_enrich(stages):
   419→        return
   420→
   421→    # Auto-confirm organize if attestation provided
   422→    if not stages.get("organize", {}).get("confirmed_at"):
   423→        if attestation:
   424→            from ._stage_validation import _auto_confirm_organize_for_complete
   425→            if not _auto_confirm_organize_for_complete(
   426→                plan=plan, stages=stages, attestation=attestation,
   427→            ):
   428→                return
   429→        else:
   430→            print(colorize("  Cannot enrich: organize stage not confirmed.", "red"))
   431→            print(colorize("  Run: desloppify plan triage --confirm organize", "dim"))
   432→            print(colorize("  Or pass --attestation to auto-confirm organize inline.", "dim"))
   433→            return
   434→
   435→    # Check shallow steps — block if any steps lack detail or issue_refs
   436→    shallow = _shallow_steps(plan)
   437→    total_bare = sum(n for _, n, _ in shallow)
   438→
   439→    if shallow:
   440→        print(colorize(f"  Cannot enrich: {total_bare} step(s) across {len(shallow)} cluster(s) lack detail or issue_refs:", "red"))
   441→        for name, bare, total in shallow:
   442→            print(colorize(f"    {name}: {bare}/{total} steps need enrichment", "yellow"))
   443→        print()
   444→        print(colorize("  Every step needs --detail (sub-points) or --issue-refs (for auto-completion).", "dim"))
   445→        print(colorize("  Fix:", "dim"))
   446→        print(colorize('    desloppify plan cluster update <name> --update-step N --detail "sub-details"', "dim"))
   447→        print(colorize("  You can also still reorganize: add/remove clusters, reorder, etc.", "dim"))
   448→        return
   449→    else:
   450→        print(colorize("  All steps have detail or issue_refs.", "green"))
   451→
   452→    report = _enrich_report_or_error(report)
   453→    if report is None:
   454→        return
   455→
   456→    stages = meta.setdefault("triage_stages", {})
   457→    cleared = _record_enrich_stage(
   458→        stages,
   459→        report=report,
   460→        shallow_count=total_bare,
   461→        existing_stage=existing_stage,
   462→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   463→    )
   464→
   465→    resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
   466→
   467→    resolved_services.append_log_entry(
   468→        plan,
   469→        "triage_enrich",
   470→        actor="user",
   471→        detail={"shallow_count": total_bare, "reuse": is_reuse},
   472→    )
   473→    resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
   474→
   475→    print(colorize(
   476→        f"  Enrich stage recorded: {total_bare} step(s) still without detail.",
   477→        "green",
   478→    ))
   479→    if is_reuse:
   480→        print(colorize("  Enrich data preserved (no changes).", "dim"))
   481→        if cleared:
   482→            print_cascade_clear_feedback(cleared, stages)
   483→    else:
   484→        print(colorize("  Now confirm the enrichment.", "yellow"))
   485→        print(colorize("    desloppify plan triage --confirm enrich", "dim"))
   486→
   487→    print_user_message(
   488→        "Hey — enrich is recorded. Before confirming, double-check"
   489→        " that every step is clear enough for an executor with zero"
   490→        " context. Add --detail to any step that's too vague. You"
   491→        " can still reorganize at this point (add/remove clusters,"
   492→        " reorder items). Once satisfied, confirm and complete triage."
   493→    )
   494→
   495→

   497→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   498→    *,
   499→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   500→) -> None:
   501→    """Public entrypoint for observe stage recording."""
   502→    _cmd_stage_observe(args, services=services)
```

```
    20→from ._stage_validation import (
    21→    _auto_confirm_enrich_for_complete,
    22→    _auto_confirm_organize_for_complete,
    23→    _completion_clusters_valid,
    24→    _completion_strategy_valid,
    25→    _confirm_existing_stages_valid,
    26→    _confirm_note_valid,
    27→    _confirm_strategy_valid,
    28→    _confirmed_text_or_error,
    29→    _note_cites_new_issues_or_error,
    30→    _require_enrich_stage_for_complete,
    31→    _require_organize_stage_for_complete,
    32→    _require_prior_strategy_for_confirm,
    33→    _resolve_completion_strategy,
    34→    _resolve_confirm_existing_strategy,
    35→)
    36→
    37→
    38→def _cmd_triage_complete(
    39→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    40→    *,
    41→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
    42→) -> None:
    43→    """Complete triage — requires organize stage (or confirm-existing path)."""
    44→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
    45→    strategy: str | None = getattr(args, "strategy", None)
    46→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attestation", None)
    47→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
    48→
    49→    if not has_triage_in_queue(plan):
    50→        print(colorize("  No planning stages in the queue — nothing to complete.", "yellow"))
    51→        return
    52→
    53→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    54→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
    55→
    56→    state = resolved_services.command_runtime(args).state
    57→    review_ids = open_review_ids_from_state(state)
    58→
    59→    # Require organize stage confirmed
    60→    if not _require_organize_stage_for_complete(
    61→        plan=plan,
    62→        meta=meta,
    63→        stages=stages,
    64→    ):
    65→        return
    66→
    67→    # Fold-confirm: auto-confirm organize if attestation provided
    68→    if not _auto_confirm_organize_for_complete(
    69→        plan=plan,
    70→        stages=stages,
    71→        attestation=attestation,
    72→    ):
    73→        return
    74→
    75→    # Require enrich stage confirmed
    76→    if not _require_enrich_stage_for_complete(
    77→        plan=plan,
    78→        meta=meta,
    79→        stages=stages,
    80→    ):
    81→        return
    82→
    83→    # Fold-confirm: auto-confirm enrich if attestation provided
    84→    if not _auto_confirm_enrich_for_complete(
    85→        plan=plan,
    86→        stages=stages,
    87→        attestation=attestation,
    88→    ):
    89→        return
    90→
    91→    # Re-validate cluster enrichment at completion time (prevents bypassing
    92→    # organize gate by editing plan.json directly)
    93→    if not _completion_clusters_valid(plan, state):
    94→        return
    95→
    96→    # Verify cluster coverage
    97→    organized, total, _clusters = triage_coverage(plan, open_review_ids=review_ids)
    98→
    99→    if total > 0 and organized == 0:
   100→        print(colorize("  Cannot complete: no issues have been organized into clusters.", "red"))
   101→        print(colorize(f"  {total} issues are waiting.", "dim"))
   102→        return
   103→
   104→    if total > 0 and organized < total:
   105→        remaining = total - organized
   106→        print(
   107→            colorize(
   108→                f"  Warning: {remaining}/{total} issues are not yet in any cluster.",
   109→                "yellow",
   110→            )
   111→        )
   112→
   113→    strategy = _resolve_completion_strategy(strategy, meta=meta)
   114→    if strategy is None:
   115→        return
   116→    if not _completion_strategy_valid(strategy):
   117→        return
   118→
   119→    # Show summary
   120→    _print_complete_summary(plan, stages)
   121→
   122→    organized, total, _ = triage_coverage(plan, open_review_ids=review_ids)
   123→
   124→    # Jump-back guidance before committing
   125→    print()
   126→    print(
   127→        colorize(
   128→            "  To revise an earlier stage: desloppify plan triage --stage <observe|reflect|organize|enrich>",
   129→            "dim",
   130→        )
   131→    )
   132→    print(colorize("  Pass --report to update, or omit to keep existing analysis.", "dim"))
   133→
   134→    resolved_services.append_log_entry(
   135→        plan,
   136→        "triage_complete",
   137→        actor="user",
   138→        detail={
   139→            "strategy_len": len(strategy.strip()),
   140→            "coverage": f"{organized}/{total}",
   141→        },
   142→    )
   143→
   144→    apply_completion(args, plan, strategy, services=resolved_services)
   145→
   146→    print_user_message(
   147→        "Hey — triage is done. Run `desloppify next` and start"
   148→        " executing the plan. Don't stop until you're done —"
   149→        " unless I've told you otherwise, just go."
   150→    )
   151→
   152→
   153→def _cmd_confirm_existing(
   154→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   155→    *,
   156→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   157→) -> None:
   158→    """Fast-track: confirm existing plan structure is still valid."""
   159→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
   160→    note: str | None = getattr(args, "note", None)
   161→    strategy: str | None = getattr(args, "strategy", None)
   162→    confirmed: str | None = getattr(args, "confirmed", None)
   163→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   164→
   165→    if not has_triage_in_queue(plan):
   166→        print(colorize("  No planning stages in the queue — nothing to confirm.", "yellow"))
   167→        return
   168→
   169→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   170→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   171→
   172→    # Require a prior completed triage — can't skip the full flow on first run
   173→    if not _require_prior_strategy_for_confirm(meta):
   174→        return
   175→
   176→    # Determine if this is a light-path (additions only) or full ceremony
   177→    runtime = resolved_services.command_runtime(args)
   178→    state = runtime.state
   179→    si = resolved_services.collect_triage_input(plan, state)
   180→    has_only_additions = bool(si.new_since_last) and not si.resolved_since_last
   181→
   182→    if not _confirm_existing_stages_valid(
   183→        stages=stages,
   184→        has_only_additions=has_only_additions,
   185→        si=si,
   186→    ):
   187→        return
   188→
   189→    # Require existing enriched clusters
   190→    clusters_with_issues = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
   191→    if not clusters_with_issues:
   192→        print(colorize("  Cannot confirm existing: no clusters with issues exist.", "red"))
   193→        print(colorize("  Use the full organize flow instead.", "dim"))
   194→        return
   195→
   196→    # Require note
   197→    if not _confirm_note_valid(note):
   198→        return
   199→
   200→    # Require strategy (default to "same" on light path)
   201→    strategy = _resolve_confirm_existing_strategy(
   202→        strategy,
   203→        has_only_additions=has_only_additions,
   204→        meta=meta,
   205→    )
   206→    if strategy is None:
   207→        return
   208→
   209→    # Strategy length check (unless "same")
   210→    if not _confirm_strategy_valid(strategy):
   211→        return
   212→
   213→    # Require --confirmed with plan review
   214→    confirmed_text = _confirmed_text_or_error(
   215→        plan=plan,
   216→        state=state,
   217→        confirmed=confirmed,
   218→    )
   219→    if confirmed_text is None:
   220→        return
   221→
   222→    # Validate: note cites at least 1 new/changed issue (if there are any)
   223→    if not _note_cites_new_issues_or_error(note, si):
   224→        return
   225→
   226→    # Record organize as confirmed-existing and complete
   227→    stages = meta.setdefault("triage_stages", {})
   228→    _record_confirm_existing_completion(
   229→        stages=stages,
   230→        note=note,
   231→        issue_count=len(clusters_with_issues),
   232→        confirmed_text=confirmed_text,
   233→    )
   234→
   235→    resolved_services.append_log_entry(
   236→        plan,
   237→        "triage_confirm_existing",
   238→        actor="user",
   239→        detail={"confirmed_text": confirmed_text},
   240→    )
   241→
   242→    apply_completion(args, plan, strategy, services=resolved_services)
   243→    print(colorize("  Confirmed existing plan — triage complete.", "green"))
   244→
   245→
   246→def cmd_triage_complete(
   247→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   248→    *,
   249→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   250→) -> None:
   251→    """Public entrypoint for triage completion."""
   252→    _cmd_triage_complete(args, services=services)
   253→
   254→
   255→def cmd_confirm_existing(
   256→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   257→    *,
   258→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   259→) -> None:
   260→    """Public entrypoint for confirm-existing completion path."""
   261→    _cmd_confirm_existing(args, services=services)
   262→
   263→
   264→__all__ = [
   265→    "cmd_confirm_existing",
   266→    "cmd_triage_complete",
   267→    "_cmd_confirm_existing",
   268→    "_cmd_triage_complete",
   269→]
   270→
```

```
1→"""Prompt construction and data models for epic triage."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     6→from typing import Any
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
     9→    Cluster,
    10→    PlanModel,
    11→    ensure_plan_defaults,
    12→    triage_clusters,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    15→
    16→
    17→@dataclass
    18→class TriageInput:
    19→    """All data the LLM needs to produce/update epics."""
    20→
    21→    open_issues: dict[str, dict]       # id -> issue (review + concerns)
    22→    mechanical_issues: dict[str, dict]  # id -> issue (non-review, for context)
    23→    existing_epics: dict[str, Cluster]    # current triage-clusters to update
    24→    dimension_scores: dict[str, Any]      # for context
    25→    new_since_last: set[str]             # issue IDs new since last triage
    26→    resolved_since_last: set[str]        # issue IDs resolved since last
    27→    previously_dismissed: list[str]      # IDs dismissed in prior triage
    28→    triage_version: int                  # next version number
    29→    resolved_issues: dict[str, dict]   # full issue objects for resolved IDs
    30→    completed_clusters: list[dict]       # clusters completed since last triage
    31→
    32→@dataclass
    33→class DismissedIssue:
    34→    """A issue the LLM says doesn't make sense."""
    35→
    36→    issue_id: str
    37→    reason: str
    38→
    39→@dataclass
    40→class ContradictionNote:
    41→    """Record of a resolved contradiction."""
    42→
    43→    kept: str
    44→    dismissed: str
    45→    reason: str
    46→
    47→@dataclass
    48→class TriageResult:
    49→    """Parsed and validated LLM triage output."""
    50→
    51→    strategy_summary: str
    52→    epics: list[dict]
    53→    dismissed_issues: list[DismissedIssue] = field(default_factory=list)
    54→    contradiction_notes: list[ContradictionNote] = field(default_factory=list)
    55→    priority_rationale: str = ""
    56→
    57→def collect_triage_input(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> TriageInput:
    58→    """Gather all data needed for the triage LLM prompt."""
    59→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    60→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
    61→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    62→    epics = triage_clusters(plan)
    63→
    64→    open_review: dict[str, dict] = {}
    65→    open_mechanical: dict[str, dict] = {}
    66→    for fid, f in issues.items():
    67→        if f.get("status") != "open":
    68→            continue
    69→        if f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns"):
    70→            open_review[fid] = f
    71→        else:
    72→            open_mechanical[fid] = f
    73→
    74→    triaged_ids = set(meta.get("triaged_ids", []))
    75→    current_review_ids = set(open_review.keys())
    76→    new_since = current_review_ids - triaged_ids
    77→    resolved_since = triaged_ids - current_review_ids
    78→    previously_dismissed = list(meta.get("dismissed_ids", []))
    79→    version = int(meta.get("version", 0)) + 1
    80→
    81→    # Resolved issue objects (for REFLECT stage)
    82→    resolved_issue_objs = {
    83→        fid: issues[fid] for fid in resolved_since if fid in issues
    84→    }
    85→
    86→    # Completed clusters since last triage completion
    87→    last_completed = meta.get("last_completed_at", "")
    88→    all_completed: list[dict] = plan.get("completed_clusters", [])
    89→    if last_completed:
    90→        recent_completed = [
    91→            c for c in all_completed
    92→            if c.get("completed_at", "") > last_completed
    93→        ]
    94→    else:
    95→        recent_completed = list(all_completed)
    96→
    97→    return TriageInput(
    98→        open_issues=open_review,
    99→        mechanical_issues=open_mechanical,
   100→        existing_epics=dict(epics),
   101→        dimension_scores=state.get("dimension_scores", {}),
   102→        new_since_last=new_since,
   103→        resolved_since_last=resolved_since,
   104→        previously_dismissed=previously_dismissed,
   105→        triage_version=version,
   106→        resolved_issues=resolved_issue_objs,
   107→        completed_clusters=recent_completed,
   108→    )
   109→
   110→_TRIAGE_SYSTEM_PROMPT = """\
   111→You are maintaining the meta-plan for this codebase. Your goal is to produce
   112→a coherent, prioritized strategy to address ALL open review issues.
   113→
   114→Your plan should:
   115→- Cluster issues by ROOT CAUSE, not by dimension or detector
   116→- Give each cluster (epic) a clear thesis: one imperative sentence
   117→- Order epics by dependency: what must be done first for later work to make sense
   118→- Dismiss issues that don't make sense, are contradictory, or are false positives
   119→- Mark which epics are agent-safe (can be executed mechanically) vs need human judgment
   120→- Avoid creating work that contradicts other work in the plan
   121→- Be ambitious but realistic — aim to resolve all issues coherently
   122→
   123→Available directions for epics: delete, merge, flatten, enforce, simplify, decompose, extract, inline.
   124→
   125→Available plan tools (the agent executing your plan has access to these):
   126→- `desloppify plan queue` — view all items in priority order
   127→- `desloppify plan focus epic/<name>` — focus the queue on one epic
   128→- `desloppify plan skip <id> --permanent --note "why" --attest "..."` — permanently dismiss
   129→- `desloppify plan skip <id> --note "revisit later"` — temporarily defer
   130→- `desloppify plan resolve <id> --note "what I did" --attest "..."` — mark resolved
   131→- `desloppify plan reorder <id> top|bottom|before|after <target>` — reorder
   132→- `desloppify plan cluster show <name>` — inspect a cluster
   133→- `desloppify scan` — re-scan after making changes to verify progress
   134→- `desloppify show review --status open` — see all open review issues
   135→
   136→Your output defines the ENTIRE work plan. Issues not assigned to any epic
   137→will remain in the queue as individual items. Dismissed issues will be
   138→removed from the queue with your stated reason.
   139→
   140→Respond with a single JSON object matching this schema:
   141→{
   142→  "strategy_summary": "2-4 sentence narrative: what the meta-plan says, top priorities, current state",
   143→  "epics": [
   144→    {
   145→      "name": "slug-name",
   146→      "thesis": "imperative one-liner",
   147→      "direction": "delete|merge|flatten|enforce|simplify|decompose|extract|inline",
   148→      "root_cause": "why this cluster exists",
   149→      "issue_ids": ["id1", "id2"],
   150→      "dismissed": ["id3"],
   151→      "agent_safe": true,
   152→      "dependency_order": 1,
   153→      "action_steps": ["step 1", "step 2"],
   154→      "status": "pending"
   155→    }
   156→  ],
   157→  "dismissed_issues": [
   158→    {"issue_id": "id", "reason": "why this issue doesn't make sense"}
   159→  ],
   160→  "contradiction_notes": [
   161→    {"kept": "issue_id", "dismissed": "issue_id", "reason": "why"}
   162→  ],
   163→  "priority_rationale": "why the dependency_order is what it is"
   164→}
   165→"""
   166→
   167→def build_triage_prompt(si: TriageInput) -> str:
   168→    """Build the user-facing prompt content with all issue data."""
   169→    parts: list[str] = []
   170→
   171→    # Section: existing epics
   172→    if si.existing_epics:
   173→        parts.append("## Existing Epics (update these, don't recreate)")
   174→        for name, epic in sorted(si.existing_epics.items()):
   175→            status = epic.get("status", "pending")
   176→            thesis = epic.get("thesis", "")
   177→            direction = epic.get("direction", "")
   178→            fids = epic.get("issue_ids", [])
   179→            parts.append(
   180→                f"- {name} [{status}] ({direction}): {thesis}"
   181→                f"\n  Issues: {', '.join(fids[:10])}"
   182→                f"{'...' if len(fids) > 10 else ''}"
   183→            )
   184→        parts.append("")
   185→
   186→    # Section: what changed
   187→    if si.new_since_last:
   188→        parts.append(f"## New issues since last triage ({len(si.new_since_last)})")
   189→        for fid in sorted(si.new_since_last):
   190→            f = si.open_issues.get(fid, {})
   191→            parts.append(f"- {fid}: {f.get('summary', '(no summary)')}")
   192→        parts.append("")
   193→
   194→    if si.resolved_since_last:
   195→        parts.append(f"## Resolved since last triage ({len(si.resolved_since_last)})")
   196→        for fid in sorted(si.resolved_since_last):
   197→            parts.append(f"- {fid}")
   198→        parts.append("")
   199→
   200→    # Section: all open review issues
   201→    parts.append(f"## All open review issues ({len(si.open_issues)})")
   202→    for fid, f in sorted(si.open_issues.items()):
   203→        detail = f.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(f.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   204→        suggestion = detail.get("suggestion", "")
   205→        dimension = detail.get("dimension", "")
   206→        confidence = f.get("confidence", "medium")
   207→        file_path = f.get("file", "")
   208→        summary = f.get("summary", "")
   209→        parts.append(f"- [{confidence}] {fid}")
   210→        parts.append(f"  File: {file_path}")
   211→        if dimension:
   212→            parts.append(f"  Dimension: {dimension}")
   213→        parts.append(f"  Summary: {summary}")
   214→        if suggestion:
   215→            parts.append(f"  Suggestion: {suggestion}")
   216→    parts.append("")
   217→
   218→    # Section: dimension scores for context
   219→    if si.dimension_scores:
   220→        parts.append("## Dimension scores (context)")
   221→        for name, data in sorted(si.dimension_scores.items()):
   222→            if isinstance(data, dict):
   223→                score = data.get("score", "?")
   224→                strict = data.get("strict", score)
   225→                issues = data.get("failing", 0)
   226→                parts.append(f"- {name}: {score}% (strict: {strict}%, {issues} issues)")
   227→        parts.append("")
   228→
   229→    # Section: previously dismissed
   230→    if si.previously_dismissed:
   231→        parts.append(f"## Previously dismissed ({len(si.previously_dismissed)})")
   232→        parts.append("Maintain unless contradicted by new evidence.")
   233→        for fid in si.previously_dismissed:
   234→            parts.append(f"- {fid}")
   235→        parts.append("")
   236→
   237→    return "\n".join(parts)
   238→
   239→__all__ = [
   240→    "_TRIAGE_SYSTEM_PROMPT",
   241→    "ContradictionNote",
   242→    "DismissedIssue",
   243→    "TriageInput",
   244→    "TriageResult",
   245→    "build_triage_prompt",
   246→    "collect_triage_input",
   247→]
   248→
```

```
1→"""Runtime helpers for review batch execution."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations

     5→import json
     6→from collections.abc import Callable

    10→from pathlib import Path
    11→from typing import TYPE_CHECKING

    13→if TYPE_CHECKING:
    14→    from .runner_parallel import BatchProgressEvent
    15→
    16→
    17→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    18→class BatchRunSummaryConfig:
    19→    """Inputs required to write the run_summary.json payload."""
    20→
    21→    created_at: str
    22→    run_stamp: str
```
