Design an implementation plan for adding subagent-delegated execution to desloppify's `plan triage` workflow. This is in the desloppify repo at /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/.

## Context

Currently, triage has 4 stages (observe → reflect → organize → enrich → commit) where the calling agent (Claude Code or Codex) does all work inline — reading issues, analyzing code, creating clusters, enriching steps — and records each stage via CLI commands. There's no subagent delegation.

The `review` command already has two subagent models:
1. **Codex direct subprocess**: `desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel` — spawns `codex exec --ephemeral` subprocesses per batch, waits, validates JSON, merges, imports
2. **Claude external session**: `desloppify review --external-start --external-runner claude` — generates blind packet + session, user pastes into Claude, submits back with `--external-submit`

We want to add the same two models to triage.

## User Requirements
1. Subagents should actually RUN CLI commands (desloppify plan cluster create/add/update, desloppify plan skip, etc.) — not just return JSON. We wait for them to complete.
2. Deep instructions — subagents need thorough guidance on what to do
3. At least one subagent per stage, possibly more (e.g., observe could be split into sub-batches by dimension group)
4. ALL subagents should have access to the codebase (can read source files) and be instructed to use it
5. Follow the same patterns as the review runner system

## Existing Review Runner Architecture (to replicate)

### Codex runner
- Command: `codex exec --ephemeral -C <repo_root> -s workspace-write -c 'approval_policy="never"' -o <output_file> <prompt>`
- Execution: `ThreadPoolExecutor` with configurable max_workers (default 8)
- Per-batch: retry logic, Popen live streaming + stall detection, timeout handling
- Results: JSON output files, validated with jq/json.loads, merged, imported
- Key files: `runner_process.py`, `_runner_process_attempts.py`, `runner_parallel.py`, `_runner_parallel_execution.py`, `batch/orchestrator.py`, `batches_runtime.py`

### Claude external
- Two-phase: `--external-start` generates blind packet + session + launch prompt file; `--external-submit` imports results with provenance
- Session has ID, token, TTL, attestation requirement
- Blind packet strips scores to prevent anchoring
- Key file: `external.py`

## Triage Stage Details

### Data available (TriageInput)
- open_issues: dict of all open review/concerns issues
- resolved_issues: dict of resolved issues  
- existing_epics: current auto-triage clusters
- dimension_scores: current scores
- new_since_last, resolved_since_last: delta tracking
- previously_dismissed: IDs dismissed in prior triage
- completed_clusters: clusters completed since last

### What each stage does today

**OBSERVE**: Agent reads issue list, produces 100+ char analysis of themes, root causes, contradictions. No CLI commands, just analysis text via `--stage observe --report "..."`.

**REFLECT**: Agent compares current issues vs resolved history, identifies recurring patterns, forms strategy. Must mention recurring dimensions. Records via `--stage reflect --report "..."`.

**ORGANIZE**: Agent runs many CLI commands:
- `desloppify plan cluster create <name> --description "..."`
- `desloppify plan cluster add <name> <issue-patterns>`
- `desloppify plan cluster update <name> --steps "step1" "step2"`
- `desloppify plan skip <patterns> --permanent --attest "..."`
- Then records via `--stage organize --report "..."`
- Validation: all review issues must be in a cluster or skipped, all clusters must have description + steps

**ENRICH**: Agent enriches each cluster step with detail and issue refs:
- `desloppify plan cluster update <name> --update-step N --detail "sub-details" --issue-refs id1 id2`
- Then records via `--stage enrich --report "..."`
- Validation: all steps must have detail or issue_refs

**COMMIT**: Agent writes strategy summary via `--complete --strategy "..."`. Validates all stages confirmed, coverage checked.

### Confirmation flow
Each stage needs confirmation: `--confirm <stage> --attestation "80+ chars mentioning actual data"`. Later stages can auto-confirm earlier ones by passing --attestation.

## Design Constraints

1. Subagents for organize and enrich MUST run CLI commands to modify plan state (not just return JSON)
2. Subagents need `-s workspace-write` (or equivalent) for file access
3. For Codex: subagents can call desloppify CLI directly since they're local subprocesses
4. For Claude external: this is trickier — Claude can't run CLI commands from an external session. Options:
   a. Claude external could return structured JSON that gets applied by an importer (like review does)
   b. Claude external could generate a script of commands to execute
   c. Skip Claude external for triage initially (Codex-only)

5. Stages are sequential (reflect needs observe output), so parallelism is WITHIN a stage, not across stages
6. Each stage's output becomes input for the next stage's subagent prompt

## Questions to Address in Your Plan

1. How should the CLI surface look? New flags on `plan triage`?
2. How should stage packets be structured? What goes in each stage's subagent prompt?
3. Should observe/reflect (analysis-only stages) have multiple subagents? How would you split them?
4. For organize/enrich (command-running stages), how do subagents coordinate? If multiple subagents are organizing, they'd conflict on cluster state.
5. How should the pipeline orchestrate stage → confirm → next stage automatically?
6. What's the merge/validation strategy after subagent completion?
7. How does Claude external work given that organize/enrich need to run commands?
8. Where in the file structure should new code live?

Please produce a detailed implementation plan with file paths, function signatures, and clear step-by-step implementation order.

1→"""Subprocess-oriented runner helpers for review batch execution."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import os
     6→import sys
     7→from pathlib import Path
     8→
     9→from ._runner_process_attempts import (
    10→    _handle_early_attempt_return,
    11→    _handle_failed_attempt,
    12→    _handle_successful_attempt,
    13→    _handle_timeout_or_stall,
    14→    _resolve_retry_config,
    15→    _run_batch_attempt,
    16→)
    17→from ._runner_process_io import _extract_payload_from_log  # noqa: F401 (runner_parallel import)
    18→from ._runner_process_types import (
    19→    CodexBatchRunnerDeps,
    20→    FollowupScanDeps,
    21→)
    22→
    23→
    24→def codex_batch_command(*, prompt: str, repo_root: Path, output_file: Path) -> list[str]:
    25→    """Build one codex exec command line for a batch prompt."""
    26→    effort = os.environ.get("DESLOPPIFY_CODEX_REASONING_EFFORT", "low").strip().lower()
    27→    if effort not in {"low", "medium", "high", "xhigh"}:
    28→        effort = "low"
    29→    return [
    30→        "codex",
    31→        "exec",
    32→        "--ephemeral",
    33→        "-C",
    34→        str(repo_root),
    35→        "-s",
    36→        "workspace-write",
    37→        "-c",
    38→        'approval_policy="never"',
    39→        "-c",
    40→        f'model_reasoning_effort="{effort}"',
    41→        "-o",
    42→        str(output_file),
    43→        prompt,
    44→    ]
    45→
    46→
    47→def run_codex_batch(
    48→    *,
    49→    prompt: str,
    50→    repo_root: Path,
    51→    output_file: Path,
    52→    log_file: Path,
    53→    deps: CodexBatchRunnerDeps,
    54→    codex_batch_command_fn=None,
    55→) -> int:
    56→    """Execute one codex batch and return a stable CLI-style status code."""
    57→    if codex_batch_command_fn is None:
    58→        codex_batch_command_fn = codex_batch_command
    59→    cmd = codex_batch_command_fn(
    60→        prompt=prompt,
    61→        repo_root=repo_root,
    62→        output_file=output_file,
    63→    )
    64→    config = _resolve_retry_config(deps)
    65→    log_sections: list[str] = []
    66→
    67→    for attempt in range(1, config.max_attempts + 1):
    68→        header, result = _run_batch_attempt(
    69→            cmd=cmd,
    70→            deps=deps,
    71→            output_file=output_file,
    72→            log_file=log_file,
    73→            log_sections=log_sections,
    74→            attempt=attempt,
    75→            max_attempts=config.max_attempts,
    76→            use_popen=config.use_popen,
    77→            live_log_interval=config.live_log_interval,
    78→            stall_seconds=config.stall_seconds,
    79→        )
    80→        early_return = _handle_early_attempt_return(result)
    81→        if early_return is not None:
    82→            return early_return
    83→        timeout_or_stall = _handle_timeout_or_stall(
    84→            header=header,
    85→            result=result,
    86→            deps=deps,
    87→            output_file=output_file,
    88→            log_file=log_file,
    89→            log_sections=log_sections,
    90→            stall_seconds=config.stall_seconds,
    91→        )
    92→        if timeout_or_stall is not None:
    93→            return timeout_or_stall
    94→
    95→        log_sections.append(
    96→            f"{header}\n\nSTDOUT:\n{result.stdout_text}\n\nSTDERR:\n{result.stderr_text}\n"
    97→        )
    98→
    99→        success_code = _handle_successful_attempt(
   100→            result=result,
   101→            output_file=output_file,
   102→            log_file=log_file,
   103→            deps=deps,
   104→            log_sections=log_sections,
   105→        )
   106→        if success_code is not None:
   107→            return success_code
   108→        failure_code = _handle_failed_attempt(
   109→            result=result,
   110→            deps=deps,
   111→            attempt=attempt,
   112→            max_attempts=config.max_attempts,
   113→            retry_backoff_seconds=config.retry_backoff_seconds,
   114→            log_file=log_file,
   115→            log_sections=log_sections,
   116→        )
   117→        if failure_code is not None:
   118→            return failure_code
   119→
   120→    deps.safe_write_text_fn(log_file, "\n\n".join(log_sections))
   121→    return 1
   122→
   123→
   124→def run_followup_scan(
   125→    *,
   126→    lang_name: str,
   127→    scan_path: str,
   128→    deps: FollowupScanDeps,
   129→    force_queue_bypass: bool = False,
   130→) -> int:
   131→    """Run a follow-up scan and return a non-zero status when it fails."""
   132→    scan_cmd = [
   133→        deps.python_executable,
   134→        "-m",
   135→        "desloppify",
   136→        "--lang",
   137→        lang_name,
   138→        "scan",
   139→        "--path",
   140→        scan_path,
   141→    ]
   142→    if force_queue_bypass:
   143→        followup_attest = (
   144→            "I understand this is not the intended workflow and "
   145→            "I am intentionally skipping queue completion"
   146→        )
   147→        scan_cmd.extend(["--force-rescan", "--attest", followup_attest])
   148→        print(
   149→            deps.colorize_fn(
   150→                "  Follow-up scan queue bypass enabled (--force-followup-scan).",
   151→                "yellow",
   152→            )
   153→        )
   154→    print(deps.colorize_fn("\n  Running follow-up scan...", "bold"))
   155→    try:
   156→        result = deps.subprocess_run(
   157→            scan_cmd,
   158→            cwd=str(deps.project_root),
   159→            timeout=deps.timeout_seconds,
   160→        )
   161→    except deps.timeout_error:
   162→        print(
   163→            deps.colorize_fn(
   164→                f"  Follow-up scan timed out after {deps.timeout_seconds}s.",
   165→                "yellow",
   166→            ),
   167→            file=sys.stderr,
   168→        )
   169→        return 124
   170→    except OSError as exc:
   171→        print(
   172→            deps.colorize_fn(f"  Follow-up scan failed: {exc}", "red"),
   173→            file=sys.stderr,
   174→        )
   175→        return 1
   176→    return int(getattr(result, "returncode", 0) or 0)
   177→
   178→
   179→__all__ = [
   180→    "CodexBatchRunnerDeps",
   181→    "FollowupScanDeps",
   182→    "_extract_payload_from_log",
   183→    "codex_batch_command",
   184→    "run_codex_batch",
   185→    "run_followup_scan",
   186→]
   187→

1→"""Parallel execution and progress-callback helpers for review batches."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→import logging
     7→import threading
     8→from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
     9→from pathlib import Path
    10→
    11→from desloppify.base.discovery.file_paths import safe_write_text
    12→
    13→from ._runner_parallel_execution import (
    14→    _drain_parallel_completions,
    15→    _execute_serial,
    16→    _queue_parallel_tasks,
    17→    _resolve_parallel_runtime,
    18→)
    19→from ._runner_parallel_progress import (
    20→    _coerce_batch_execution_options,
    21→)
    22→from ._runner_parallel_types import (
    23→    BatchExecutionOptions,
    24→    BatchProgressEvent,
    25→    BatchResult,
    26→    BatchTask,
    27→)
    28→from .runner_process import _extract_payload_from_log
    29→
    30→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    31→
    32→
    33→def execute_batches(
    34→    *,
    35→    tasks: dict[int, BatchTask],
    36→    options: BatchExecutionOptions | None = None,
    37→    progress_fn=None,
    38→    error_log_fn=None,
    39→) -> list[int]:
    40→    """Run indexed tasks and return failed index list.
    41→
    42→    Each value in *tasks* is a zero-arg callable returning an int exit code.
    43→    All domain knowledge (files, prompts, etc.) is pre-bound by the caller.
    44→    """
    45→    resolved_options = _coerce_batch_execution_options(options)
    46→    contract_cache: dict[int, str] = {}
    47→    indexes = sorted(tasks)
    48→    if resolved_options.run_parallel:
    49→        max_workers, heartbeat = _resolve_parallel_runtime(
    50→            indexes=indexes,
    51→            max_parallel_workers=resolved_options.max_parallel_workers,
    52→            heartbeat_seconds=resolved_options.heartbeat_seconds,
    53→        )
    54→        failures: set[int] = set()
    55→        progress_failures: set[int] = set()
    56→        started_at: dict[int, float] = {}
    57→        lock = threading.Lock()
    58→        with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=max_workers) as executor:
    59→            futures = _queue_parallel_tasks(
    60→                executor=executor,
    61→                indexes=indexes,
    62→                tasks=tasks,
    63→                progress_fn=progress_fn,
    64→                error_log_fn=error_log_fn,
    65→                contract_cache=contract_cache,
    66→                max_workers=max_workers,
    67→                failures=failures,
    68→                progress_failures=progress_failures,
    69→                started_at=started_at,
    70→                lock=lock,
    71→                clock_fn=resolved_options.clock_fn,
    72→            )
    73→            pending = set(futures.keys())
    74→            _drain_parallel_completions(
    75→                pending=pending,
    76→                futures=futures,
    77→                heartbeat=heartbeat,
    78→                indexes=indexes,
    79→                progress_fn=progress_fn,
    80→                error_log_fn=error_log_fn,
    81→                contract_cache=contract_cache,
    82→                failures=failures,
    83→                progress_failures=progress_failures,
    84→                started_at=started_at,
    85→                lock=lock,
    86→                clock_fn=resolved_options.clock_fn,
    87→            )
    88→        return sorted(failures)
    89→    return _execute_serial(
    90→        tasks=tasks,
    91→        indexes=indexes,
    92→        progress_fn=progress_fn,
    93→        error_log_fn=error_log_fn,
    94→        clock_fn=resolved_options.clock_fn,
    95→        contract_cache=contract_cache,
    96→    )
    97→
    98→
    99→def collect_batch_results(
   100→    *,
   101→    selected_indexes: list[int],
   102→    failures: list[int],
   103→    output_files: dict[int, Path],
   104→    allowed_dims: set[str],
   105→    extract_payload_fn,
   106→    normalize_result_fn,
   107→) -> tuple[list[BatchResult], list[int]]:
   108→    """Parse and normalize batch outputs, preserving prior failures."""
   109→    batch_results: list[BatchResult] = []
   110→    failure_set = set(failures)
   111→    for idx in selected_indexes:
   112→        had_execution_failure = idx in failure_set
   113→        raw_path = output_files[idx]
   114→        payload = None
   115→        parsed_from_log = False
   116→        if raw_path.exists():
   117→            try:
   118→                payload = extract_payload_fn(raw_path.read_text())
   119→            except OSError as exc:
   120→                logger.warning("Failed reading batch payload %s: %s", raw_path, exc)
   121→                payload = None
   122→        if payload is None:
   123→            payload = _extract_payload_from_log(idx, raw_path, extract_payload_fn)
   124→            parsed_from_log = payload is not None
   125→        if payload is None:
   126→            failure_set.add(idx)
   127→            continue
   128→        if parsed_from_log:
   129→            try:
   130→                safe_write_text(raw_path, json.dumps(payload, indent=2) + "\n")
   131→            except OSError as exc:
   132→                logger.warning("Failed writing normalized batch payload %s: %s", raw_path, exc)
   133→        try:
   134→            assessments, issues, dimension_notes, quality = normalize_result_fn(
   135→                payload,
   136→                allowed_dims,
   137→            )
   138→        except ValueError as exc:
   139→            logger.debug("Invalid batch payload at index %s (%s): %s", idx, raw_path, exc)
   140→            failure_set.add(idx)
   141→            continue
   142→        if had_execution_failure:
   143→            failure_set.discard(idx)
   144→        batch_results.append(
   145→            BatchResult(
   146→                batch_index=idx + 1,
   147→                assessments=assessments,
   148→                dimension_notes=dimension_notes,
   149→                issues=issues,
   150→                quality=quality,
   151→            )
   152→        )
   153→    return batch_results, sorted(failure_set)
   154→
   155→
   156→__all__ = [
   157→    "BatchResult",
   158→    "BatchExecutionOptions",
   159→    "BatchProgressEvent",
   160→    "collect_batch_results",
   161→    "execute_batches",
   162→]
   163→

    61→def _utc_now() -> datetime:
    62→    return datetime.now(UTC)
    63→
    64→
    65→def _iso_seconds(dt: datetime) -> str:
    66→    return dt.isoformat(timespec="seconds")
    67→
    68→
    69→def _parse_iso(raw: object) -> datetime | None:
    70→    if not isinstance(raw, str) or not raw.strip():
    71→        return None
    72→    try:
    73→        dt = datetime.fromisoformat(raw)
    74→    except ValueError:
    75→        return None
    76→    if dt.tzinfo is None:
    77→        return dt.replace(tzinfo=UTC)
    78→    return dt.astimezone(UTC)
    79→
    80→
    81→def _session_id() -> str:
    82→    return f"ext_{run_stamp()}_{secrets.token_hex(4)}"
    83→
    84→
    85→def _session_dir(session_id: str) -> Path:
    86→    return _external_session_root() / session_id
    87→
    88→
    89→def _session_file(session_id: str) -> Path:
    90→    return _session_dir(session_id) / "session.json"
    91→
    92→
    93→def _validate_session_id(session_id: str) -> None:
    94→    if not session_id.strip():
    95→        raise CommandError("Error: --session-id is required.", exit_code=2)
    96→    invalid_chars = {"/", "\\", ".."}
    97→    if any(part in session_id for part in invalid_chars):
    98→        raise CommandError("Error: invalid --session-id value.", exit_code=2)
    99→
   100→
   101→def _load_json_object(path: Path, *, label: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
   102→    if not path.exists():
   103→        raise CommandError(f"Error: {label} not found: {path}")
   104→    try:
   105→        payload = json.loads(path.read_text())
   106→    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
   107→        raise CommandError(f"Error: failed reading {label}: {exc}") from exc
   108→    if not isinstance(payload, dict):
   109→        raise CommandError(f"Error: {label} must contain a JSON object.")
   110→    return payload
   111→
   112→
   113→def _session_payload(session_id: str) -> tuple[Path, dict[str, Any]]:
   114→    _validate_session_id(session_id)
   115→    path = _session_file(session_id)
   116→    payload = _load_json_object(path, label="session")
   117→    payload_id = str(payload.get("session_id", "")).strip()
   118→    if payload_id != session_id:
   119→        raise CommandError(
   120→            f"Error: session id mismatch in {path} (expected {session_id}, found {payload_id or '<missing>'}).",
   121→        )
   122→    return path, payload
   123→
   124→
   125→def _prepare_packet_snapshot(
   126→    args,
   127→    state: dict,
   128→    lang,
   129→    *,
   130→    config: dict[str, Any],
   131→) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], Path, Path]:
   132→    """Prepare holistic review packet and persist immutable+blind snapshots."""
   133→    path = Path(getattr(args, "path", ".") or ".")
   134→    dims = parse_dimensions(args)
   135→    dimensions = list(dims) if dims else None
   136→    retrospective = bool(getattr(args, "retrospective", False))
   137→    retrospective_max_issues = coerce_positive_int(
   138→        getattr(args, "retrospective_max_issues", None),
   139→        default=30,
   140→    )
   141→    retrospective_max_batch_items = coerce_positive_int(
   142→        getattr(args, "retrospective_max_batch_items", None),
   143→        default=20,
   144→    )
   145→    lang_run, found_files = setup_lang_concrete(lang, path, config)
   146→    narrative = narrative_mod.compute_narrative(
   147→        state,
   148→        context=narrative_mod.NarrativeContext(lang=lang_run.name, command="review"),
   149→    )
   150→    packet = review_mod.prepare_holistic_review(
   151→        path,
   152→        lang_run,
   153→        state,
   154→        options=review_mod.HolisticReviewPrepareOptions(
   155→            dimensions=dimensions,
   156→            files=found_files or None,
   157→            include_issue_history=retrospective,
   158→            issue_history_max_issues=retrospective_max_issues,
   159→            issue_history_max_batch_items=retrospective_max_batch_items,
   160→        ),
   161→    )
   162→    packet["narrative"] = narrative
   163→    next_command = "desloppify review --external-submit --session-id <id> --import <file>"
   164→    if retrospective:
   165→        next_command += (
   166→            " --retrospective"
   167→            f" --retrospective-max-issues {retrospective_max_issues}"
   168→            f" --retrospective-max-batch-items {retrospective_max_batch_items}"
   169→        )
   170→    packet["next_command"] = next_command
   171→    write_query(packet)
   172→
   173→    stamp = run_stamp()
   174→    blind_packet_path = _blind_packet_path()
   175→    packet_path, blind_path = write_packet_snapshot(
   176→        packet,
   177→        stamp=stamp,
   178→        review_packet_dir=_review_packet_dir(),
   179→        blind_path=blind_packet_path,
   180→        safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,
   181→    )
   182→    return packet, packet_path, blind_path
   183→
   184→
   185→def _build_template_payload(packet: dict[str, Any], *, session_id: str, token: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
   186→    dimensions = [
   187→        dim
   188→        for dim in packet.get("dimensions", [])
   189→        if isinstance(dim, str) and dim.strip()
   190→    ]
   191→    return {
   192→        "session": {
   193→            "id": session_id,
   194→            "token": token,
   195→        },
   196→        "assessments": {dim: 0 for dim in dimensions},
   197→        "dimension_notes": {},
   198→        "issues": [],
   199→    }
   200→
   201→
   202→def _build_claude_launch_prompt(
   203→    *,
   204→    session_id: str,
   205→    token: str,
   206→    blind_path: Path,
   207→    template_path: Path,
   208→    output_path: Path,
   209→    packet: dict[str, Any],
   210→) -> str:
   211→    """Build a copy/paste-ready prompt for a Claude blind reviewer subagent."""
   212→    header = (
   213→        "# Claude Blind Reviewer Launch Prompt\n\n"
   214→        "You are an isolated blind reviewer. Do not use prior chat context, "
   215→        "prior score history, or target-score anchoring.\n\n"
   216→        f"Session id: {session_id}\n"
   217→        f"Session token: {token}\n"
   218→        f"Blind packet: {blind_path}\n"
   219→        f"Template JSON: {template_path}\n"
   220→        f"Output JSON path: {output_path}\n\n"
   221→    )
   222→
   223→    raw_batches = packet.get("investigation_batches", [])
   224→    if not isinstance(raw_batches, list):
   225→        raw_batches = []
   226→    raw_dim_prompts = packet.get("dimension_prompts")
   227→    dim_prompts: dict[str, dict[str, object]] = (
   228→        raw_dim_prompts if isinstance(raw_dim_prompts, dict) else {}
   229→    )
   230→    batches = explode_to_single_dimension(
   231→        [b for b in raw_batches if isinstance(b, dict)],
   232→        dimension_prompts=dim_prompts or None,
   233→    )
   234→
   235→    all_dims: set[str] = set()
   236→    combined_cap = 0
   237→    batch_sections: list[str] = []
   238→    for i, batch in enumerate(batches):
   239→        ctx = build_batch_context(batch, i)
   240→        all_dims.update(ctx.dimension_set)
   241→        combined_cap += ctx.issues_cap
   242→
   243→        section = (
   244→            f"--- Batch {i + 1}: {ctx.name} ---\n"
   245→            f"Rationale: {ctx.rationale}\n"
   246→        )
   247→        section += render_dimension_prompts_block(ctx.dimensions, dim_prompts)
   248→        section += render_seed_files_block(ctx)
   249→        section += render_historical_focus(batch)
   250→        section += render_mechanical_concern_signals(batch)
   251→        batch_sections.append(section)
   252→
   253→    if not combined_cap:
   254→        combined_cap = 10
   255→
   256→    output_schema = (
   257→        "Output schema:\n"
   258→        "{\n"
   259→        '  "session": {"id": "<preserve from template>", "token": "<preserve from template>"},\n'
   260→        '  "assessments": {"<dimension>": <0-100 with one decimal place>},\n'
   261→        '  "dimension_notes": {\n'
   262→        '    "<dimension>": {\n'
   263→        '      "evidence": ["specific code observations"],\n'
   264→        '      "impact_scope": "local|module|subsystem|codebase",\n'
   265→        '      "fix_scope": "single_edit|multi_file_refactor|architectural_change",\n'
   266→        '      "confidence": "high|medium|low"\n'
   267→        "    }\n"
   268→        "  },\n"
   269→        '  "issues": [{\n'
   270→        '    "dimension": "<dimension>",\n'
   271→        '    "identifier": "short_id",\n'
   272→        '    "summary": "one-line defect summary",\n'
   273→        '    "related_files": ["relative/path.py"],\n'
   274→        '    "evidence": ["specific code observation"],\n'
   275→        '    "suggestion": "concrete fix recommendation",\n'
   276→        '    "confidence": "high|medium|low",\n'
   277→        '    "impact_scope": "local|module|subsystem|codebase",\n'
   278→        '    "fix_scope": "single_edit|multi_file_refactor|architectural_change",\n'
   279→        '    "root_cause_cluster": "optional_cluster_name"\n'
   280→        "  }]\n"
   281→        "}\n\n"
   282→    )
   283→
   284→    session_requirements = (
   285→        "Session requirements:\n"
   286→        f"1. Keep `session.id` exactly `{session_id}`.\n"
   287→        f"2. Keep `session.token` exactly `{token}`.\n"
   288→        "3. Do not include provenance metadata (CLI injects canonical provenance).\n"
   289→    )
   290→
   291→    return join_non_empty_sections(
   292→        header,
   293→        *batch_sections,
   294→        render_scoring_frame(),
   295→        render_scan_evidence_note(),
   296→        render_task_requirements(issues_cap=combined_cap, dim_set=all_dims),
   297→        render_scope_enums(),
   298→        output_schema,
   299→        session_requirements,
   300→    )
   301→
   302→
   303→def do_external_start(args, state, lang, *, config: dict[str, Any] | None = None) -> None:
   304→    """Start an external review session with CLI-issued provenance context."""
   305→    config = config or {}
   306→    runner = str(getattr(args, "external_runner", "claude")).strip().lower()
   307→    if runner not in _EXTERNAL_SUPPORTED_RUNNERS:
   308→        raise CommandError(
   309→            f"Error: unsupported external runner '{runner}'. Supported: claude.",
   310→            exit_code=2,
   311→        )
   312→    ttl_hours = int(getattr(args, "session_ttl_hours", 24) or 0)
   313→    if ttl_hours <= 0:
   314→        raise CommandError("Error: --session-ttl-hours must be > 0.", exit_code=2)
   315→
   316→    packet, packet_path, blind_path = _prepare_packet_snapshot(
   317→        args,
   318→        state,
   319→        lang,
   320→        config=config,
   321→    )
   322→    packet_hash = sha256_file(blind_path)
   323→    if not isinstance(packet_hash, str):
   324→        raise CommandError(f"Error: failed to hash blind packet: {blind_path}")
   325→
   326→    now = _utc_now()

   422→    print(colorize(f"  4. Optional auto-rescan: `{submit_with_scan_cmd}`", "dim"))
   423→
   424→
   425→def _canonical_external_payload(
   426→    raw_payload: dict[str, Any],
   427→    *,
   428→    session: dict[str, Any],
   429→) -> dict[str, Any]:
   430→    """Return import payload with canonical provenance and required session token."""
   431→    session_meta = raw_payload.get("session")
   432→    if not isinstance(session_meta, dict):
   433→        raise CommandError(
   434→            "Error: external reviewer payload must include top-level `session` object."
   435→            ' Expected: {"session":{"id":"...","token":"..."},"assessments":{...},"issues":[...]}',
   436→        )
   437→
   438→    payload_id = str(session_meta.get("id", "")).strip()
   439→    payload_token = str(session_meta.get("token", "")).strip()
   440→    expected_id = str(session.get("session_id", "")).strip()
   441→    expected_token = str(session.get("token", "")).strip()
   442→    if payload_id != expected_id or payload_token != expected_token:
   443→        raise CommandError(
   444→            "Error: session id/token mismatch in external reviewer payload."
   445→            " Regenerate output using the session template/instructions.",
   446→        )
   447→
   448→    payload = {
   449→        key: value
   450→        for key, value in raw_payload.items()
   451→        if key not in {"session", "provenance"}
   452→    }
   453→    payload["provenance"] = {
   454→        "kind": "blind_review_batch_import",
   455→        "blind": True,
   456→        "runner": str(session.get("runner", "claude")),
   457→        "session_id": str(session.get("session_id", "")),
   458→        "created_at": _iso_seconds(_utc_now()),
   459→        "packet_path": str(session.get("blind_packet_path", "")),
   460→        "packet_sha256": str(session.get("packet_sha256", "")),
   461→    }
   462→    return payload
   463→
   464→
   465→def _ensure_session_open(session: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   466→    status = str(session.get("status", "open")).strip().lower()
   467→    if status == "open":
   468→        return
   469→    raise CommandError(
   470→        f"Error: session is not open (status={status or 'unknown'}). Start a new session with --external-start.",
   471→    )
   472→
   473→
   474→def _ensure_session_not_expired(session: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   475→    expires_at = _parse_iso(session.get("expires_at"))
   476→    if expires_at is None:
   477→        raise CommandError(
   478→            "Error: session metadata is missing/invalid expires_at.",
   479→        )
   480→    now = _utc_now()
   481→    if now <= expires_at:
   482→        return
   483→    raise CommandError(
   484→        f"Error: session expired at {session.get('expires_at')}. Start a new session with --external-start.",
   485→    )
   486→
   487→
   488→def do_external_submit(
   489→    *,
   490→    import_file: str,
   491→    session_id: str,
   492→    state: dict,
   493→    lang,
   494→    state_file,
   495→    config: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
   496→    allow_partial: bool = False,
   497→    scan_after_import: bool = False,
   498→    scan_path: str = ".",
   499→    dry_run: bool = False,
   500→) -> None:
   501→    """Submit external reviewer output via session, adding canonical provenance."""
   502→    config = config or {}
   503→    session_path, session = _session_payload(session_id)
   504→    _ensure_session_open(session)
   505→    _ensure_session_not_expired(session)
   506→
   507→    if str(session.get("runner", "")).strip().lower() not in _EXTERNAL_SUPPORTED_RUNNERS:
   508→        raise CommandError(
   509→            "Error: only Claude external sessions currently support durable score submit.",
   510→        )
   511→
   512→    issues_path = Path(import_file)
   513→    raw_payload = _load_json_object(issues_path, label="external issues")
   514→    canonical_payload = _canonical_external_payload(raw_payload, session=session)
   515→
   516→    stamp = run_stamp()
   517→    session_dir = session_path.parent
   518→    canonical_path = session_dir / f"canonical_import_{stamp}.json"
   519→    safe_write_text(canonical_path, json.dumps(canonical_payload, indent=2) + "\n")
   520→
   521→    if dry_run:
   522→        do_validate_import(
   523→            str(canonical_path),
   524→            lang,
   525→            allow_partial=allow_partial,
   526→            attested_external=True,
   527→            manual_attest=str(session.get("attest", EXTERNAL_ATTEST_TEXT)),
   528→        )
   529→        return
   530→
   531→    do_import(
   532→        str(canonical_path),
   533→        state,
   534→        lang,
   535→        state_file,
   536→        config=config,
   537→        allow_partial=allow_partial,
   538→        attested_external=True,
   539→        manual_attest=str(session.get("attest", EXTERNAL_ATTEST_TEXT)),
   540→    )
   541→
   542→    submitted_at = _iso_seconds(_utc_now())
   543→    session["status"] = "submitted"
   544→    session["submitted_at"] = submitted_at
   545→    session["submitted_input_file"] = str(issues_path)
   546→    session["submitted_canonical_file"] = str(canonical_path)
   547→    safe_write_text(session_path, json.dumps(session, indent=2) + "\n")
   548→
   549→    if scan_after_import:
   550→        code = run_followup_scan(
   551→            lang_name=lang.name,
   552→            scan_path=scan_path,
   553→            deps=FollowupScanDeps(
   554→                project_root=_runtime_project_root(),
   555→                timeout_seconds=FOLLOWUP_SCAN_TIMEOUT_SECONDS,
   556→                python_executable=sys.executable,
   557→                subprocess_run=subprocess.run,
   558→                timeout_error=subprocess.TimeoutExpired,
   559→                colorize_fn=colorize,
   560→            ),
   561→        )
   562→        if code != 0:
   563→            raise CommandError(f"External review exited with code {code}", exit_code=code)
   564→
   565→
   566→__all__ = ["do_external_start", "do_external_submit", "EXTERNAL_ATTEST_TEXT"]
   567→

1→"""Packet sanitization, hashing, and artifact layout helpers for review batches."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→from copy import deepcopy
     7→from datetime import UTC, datetime
     8→from hashlib import sha256
     9→from pathlib import Path
    10→from typing import Any
    11→
    12→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import PacketValidationError
    13→
    14→_BLIND_PACKET_DROP_KEYS = {
    15→    "narrative",
    16→    "next_command",
    17→    "score_snapshot",
    18→    "strict_target",
    19→    "strict_target_progress",
    20→    "subjective_at_target",
    21→}
    22→
    23→_BLIND_CONFIG_SCORE_HINT_KEYS = {
    24→    "target_strict_score",
    25→    "strict_target_score",
    26→    "target_score",
    27→    "strict_score",
    28→    "objective_score",
    29→    "overall_score",
    30→    "verified_strict_score",
    31→}
    32→
    33→
    34→def write_packet_snapshot(
    35→    packet: dict[str, Any],
    36→    *,
    37→    stamp: str,
    38→    review_packet_dir: Path,
    39→    blind_path: Path,
    40→    safe_write_text_fn,
    41→) -> tuple[Path, Path]:
    42→    """Persist immutable and blind packet snapshots for runner workflows."""
    43→    review_packet_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    44→    packet_path = review_packet_dir / f"holistic_packet_{stamp}.json"
    45→    safe_write_text_fn(packet_path, json.dumps(packet, indent=2) + "\n")
    46→    blind_packet = _build_blind_packet(packet)
    47→    safe_write_text_fn(blind_path, json.dumps(blind_packet, indent=2) + "\n")
    48→    return packet_path, blind_path
    49→
    50→
    51→def _build_blind_packet(packet: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    52→    """Return a blind-review packet with score anchoring metadata removed."""
    53→    blind = deepcopy(packet)
    54→    for key in _BLIND_PACKET_DROP_KEYS:
    55→        blind.pop(key, None)
    56→
    57→    config = blind.get("config")
    58→    if isinstance(config, dict):
    59→        sanitized = _sanitize_blind_config(config)
    60→        if sanitized:
    61→            blind["config"] = sanitized
    62→        else:
    63→            blind.pop("config", None)
    64→    return blind
    65→
    66→
    67→def build_blind_packet(packet: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    68→    """Public wrapper for blind packet sanitization."""
    69→    return _build_blind_packet(packet)
    70→
    71→
    72→def run_stamp() -> str:
    73→    """Stable UTC run stamp for artifact paths."""
    74→    return datetime.now(UTC).strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")
    75→
    76→
    77→def _sanitize_blind_config(config: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    78→    """Drop score/target hints from config while preserving unrelated options."""
    79→    sanitized: dict[str, Any] = {}
    80→    for key, value in config.items():
    81→        lowered = key.strip().lower()
    82→        if not lowered:
    83→            continue
    84→        if lowered in _BLIND_CONFIG_SCORE_HINT_KEYS:
    85→            continue
    86→        if "target" in lowered:
    87→            continue
    88→        if lowered.endswith("_score"):
    89→            continue
    90→        sanitized[key] = value
    91→    return sanitized
    92→
    93→
    94→def sha256_file(path: Path) -> str | None:
    95→    """Compute sha256 hex digest for path contents (or None on read failure)."""
    96→    try:
    97→        data = path.read_bytes()
    98→    except OSError:
    99→        return None
   100→    return sha256(data).hexdigest()
   101→
   102→
   103→def build_batch_import_provenance(
   104→    *,
   105→    runner: str,
   106→    blind_packet_path: Path,
   107→    run_stamp: str,
   108→    batch_indexes: list[int],
   109→) -> dict[str, Any]:
   110→    """Build provenance payload used to trust assessment-bearing imports."""
   111→    packet_hash = sha256_file(blind_packet_path)
   112→    batch_indexes_1 = sorted({int(index) + 1 for index in batch_indexes})
   113→    return {
   114→        "kind": "blind_review_batch_import",
   115→        "blind": True,
   116→        "runner": runner,
   117→        "run_stamp": run_stamp,
   118→        "created_at": datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(timespec="seconds"),
   119→        "batch_count": len(batch_indexes_1),
   120→        "batch_indexes": batch_indexes_1,
   121→        "packet_path": str(blind_packet_path),
   122→        "packet_sha256": packet_hash,
   123→    }
   124→
   125→
   126→def selected_batch_indexes(
   127→    *,
   128→    raw_selection: str | None,
   129→    batch_count: int,
   130→    parse_fn,
   131→    colorize_fn,
   132→) -> list[int]:
   133→    """Validate selected batch indexes or raise CommandError."""
   134→    try:
   135→        selected = parse_fn(raw_selection, batch_count)
   136→    except ValueError as exc:
   137→        raise PacketValidationError(str(exc), exit_code=2) from exc
   138→    if selected:
   139→        return selected
   140→    raise PacketValidationError("no batches selected", exit_code=2)
   141→
   142→
   143→def prepare_run_artifacts(
   144→    *,
   145→    stamp: str,
   146→    selected_indexes: list[int],
   147→    batches: list[dict[str, Any]],
   148→    packet_path: Path,
   149→    run_root: Path,
   150→    repo_root: Path,
   151→    build_prompt_fn,
   152→    safe_write_text_fn,
   153→    colorize_fn,
   154→) -> tuple[Path, Path, dict[int, Path], dict[int, Path], dict[int, Path]]:
   155→    """Build prompt/output/log paths and persist prompts for selected batches."""
   156→    run_dir = run_root / stamp
   157→    prompts_dir = run_dir / "prompts"
   158→    results_dir = run_dir / "results"
   159→    logs_dir = run_dir / "logs"
   160→    prompts_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   161→    results_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   162→    logs_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   163→
   164→    selected_1_based = [idx + 1 for idx in selected_indexes]
   165→    print(colorize_fn(f"\n  Running holistic batches: {selected_1_based}", "bold"))
   166→    print(colorize_fn(f"  Run artifacts: {run_dir}", "dim"))
   167→
   168→    prompt_files: dict[int, Path] = {}
   169→    output_files: dict[int, Path] = {}
   170→    log_files: dict[int, Path] = {}
   171→    for idx in selected_indexes:
   172→        batch = batches[idx] if isinstance(batches[idx], dict) else {}
   173→        prompt_text = build_prompt_fn(
   174→            repo_root=repo_root,
   175→            packet_path=packet_path,
   176→            batch_index=idx,
   177→            batch=batch,
   178→        )
   179→        prompt_file = prompts_dir / f"batch-{idx + 1}.md"
   180→        output_file = results_dir / f"batch-{idx + 1}.raw.txt"
   181→        log_file = logs_dir / f"batch-{idx + 1}.log"
   182→        safe_write_text_fn(prompt_file, prompt_text)
   183→        prompt_files[idx] = prompt_file
   184→        output_files[idx] = output_file
   185→        log_files[idx] = log_file
   186→    return run_dir, logs_dir, prompt_files, output_files, log_files
   187→
   188→

   193→    "run_stamp",
   194→    "selected_batch_indexes",
   195→    "sha256_file",
   196→    "write_packet_snapshot",
   197→]

    94→    runtime = resolved_services.command_runtime(args)
    95→    si = resolved_services.collect_triage_input(plan, runtime.state)
    96→    print(colorize("  Planning mode started (5 stages queued).", "green"))
    97→    print(f"  Open review issues: {len(si.open_issues)}")
    98→    print(colorize("  Begin with observe:", "dim"))
    99→    print(colorize(f"    {TRIAGE_CMD_OBSERVE}", "dim"))
   100→
   101→
   102→def cmd_plan_triage(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   103→    """Run epic triage: staged workflow OBSERVE → REFLECT → ORGANIZE → ENRICH → COMMIT."""
   104→    resolved_services = _build_triage_services()
   105→    runtime = resolved_services.command_runtime(args)
   106→    state = runtime.state
   107→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   108→        return
   109→
   110→    if getattr(args, "start", False):
   111→        _cmd_triage_start(args, services=resolved_services)
   112→        return
   113→    if getattr(args, "confirm", None):
   114→        _confirmations_mod.cmd_confirm_stage(args, services=resolved_services)
   115→        return
   116→    if getattr(args, "complete", False):
   117→        _completion_mod.cmd_triage_complete(args, services=resolved_services)
   118→        return
   119→    if getattr(args, "confirm_existing", False):
   120→        _completion_mod.cmd_confirm_existing(args, services=resolved_services)
   121→        return
   122→
   123→    stage = getattr(args, "stage", None)
   124→    if stage == "observe":
   125→        _flow_mod.cmd_stage_observe(args, services=resolved_services)
   126→        return
   127→    if stage == "reflect":
   128→        _flow_mod.cmd_stage_reflect(args, services=resolved_services)
   129→        return
   130→    if stage == "organize":
   131→        _flow_mod.cmd_stage_organize(args, services=resolved_services)
   132→        return
   133→    if stage == "enrich":
   134→        _flow_mod.cmd_stage_enrich(args, services=resolved_services)
   135→        return
   136→
   137→    if getattr(args, "dry_run", False):
   138→        plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   139→        si = resolved_services.collect_triage_input(plan, state)
   140→        prompt = resolved_services.build_triage_prompt(si)
   141→        print(colorize("  Epic triage — dry run", "bold"))
   142→        print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
   143→        print(f"  Open review issues: {len(si.open_issues)}")
   144→        print(f"  Existing epics: {len(si.existing_epics)}")
   145→        print(f"  New since last: {len(si.new_since_last)}")
   146→        print(f"  Resolved since last: {len(si.resolved_since_last)}")
   147→        print(colorize("\n  Prompt that would be sent to LLM:", "dim"))
   148→        print()
   149→        print(prompt)
   150→        return
   151→
   152→    _display_mod.cmd_triage_dashboard(args, services=resolved_services)
   153→
   154→__all__ = [
   155→    "_MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN",
   156→    "_triage_coverage",
   157→    "_validate_attestation",
   158→    "cmd_plan_triage",
   159→]
   160→

1→"""Shared dependency bundle for triage command modules."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→from dataclasses import dataclass
     7→from typing import Any, Callable
     8→
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
    10→from desloppify.engine.plan import (
    11→    append_log_entry,
    12→    build_triage_prompt,
    13→    collect_triage_input,
    14→    detect_recurring_patterns,
    15→    extract_issue_citations,
    16→    load_plan,
    17→    save_plan,
    18→)
    19→
    20→
    21→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    22→class TriageServices:
    23→    """Callables shared across triage handler modules."""
    24→
    25→    command_runtime: Callable[[argparse.Namespace], Any]
    26→    load_plan: Callable[..., dict]
    27→    save_plan: Callable[..., None]
    28→    collect_triage_input: Callable[[dict, dict], Any]
    29→    detect_recurring_patterns: Callable[[dict, dict], dict]
    30→    append_log_entry: Callable[..., Any]
    31→    extract_issue_citations: Callable[[str, set[str]], set[str]]
    32→    build_triage_prompt: Callable[[Any], str]
    33→
    34→
    35→def default_triage_services() -> TriageServices:
    36→    """Return the default runtime triage service bundle."""
    37→    return TriageServices(
    38→        command_runtime=command_runtime,
    39→        load_plan=load_plan,

    46→    )

    45→def _cmd_stage_observe(
    46→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    47→    *,
    48→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
    49→) -> None:
    50→    """Record the OBSERVE stage: agent analyses themes and root causes.
    51→
    52→    No citation gate — the point is genuine analysis, not ID-stuffing.
    53→    Just requires a 100-char report describing what the agent observed.
    54→    """

    56→
    57→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()

    59→    state = runtime.state
    60→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
    61→

    64→        inject_triage_stages(plan)
    65→        meta = plan.setdefault("epic_triage_meta", {})
    66→        meta["triage_stages"] = {}
    67→        resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
    68→        print(colorize("  Planning mode auto-started (5 stages queued).", "cyan"))
    69→
    70→    meta = plan.setdefault("epic_triage_meta", {})
    71→    stages = meta.setdefault("triage_stages", {})
    72→    existing_stage = stages.get("observe")
    73→
    74→    # Jump-back: reuse existing report if no --report provided
    75→    report, is_reuse = _resolve_reusable_report(report, existing_stage)
    76→    if not report:
    77→        _print_observe_report_requirement()
    78→        return
    79→
    80→    si = resolved_services.collect_triage_input(plan, state)
    81→    issue_count = len(si.open_issues)
    82→
    83→    # Edge case: 0 issues
    84→    if issue_count == 0:
    85→        cleared = _record_observe_stage(

    87→            report=report,
    88→            issue_count=0,
    89→            cited_ids=[],
    90→            existing_stage=existing_stage,
    91→            is_reuse=is_reuse,
    92→        )
    93→        resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
    94→        print(colorize("  Observe stage recorded (no issues to analyse).", "green"))
    95→        if is_reuse:
    96→            print(colorize("  Observe data preserved (no changes).", "dim"))
    97→            if cleared:
    98→                print_cascade_clear_feedback(cleared, stages)
    99→        return
   100→
   101→    # Validation: report length (no citation counting)
   102→    min_chars = 50 if issue_count <= 3 else 100
   103→    if len(report) < min_chars:

   105→        print(colorize("  Describe themes, root causes, contradictions, and how issues relate.", "dim"))
   106→        return
   107→
   108→    # Save stage (still extract citations for analytics, but don't gate on them)
   109→    valid_ids = set(si.open_issues.keys())

   111→

   156→
   157→def _cmd_stage_reflect(
   158→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   159→    *,
   160→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   161→) -> None:
   162→    """Record the REFLECT stage: compare current issues against completed work.
   163→
   164→    Forces the agent to consider what was previously resolved and whether
   165→    similar issues are recurring. Requires a 100-char report (50 if ≤3 issues).
   166→    If recurring patterns are detected, the report must mention at least one
   167→    recurring dimension name.
   168→    """
   169→    report: str | None = getattr(args, "report", None)
   170→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attestation", None)
   171→
   172→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
   173→    runtime = resolved_services.command_runtime(args)
   174→    state = runtime.state
   175→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   176→
   177→    if not has_triage_in_queue(plan):
   178→        print(colorize("  No planning stages in the queue — nothing to reflect on.", "yellow"))
   179→        return
   180→
   181→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   182→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   183→
   184→    # Jump-back: reuse existing report if no --report provided
   185→    existing_stage = stages.get("reflect")
   186→    is_reuse = False
   187→    if not report and existing_stage and existing_stage.get("report"):
   188→        report = existing_stage["report"]
   189→        is_reuse = True
   190→    elif not report:
   191→        _print_reflect_report_requirement()
   192→        return
   193→
   194→    if "observe" not in stages:
   195→        print(colorize("  Cannot reflect: observe stage not complete.", "red"))
   196→        print(colorize('  Run: desloppify plan triage --stage observe --report "..."', "dim"))
   197→        return
   198→
   199→    si = resolved_services.collect_triage_input(plan, state)
   200→
   201→    # Fold-confirm: auto-confirm observe if attestation provided
   202→    if not _auto_confirm_observe_if_attested(
   203→        plan=plan,
   204→        stages=stages,
   205→        attestation=attestation,
   206→        triage_input=si,
   207→    ):
   208→        return
   209→
   210→    issue_count = len(si.open_issues)
   211→
   212→    # Validation: report length
   213→    min_chars = 50 if issue_count <= 3 else 100
   214→    if len(report) < min_chars:
   215→        print(colorize(f"  Report too short: {len(report)} chars (minimum {min_chars}).", "red"))
   216→        print(colorize("  Describe how current issues relate to previously completed work.", "dim"))
   217→        return
   218→
   219→    # Detect recurring patterns
   220→    recurring = resolved_services.detect_recurring_patterns(
   221→        si.open_issues,
   222→        si.resolved_issues,
   223→    )
   224→    recurring_dims = sorted(recurring.keys())
   225→
   226→    # If recurring patterns exist, report must mention at least one dimension
   227→    if not _validate_recurring_dimension_mentions(
   228→        report=report,
   229→        recurring_dims=recurring_dims,
   230→        recurring=recurring,
   231→    ):
   232→        return
   233→
   234→    # Save stage
   235→    stages = meta.setdefault("triage_stages", {})
   236→    reflect_stage = {
   237→        "stage": "reflect",
   238→        "report": report,
   239→        "cited_ids": [],
   240→        "timestamp": utc_now(),
   241→        "issue_count": issue_count,
   242→    }
   243→    reflect_stage["recurring_dims"] = recurring_dims
   244→    stages["reflect"] = reflect_stage
   245→
   246→    # Jump-back: preserve or clear confirmation
   247→    if is_reuse and existing_stage and existing_stage.get("confirmed_at"):
   248→        stages["reflect"]["confirmed_at"] = existing_stage["confirmed_at"]
   249→        stages["reflect"]["confirmed_text"] = existing_stage.get("confirmed_text", "")
   250→    cleared = cascade_clear_later_confirmations(stages, "reflect")
   251→
   252→    resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
   253→
   254→    reflect_detail = {
   255→        "issue_count": issue_count,
   256→        "reuse": is_reuse,
   257→    }
   258→    reflect_detail["recurring_dims"] = recurring_dims
   259→    resolved_services.append_log_entry(
   260→        plan,
   261→        "triage_reflect",
   262→        actor="user",
   263→        detail=reflect_detail,
   264→    )
   265→    resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
   266→
   267→    print_reflect_result(
   268→        issue_count=issue_count,
   269→        recurring_dims=recurring_dims,
   270→        recurring=recurring,
   271→        report=report,
   272→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   273→        cleared=cleared,
   274→        stages=stages,
   275→    )
   276→
   277→
   278→def _cmd_stage_organize(
   279→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   280→    *,
   281→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   282→) -> None:
   283→    """Record the ORGANIZE stage: validates cluster enrichment.
   284→
   285→    Instead of gating on a text report, validates that the plan data
   286→    itself has been enriched: each manual cluster needs description +
   287→    action_steps. This forces the agent to actually think about each
   288→    cluster's execution plan.
   289→    """
   290→    report: str | None = getattr(args, "report", None)
   291→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attestation", None)
   292→
   293→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
   294→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   295→
   296→    if not has_triage_in_queue(plan):
   297→        print(colorize("  No planning stages in the queue — nothing to organize.", "yellow"))
   298→        return
   299→
   300→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   301→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   302→
   303→    # Jump-back: reuse existing report if no --report provided
   304→    existing_stage = stages.get("organize")
   305→    is_reuse = False
   306→    if not report and existing_stage and existing_stage.get("report"):
   307→        report = existing_stage["report"]
   308→        is_reuse = True
   309→
   310→    if not _require_reflect_stage_for_organize(stages):
   311→        return
   312→
   313→    # Fold-confirm: auto-confirm reflect if attestation provided
   314→    if not _auto_confirm_reflect_for_organize(
   315→        args=args,
   316→        plan=plan,
   317→        stages=stages,
   318→        attestation=attestation,
   319→    ):
   320→        return
   321→
   322→    # Validate: at least 1 manual cluster with issues
   323→    manual_clusters = _manual_clusters_or_error(plan)
   324→    if manual_clusters is None:
   325→        return
   326→
   327→    # Validate: all manual clusters are enriched
   328→    if not _clusters_enriched_or_error(plan):
   329→        return
   330→
   331→    # Validate: all review issues are in a manual cluster (or wontfixed)
   332→    state = resolved_services.command_runtime(args).state
   333→    if not _unclustered_review_issues_or_error(plan, state):
   334→        return
   335→
   336→    # Warn if few/no cluster operations since reflect
   337→    reflect_ts = stages.get("reflect", {}).get("timestamp", "")
   338→    if reflect_ts and not is_reuse:
   339→        activity = count_log_activity_since(plan, reflect_ts)
   340→        cluster_ops = sum(
   341→            activity.get(k, 0)
   342→            for k in ("cluster_create", "cluster_add", "cluster_update", "cluster_remove")
   343→        )
   344→        if cluster_ops == 0:
   345→            print(colorize("  Warning: no cluster operations logged since reflect.", "yellow"))
   346→            print(colorize("  Did you create clusters, add issues, and enrich them?", "yellow"))
   347→            print(colorize("  The organize stage should reflect real work — not just recording a report.", "dim"))
   348→            print()
   349→        elif cluster_ops < 3:
   350→            print(colorize(f"  Note: only {cluster_ops} cluster operation(s) logged since reflect.", "yellow"))
   351→            print(colorize("  Make sure all clusters are properly set up before proceeding.", "dim"))
   352→            print()
   353→
   354→    report = _organize_report_or_error(report)
   355→    if report is None:
   356→        return
   357→
   358→    stages = meta.setdefault("triage_stages", {})
   359→    cleared = _record_organize_stage(
   360→        stages,
   361→        report=report,
   362→        issue_count=len(manual_clusters),
   363→        existing_stage=existing_stage,
   364→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   365→    )
   366→
   367→    resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
   368→
   369→    resolved_services.append_log_entry(
   370→        plan,
   371→        "triage_organize",
   372→        actor="user",
   373→        detail={"cluster_count": len(manual_clusters), "reuse": is_reuse},
   374→    )
   375→    resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
   376→
   377→    print_organize_result(
   378→        manual_clusters=manual_clusters,
   379→        plan=plan,
   380→        report=report,
   381→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   382→        cleared=cleared,
   383→        stages=stages,
   384→    )
   385→
   386→
   387→def _cmd_stage_enrich(
   388→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   389→    *,
   390→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   391→) -> None:
   392→    """Record the ENRICH stage: validate steps have detail/refs for executor readiness.
   393→
   394→    This stage sits between organize and commit. It checks whether action steps
   395→    are detailed enough for an executor with zero context. Steps should have
   396→    ``detail`` (sub-points) and/or ``issue_refs`` (for auto-completion).
   397→
   398→    The agent can still go back and organize (add/remove clusters, reorder)
   399→    at this point — the stage just validates step quality.
   400→    """
   401→    report: str | None = getattr(args, "report", None)
   402→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attestation", None)
   403→
   404→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
   405→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   406→
   407→    if not has_triage_in_queue(plan):
   408→        print(colorize("  No planning stages in the queue — nothing to enrich.", "yellow"))
   409→        return
   410→
   411→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   412→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   413→
   414→    # Jump-back: reuse existing report if no --report provided
   415→    existing_stage = stages.get("enrich")
   416→    report, is_reuse = _resolve_reusable_report(report, existing_stage)
   417→
   418→    if not _require_organize_stage_for_enrich(stages):
   419→        return
   420→
   421→    # Auto-confirm organize if attestation provided
   422→    if not stages.get("organize", {}).get("confirmed_at"):
   423→        if attestation:
   424→            from ._stage_validation import _auto_confirm_organize_for_complete
   425→            if not _auto_confirm_organize_for_complete(
   426→                plan=plan, stages=stages, attestation=attestation,
   427→            ):
   428→                return
   429→        else:
   430→            print(colorize("  Cannot enrich: organize stage not confirmed.", "red"))
   431→            print(colorize("  Run: desloppify plan triage --confirm organize", "dim"))
   432→            print(colorize("  Or pass --attestation to auto-confirm organize inline.", "dim"))
   433→            return
   434→
   435→    # Check shallow steps — block if any steps lack detail or issue_refs
   436→    shallow = _shallow_steps(plan)
   437→    total_bare = sum(n for _, n, _ in shallow)
   438→
   439→    if shallow:
   440→        print(colorize(f"  Cannot enrich: {total_bare} step(s) across {len(shallow)} cluster(s) lack detail or issue_refs:", "red"))
   441→        for name, bare, total in shallow:
   442→            print(colorize(f"    {name}: {bare}/{total} steps need enrichment", "yellow"))
   443→        print()
   444→        print(colorize("  Every step needs --detail (sub-points) or --issue-refs (for auto-completion).", "dim"))
   445→        print(colorize("  Fix:", "dim"))
   446→        print(colorize('    desloppify plan cluster update <name> --update-step N --detail "sub-details"', "dim"))
   447→        print(colorize("  You can also still reorganize: add/remove clusters, reorder, etc.", "dim"))
   448→        return
   449→    else:
   450→        print(colorize("  All steps have detail or issue_refs.", "green"))
   451→
   452→    report = _enrich_report_or_error(report)
   453→    if report is None:
   454→        return
   455→
   456→    stages = meta.setdefault("triage_stages", {})
   457→    cleared = _record_enrich_stage(
   458→        stages,
   459→        report=report,
   460→        shallow_count=total_bare,
   461→        existing_stage=existing_stage,
   462→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   463→    )
   464→
   465→    resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
   466→
   467→    resolved_services.append_log_entry(
   468→        plan,
   469→        "triage_enrich",
   470→        actor="user",
   471→        detail={"shallow_count": total_bare, "reuse": is_reuse},
   472→    )
   473→    resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
   474→
   475→    print(colorize(
   476→        f"  Enrich stage recorded: {total_bare} step(s) still without detail.",
   477→        "green",
   478→    ))
   479→    if is_reuse:
   480→        print(colorize("  Enrich data preserved (no changes).", "dim"))
   481→        if cleared:
   482→            print_cascade_clear_feedback(cleared, stages)
   483→    else:
   484→        print(colorize("  Now confirm the enrichment.", "yellow"))
   485→        print(colorize("    desloppify plan triage --confirm enrich", "dim"))
   486→
   487→    print_user_message(

   491→        " can still reorganize at this point (add/remove clusters,"

    38→def _cmd_triage_complete(
    39→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    40→    *,
    41→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
    42→) -> None:
    43→    """Complete triage — requires organize stage (or confirm-existing path)."""
    44→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
    45→    strategy: str | None = getattr(args, "strategy", None)
    46→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attestation", None)
    47→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
    48→
    49→    if not has_triage_in_queue(plan):
    50→        print(colorize("  No planning stages in the queue — nothing to complete.", "yellow"))
    51→        return
    52→
    53→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    54→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
    55→
    56→    state = resolved_services.command_runtime(args).state
    57→    review_ids = open_review_ids_from_state(state)
    58→
    59→    # Require organize stage confirmed
    60→    if not _require_organize_stage_for_complete(
    61→        plan=plan,
    62→        meta=meta,
    63→        stages=stages,
    64→    ):
    65→        return
    66→
    67→    # Fold-confirm: auto-confirm organize if attestation provided
    68→    if not _auto_confirm_organize_for_complete(
    69→        plan=plan,
    70→        stages=stages,
    71→        attestation=attestation,
    72→    ):
    73→        return
    74→
    75→    # Require enrich stage confirmed
    76→    if not _require_enrich_stage_for_complete(
    77→        plan=plan,
    78→        meta=meta,
    79→        stages=stages,
    80→    ):
    81→        return
    82→
    83→    # Fold-confirm: auto-confirm enrich if attestation provided
    84→    if not _auto_confirm_enrich_for_complete(
    85→        plan=plan,
    86→        stages=stages,
    87→        attestation=attestation,
    88→    ):
    89→        return
    90→
    91→    # Re-validate cluster enrichment at completion time (prevents bypassing
    92→    # organize gate by editing plan.json directly)
    93→    if not _completion_clusters_valid(plan, state):
    94→        return
    95→
    96→    # Verify cluster coverage
    97→    organized, total, _clusters = triage_coverage(plan, open_review_ids=review_ids)
    98→
    99→    if total > 0 and organized == 0:
   100→        print(colorize("  Cannot complete: no issues have been organized into clusters.", "red"))
   101→        print(colorize(f"  {total} issues are waiting.", "dim"))
   102→        return
   103→
   104→    if total > 0 and organized < total:
   105→        remaining = total - organized
   106→        print(
   107→            colorize(
   108→                f"  Warning: {remaining}/{total} issues are not yet in any cluster.",
   109→                "yellow",
   110→            )
   111→        )
   112→
   113→    strategy = _resolve_completion_strategy(strategy, meta=meta)
   114→    if strategy is None:
   115→        return
   116→    if not _completion_strategy_valid(strategy):

   118→
   119→    # Show summary

   121→
   122→    organized, total, _ = triage_coverage(plan, open_review_ids=review_ids)
   123→
   124→    # Jump-back guidance before committing
   125→    print()
   126→    print(

   129→            "dim",

   131→    )

   133→
   134→    resolved_services.append_log_entry(

   136→        "triage_complete",
   137→        actor="user",
   138→        detail={
   139→            "strategy_len": len(strategy.strip()),
   140→            "coverage": f"{organized}/{total}",
   141→        },
   142→    )
   143→
   144→    apply_completion(args, plan, strategy, services=resolved_services)
   145→
   146→    print_user_message(
   147→        "Hey — triage is done. Run `desloppify next` and start"
   148→        " executing the plan. Don't stop until you're done —"
   149→        " unless I've told you otherwise, just go."
   150→    )

   153→def _cmd_confirm_existing(
   154→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   155→    *,
   156→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   157→) -> None:
   158→    """Fast-track: confirm existing plan structure is still valid."""
   159→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
   160→    note: str | None = getattr(args, "note", None)
   161→    strategy: str | None = getattr(args, "strategy", None)
   162→    confirmed: str | None = getattr(args, "confirmed", None)
   163→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   164→
   165→    if not has_triage_in_queue(plan):
   166→        print(colorize("  No planning stages in the queue — nothing to confirm.", "yellow"))
   167→        return
   168→
   169→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   170→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   171→
   172→    # Require a prior completed triage — can't skip the full flow on first run
   173→    if not _require_prior_strategy_for_confirm(meta):
   174→        return
   175→
   176→    # Determine if this is a light-path (additions only) or full ceremony
   177→    runtime = resolved_services.command_runtime(args)
   178→    state = runtime.state
   179→    si = resolved_services.collect_triage_input(plan, state)
   180→    has_only_additions = bool(si.new_since_last) and not si.resolved_since_last
   181→
   182→    if not _confirm_existing_stages_valid(
   183→        stages=stages,
   184→        has_only_additions=has_only_additions,
   185→        si=si,
   186→    ):
   187→        return
   188→
   189→    # Require existing enriched clusters
   190→    clusters_with_issues = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
   191→    if not clusters_with_issues:
   192→        print(colorize("  Cannot confirm existing: no clusters with issues exist.", "red"))
   193→        print(colorize("  Use the full organize flow instead.", "dim"))
   194→        return
   195→
   196→    # Require note
   197→    if not _confirm_note_valid(note):
   198→        return
   199→
   200→    # Require strategy (default to "same" on light path)
   201→    strategy = _resolve_confirm_existing_strategy(
   202→        strategy,
   203→        has_only_additions=has_only_additions,
   204→        meta=meta,
   205→    )
   206→    if strategy is None:
   207→        return
   208→
   209→    # Strategy length check (unless "same")
   210→    if not _confirm_strategy_valid(strategy):
   211→        return
   212→
   213→    # Require --confirmed with plan review
   214→    confirmed_text = _confirmed_text_or_error(
   215→        plan=plan,
   216→        state=state,
   217→        confirmed=confirmed,
   218→    )
   219→    if confirmed_text is None:
   220→        return
   221→
   222→    # Validate: note cites at least 1 new/changed issue (if there are any)
   223→    if not _note_cites_new_issues_or_error(note, si):
   224→        return
   225→
   226→    # Record organize as confirmed-existing and complete
   227→    stages = meta.setdefault("triage_stages", {})
   228→    _record_confirm_existing_completion(
   229→        stages=stages,
   230→        note=note,
   231→        issue_count=len(clusters_with_issues),
   232→        confirmed_text=confirmed_text,
   233→    )
   234→
   235→    resolved_services.append_log_entry(
   236→        plan,
   237→        "triage_confirm_existing",
   238→        actor="user",
   239→        detail={"confirmed_text": confirmed_text},
   240→    )
   241→
   242→    apply_completion(args, plan, strategy, services=resolved_services)
   243→    print(colorize("  Confirmed existing plan — triage complete.", "green"))
   244→
   245→
   246→def cmd_triage_complete(
   247→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   248→    *,
   249→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   250→) -> None:
   251→    """Public entrypoint for triage completion."""
   252→    _cmd_triage_complete(args, services=services)
   253→
   254→
   255→def cmd_confirm_existing(
   256→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   257→    *,
   258→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   259→) -> None:
   260→    """Public entrypoint for confirm-existing completion path."""
   261→    _cmd_confirm_existing(args, services=services)

    77→def _merge_batch_results(batch_results: list[object]) -> dict[str, object]:
    78→    """Deterministically merge assessments/issues across batch outputs."""
    79→    normalized_results: list[batch_core_mod.BatchResultPayload] = []
    80→    for result in batch_results:
    81→        if hasattr(result, "to_dict") and callable(result.to_dict):
    82→            payload = result.to_dict()
    83→            if isinstance(payload, dict):
    84→                normalized_results.append(cast(batch_core_mod.BatchResultPayload, payload))
    85→                continue
    86→        if isinstance(result, dict):
    87→            normalized_results.append(cast(batch_core_mod.BatchResultPayload, result))
    88→    return batch_core_mod.merge_batch_results(
    89→        normalized_results,
    90→        abstraction_sub_axes=ABSTRACTION_SUB_AXES,
    91→        abstraction_component_names=ABSTRACTION_COMPONENT_NAMES,
    92→    )

    95→def _load_or_prepare_packet(
    96→    args,
    97→    *,
    98→    state: dict,
    99→    lang,
   100→    config: dict,
   101→    stamp: str,
   102→) -> tuple[dict, Path, Path]:
   103→    """Load packet override or prepare a fresh packet snapshot."""
   104→    packet_override = getattr(args, "packet", None)
   105→    if packet_override:
   106→        packet_path = Path(packet_override)
   107→        if not packet_path.exists():
   108→            raise PacketValidationError(f"packet not found: {packet_override}", exit_code=1)
   109→        try:
   110→            packet = json.loads(packet_path.read_text())
   111→        except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
   112→            raise PacketValidationError(f"reading packet: {exc}", exit_code=1) from exc
   113→        blind_path = _blind_packet_path()
   114→        blind_packet = build_blind_packet(packet)
   115→        safe_write_text(blind_path, json.dumps(blind_packet, indent=2) + "\n")
   116→        print(colorize(f"  Immutable packet: {packet_path}", "dim"))
   117→        print(colorize(f"  Blind packet: {blind_path}", "dim"))
   118→        return packet, packet_path, blind_path
   119→
   120→    path = Path(args.path)
   121→    dims = parse_dimensions(args)
   122→    dimensions = list(dims) if dims else None
   123→    retrospective = bool(getattr(args, "retrospective", False))
   124→    retrospective_max_issues = coerce_positive_int(
   125→        getattr(args, "retrospective_max_issues", None),
   126→        default=30,
   127→        minimum=1,
   128→    )
   129→    retrospective_max_batch_items = coerce_positive_int(
   130→        getattr(args, "retrospective_max_batch_items", None),
   131→        default=20,
   132→        minimum=1,
   133→    )
   134→    lang_run, found_files = _setup_lang(lang, path, config)
   135→    lang_name = lang_run.name
   136→    narrative = narrative_mod.compute_narrative(
   137→        state,
   138→        context=narrative_mod.NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="review"),
   139→    )
   140→
   141→    blind_path = _blind_packet_path()
   142→    packet = review_mod.prepare_holistic_review(
   143→        path,
   144→        lang_run,
   145→        state,

   149→            max_files_per_batch=coerce_review_batch_file_limit(config),
   150→            include_issue_history=retrospective,
   151→            issue_history_max_issues=retrospective_max_issues,
   152→            issue_history_max_batch_items=retrospective_max_batch_items,
   153→        ),
   154→    )
   155→    packet["config"] = redacted_review_config(config)
   156→    packet["narrative"] = narrative
   157→    next_command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   158→    if retrospective:
   159→        next_command += (
   160→            " --retrospective"
   161→            f" --retrospective-max-issues {retrospective_max_issues}"
   162→            f" --retrospective-max-batch-items {retrospective_max_batch_items}"
   163→        )
   164→    packet["next_command"] = next_command
   165→    write_query_best_effort(
   166→        packet,

   168→    )
   169→    packet_path, blind_saved = write_packet_snapshot(
   170→        packet,
   171→        stamp=stamp,
   172→        review_packet_dir=_review_packet_dir(),
   173→        blind_path=blind_path,
   174→        safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,
   175→    )
   176→    print(colorize(f"  Immutable packet: {packet_path}", "dim"))
   177→    print(colorize(f"  Blind packet: {blind_saved}", "dim"))
   178→    return packet, packet_path, blind_saved

   181→def do_run_batches(args, state, lang, state_file, config: dict | None = None) -> None:
   182→    """Run holistic investigation batches with a local subagent runner."""
   183→    from ..runtime.policy import resolve_batch_run_policy
   184→
   185→    runtime_project_root = _runtime_project_root()
   186→    policy = resolve_batch_run_policy(args)
   187→    batch_timeout_seconds = policy.batch_timeout_seconds

   190→    batch_heartbeat_seconds = policy.heartbeat_seconds
   191→    batch_live_log_interval_seconds = (
   192→        max(1.0, min(batch_heartbeat_seconds, 10.0))
   193→        if batch_heartbeat_seconds > 0
   194→        else 5.0
   195→    )
   196→    batch_stall_kill_seconds = policy.stall_kill_seconds
   197→
   198→    def _prepare_run_artifacts(*, stamp, selected_indexes, batches, packet_path, run_root, repo_root):
   199→        return prepare_run_artifacts(
   200→            stamp=stamp,
