```
   202→            batches=batches,
   203→            packet_path=packet_path,
   204→            run_root=run_root,
   205→            repo_root=repo_root,
   206→            build_prompt_fn=batch_core_mod.build_batch_prompt,
   207→            safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,
   208→            colorize_fn=colorize,
   209→        )
   210→
   211→    def _collect_batch_results(*, selected_indexes, failures, output_files, allowed_dims):
   212→        return collect_batch_results(
   213→            selected_indexes=selected_indexes,
   214→            failures=failures,
   215→            output_files=output_files,
   216→            allowed_dims=allowed_dims,
   217→            extract_payload_fn=lambda raw: batch_core_mod.extract_json_payload(raw, log_fn=log),
   218→            normalize_result_fn=lambda payload, dims: batch_core_mod.normalize_batch_result(
   219→                payload,
   220→                dims,
   221→                max_batch_issues=max_batch_issues_for_dimension_count(
   222→                    len(dims)
   223→                ),
   224→                abstraction_sub_axes=ABSTRACTION_SUB_AXES,
   225→            ),
   226→        )
   227→
   228→    return review_batches_mod.do_run_batches(
   229→        args,
   230→        state,
   231→        lang,
   232→        state_file,
   233→        config=config,
   234→        run_stamp_fn=run_stamp,
   235→        load_or_prepare_packet_fn=_load_or_prepare_packet,
   236→        selected_batch_indexes_fn=lambda args, *, batch_count: selected_batch_indexes(
   237→            raw_selection=getattr(args, "only_batches", None),
   238→            batch_count=batch_count,
   239→            parse_fn=batch_core_mod.parse_batch_selection,
   240→            colorize_fn=colorize,
   241→        ),
   242→        prepare_run_artifacts_fn=_prepare_run_artifacts,
   243→        run_codex_batch_fn=lambda *, prompt, repo_root, output_file, log_file: run_codex_batch(
   244→            prompt=prompt,
   245→            repo_root=repo_root,
   246→            output_file=output_file,
   247→            log_file=log_file,
   248→            deps=CodexBatchRunnerDeps(
   249→                timeout_seconds=batch_timeout_seconds,
   250→                subprocess_run=subprocess.run,
   251→                timeout_error=subprocess.TimeoutExpired,
   252→                safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,
   253→                use_popen_runner=(getattr(subprocess.run, "__module__", "") == "subprocess"),
   254→                subprocess_popen=subprocess.Popen,
   255→                live_log_interval_seconds=batch_live_log_interval_seconds,
   256→                stall_after_output_seconds=batch_stall_kill_seconds,
   257→                max_retries=batch_max_retries,
   258→                retry_backoff_seconds=batch_retry_backoff_seconds,
   259→            ),
   260→        ),
   261→        execute_batches_fn=execute_batches,
   262→        collect_batch_results_fn=_collect_batch_results,
   263→        print_failures_fn=print_failures,
   264→        print_failures_and_raise_fn=print_failures_and_raise,
   265→        merge_batch_results_fn=_merge_batch_results,
   266→        build_import_provenance_fn=build_batch_import_provenance,
   267→        do_import_fn=_do_import,
   268→        run_followup_scan_fn=lambda *, lang_name, scan_path: run_followup_scan(
   269→            lang_name=lang_name,
   270→            scan_path=scan_path,
   271→            deps=FollowupScanDeps(
   272→                project_root=runtime_project_root,
   273→                timeout_seconds=FOLLOWUP_SCAN_TIMEOUT_SECONDS,
   274→                python_executable=sys.executable,
   275→                subprocess_run=subprocess.run,
   276→                timeout_error=subprocess.TimeoutExpired,
   277→                colorize_fn=colorize,
   278→            ),
   279→        ),
   280→        safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,
   281→        colorize_fn=colorize,
   282→        project_root=runtime_project_root,
   283→        subagent_runs_dir=_subagent_runs_dir(),
   284→    )
   285→
   286→def _validate_run_dir(run_dir: Path) -> tuple[dict, Path, str]:
   287→    """Validate run directory, load summary, and return (summary, blind_packet_path, immutable_packet_path).
   288→
   289→    Raises CommandError on any validation failure.
   290→    """
   291→    if not run_dir.is_dir():
   292→        raise CommandError(f"run directory not found: {run_dir}", exit_code=1)
   293→
   294→    summary_path = run_dir / "run_summary.json"
   295→    if not summary_path.exists():
   296→        raise CommandError(f"no run_summary.json in {run_dir}", exit_code=1)
   297→    try:
   298→        summary = json.loads(summary_path.read_text())
   299→    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
   300→        raise CommandError(f"Error reading run summary: {exc}", exit_code=1) from exc
   301→
   302→    successful = summary.get("successful_batches", [])
   303→    blind_packet_path = Path(str(summary.get("blind_packet", "")))
   304→    immutable_packet_path = str(summary.get("immutable_packet", ""))
   305→
   306→    if not successful:
   307→        raise CommandError("no successful batches in run summary.", exit_code=1)
   308→    if not blind_packet_path.exists():
   309→        raise PacketValidationError(f"blind packet not found: {blind_packet_path}", exit_code=1)
   310→
   311→    try:
   312→        packet = json.loads(Path(immutable_packet_path).read_text())
   313→    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
   314→        raise PacketValidationError(f"Error reading immutable packet: {exc}", exit_code=1) from exc
   315→
   316→    summary["_packet"] = packet
   317→    return summary, blind_packet_path, immutable_packet_path
   318→
   319→
   320→def do_import_run(
   321→    run_dir_path: str,
   322→    state: dict,
   323→    lang,
   324→    state_file: str,
   325→    *,
   326→    config: dict | None = None,
   327→    allow_partial: bool = False,
   328→    scan_after_import: bool = False,
   329→    scan_path: str = ".",
   330→) -> None:
   331→    """Re-import results from a completed run directory.
   332→
   333→    Replays the merge+provenance+import step that normally runs at the end of
   334→    ``--run-batches``.  Useful when the original pipeline was interrupted (e.g.
   335→    broken pipe from background execution) but all batch results completed.
   336→    """
   337→    run_dir = Path(run_dir_path)
   338→    summary, blind_packet_path, _immutable_path = _validate_run_dir(run_dir)
   339→
   340→    runner = str(summary.get("runner", "codex"))
   341→    stamp = str(summary.get("run_stamp", ""))
   342→    successful = summary.get("successful_batches", [])
   343→    packet = summary.pop("_packet", {})
   344→    allowed_dims = {str(d) for d in packet.get("dimensions", []) if isinstance(d, str)}
   345→
   346→    # -- locate and parse raw batch results --
   347→    results_dir = run_dir / "results"
   348→    selected_indexes = [idx - 1 for idx in successful]  # convert 1-based to 0-based
   349→    output_files = {
   350→        idx: results_dir / f"batch-{idx + 1}.raw.txt"
   351→        for idx in selected_indexes
   352→    }
   353→
   354→    missing = [idx + 1 for idx in selected_indexes if not output_files[idx].exists()]
   355→    if missing:
   356→        raise CommandError(f"missing result files for batches: {missing}", exit_code=1)
   357→
   358→    batch_results, failures = collect_batch_results(
   359→        selected_indexes=selected_indexes,
   360→        failures=[],
   361→        output_files=output_files,
   362→        allowed_dims=allowed_dims,
   363→        extract_payload_fn=lambda raw: batch_core_mod.extract_json_payload(raw, log_fn=log),
   364→        normalize_result_fn=lambda payload, dims: batch_core_mod.normalize_batch_result(
   365→            payload,
   366→            dims,
   367→            max_batch_issues=max_batch_issues_for_dimension_count(len(dims)),
   368→            abstraction_sub_axes=ABSTRACTION_SUB_AXES,
   369→        ),
   370→    )
   371→
   372→    if not batch_results:
   373→        raise CommandError("no valid batch results could be parsed.", exit_code=1)
   374→
   375→    print(colorize(f"  Parsed {len(batch_results)} batch results from {run_dir}", "bold"))
   376→    if failures:
   377→        print(colorize(f"  Warning: {len(failures)} batches failed to parse: {[f + 1 for f in failures]}", "yellow"))
   378→
   379→    # -- merge --
   380→    merged = _merge_batch_results(batch_results)
   381→
   382→    # -- build provenance --
   383→    successful_indexes = [idx for idx in selected_indexes if idx not in set(failures)]
   384→    merged["provenance"] = build_batch_import_provenance(
   385→        runner=runner,
   386→        blind_packet_path=blind_packet_path,
   387→        run_stamp=stamp,
   388→        batch_indexes=successful_indexes,
   389→    )
   390→
   391→    # -- write merged output --
   392→    merged_path = run_dir / "holistic_issues_merged.json"
   393→    safe_write_text(merged_path, json.dumps(merged, indent=2) + "\n")
   394→    print(colorize(f"  Merged output: {merged_path}", "bold"))
   395→
   396→    # -- import with trusted source --
   397→    _do_import(
   398→        str(merged_path),
   399→        state,
   400→        lang,
   401→        state_file,
   402→        config=config,
   403→        allow_partial=allow_partial,
   404→        trusted_assessment_source=True,
   405→        trusted_assessment_label=f"trusted import-run replay from {run_dir.name}",
   406→    )
   407→
   408→    # -- optional follow-up scan --
   409→    if scan_after_import:
   410→        lang_name = getattr(lang, "name", None) or str(getattr(lang, "lang", ""))
   411→        if lang_name:
   412→            run_followup_scan(
   413→                lang_name=lang_name,
   414→                scan_path=scan_path,
   415→                deps=FollowupScanDeps(
   416→                    project_root=_runtime_project_root(),
   417→                    timeout_seconds=FOLLOWUP_SCAN_TIMEOUT_SECONDS,
   418→                    python_executable=sys.executable,
   419→                    subprocess_run=subprocess.run,
   420→                    timeout_error=subprocess.TimeoutExpired,
   421→                    colorize_fn=colorize,
   422→                ),
   423→            )
   424→
```
```

1→"""Helper utilities for plan triage workflow."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→from collections import defaultdict
     7→
     8→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
     9→from desloppify.engine.plan import (
    10→    TRIAGE_IDS,
    11→    TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS,
    12→    purge_ids,
    13→    review_issue_snapshot_hash,
    14→)
    15→from desloppify.state import utc_now
    16→
    17→from .services import TriageServices, default_triage_services
    18→
    19→_STAGE_ORDER = ["observe", "reflect", "organize", "enrich"]
    20→
    21→
    22→def has_triage_in_queue(plan: dict) -> bool:
    23→    """Check if any triage stage ID is in the queue."""
    24→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    25→    return bool(order & TRIAGE_IDS)
    26→
    27→def inject_triage_stages(plan: dict) -> None:
    28→    """Inject all 4 triage stage IDs into the queue (fresh start)."""
    29→    order: list[str] = plan.setdefault("queue_order", [])
    30→    existing = set(order)
    31→    for sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS:
    32→        if sid not in existing:
    33→            order.insert(0 if sid == TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS[0] else len(order), sid)
    34→    # Re-insert in correct order at front
    35→    for sid in reversed(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS):
    36→        if sid in order:
    37→            order.remove(sid)
    38→    insert_at = 0
    39→    for sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS:
    40→        order.insert(insert_at, sid)
    41→        insert_at += 1
    42→
    43→def purge_triage_stage(plan: dict, stage_name: str) -> None:
    44→    """Purge a single triage stage ID from the queue."""
    45→    sid = f"triage::{stage_name}"
    46→    purge_ids(plan, [sid])
    47→
    48→def cascade_clear_later_confirmations(stages: dict, from_stage: str) -> list[str]:
    49→    """Clear confirmed_at/confirmed_text on stages AFTER *from_stage*. Returns cleared names."""
    50→    try:
    51→        idx = _STAGE_ORDER.index(from_stage)
    52→    except ValueError:
    53→        return []
    54→    cleared: list[str] = []
    55→    for later in _STAGE_ORDER[idx + 1:]:
    56→        if later in stages and stages[later].get("confirmed_at"):
    57→            stages[later].pop("confirmed_at", None)
    58→            stages[later].pop("confirmed_text", None)
    59→            cleared.append(later)
    60→    return cleared
    61→
    62→def print_cascade_clear_feedback(cleared: list[str], stages: dict) -> None:
    63→    """Print yellow cascade-clear message with next-step guidance."""
    64→    if not cleared:
    65→        return
    66→    print(colorize(f"  Cleared confirmations on: {', '.join(cleared)}", "yellow"))
    67→    next_unconfirmed = next(
    68→        (s for s in _STAGE_ORDER if s in stages and not stages[s].get("confirmed_at")),
    69→        None,
    70→    )
    71→    if next_unconfirmed:
    72→        print(colorize(
    73→            f"  Re-confirm with: desloppify plan triage --confirm {next_unconfirmed}",
    74→            "dim",
    75→        ))
    76→
    77→def observe_dimension_breakdown(si) -> tuple[dict[str, int], list[str]]:
    78→    """Count issues per dimension from a TriageInput. Returns (by_dim, sorted_dim_names)."""
    79→    by_dim: dict[str, int] = defaultdict(int)
    80→    for _fid, f in si.open_issues.items():
    81→        detail = f.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(f.get("detail"), dict) else {}
    82→        dim = detail.get("dimension", "unknown")
    83→        by_dim[dim] += 1
    84→    dim_names = sorted(by_dim, key=lambda d: (-by_dim[d], d))
    85→    return dict(by_dim), dim_names
    86→
    87→def open_review_ids_from_state(state: dict) -> set[str]:
    88→    """Return IDs of all open review/concerns issues in state."""
    89→    return {
    90→        fid for fid, f in state.get("issues", {}).items()
    91→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
    92→    }
    93→
    94→def triage_coverage(
    95→    plan: dict,
    96→    open_review_ids: set[str] | None = None,
    97→) -> tuple[int, int, dict]:
    98→    """Return (organized, total, clusters) for review issues in triage.
    99→
   100→    When *open_review_ids* is provided, use it as the full set of review
   101→    issues (from state) instead of falling back to queue_order.
   102→    """
   103→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   104→    all_cluster_ids: set[str] = set()
   105→    for c in clusters.values():
   106→        all_cluster_ids.update(c.get("issue_ids", []))
   107→    if open_review_ids is not None:
   108→        review_ids = list(open_review_ids)
   109→    else:
   110→        review_ids = [
   111→            fid for fid in plan.get("queue_order", [])
   112→            if not fid.startswith("triage::") and not fid.startswith("workflow::") and (fid.startswith("review::") or fid.startswith("concerns::"))
   113→        ]
   114→    organized = sum(1 for fid in review_ids if fid in all_cluster_ids)
   115→    return organized, len(review_ids), clusters
   116→
   117→def manual_clusters_with_issues(plan: dict) -> list[str]:
   118→    """Return names of non-auto clusters that have issues."""
   119→    return [
   120→        name for name, c in plan.get("clusters", {}).items()
   121→        if c.get("issue_ids") and not c.get("auto")
   122→    ]
   123→
   124→def apply_completion(
   125→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   126→    plan: dict,
   127→    strategy: str,
   128→    *,
   129→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   130→) -> None:
   131→    """Shared completion logic: update meta, remove triage::pending, save."""
   132→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
   133→    runtime = resolved_services.command_runtime(args)
   134→    state = runtime.state
   135→
   136→    organized, total, clusters = triage_coverage(
   137→        plan, open_review_ids=open_review_ids_from_state(state),
   138→    )
   139→
   140→    # Purge all triage stage IDs.
   141→    purge_ids(plan, list(TRIAGE_IDS))
   142→
   143→    current_hash = review_issue_snapshot_hash(state)
   144→
   145→    meta = plan.setdefault("epic_triage_meta", {})
   146→    meta["issue_snapshot_hash"] = current_hash
   147→    open_ids = sorted(
   148→        fid for fid, f in state.get("issues", {}).items()
   149→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   150→    )
   151→    meta["triaged_ids"] = open_ids
   152→    if strategy.strip().lower() != "same":
   153→        meta["strategy_summary"] = strategy
   154→    meta["trigger"] = "manual_triage"
   155→    meta["last_completed_at"] = utc_now()
   156→    # Archive stages before clearing so previous analysis is preserved
   157→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   158→    if stages:
   159→        meta["last_triage"] = {
   160→            "completed_at": utc_now(),
   161→            "stages": {k: dict(v) for k, v in stages.items()},
   162→            "strategy": strategy if strategy.strip().lower() != "same" else meta.get("strategy_summary", ""),
   163→        }
   164→    meta["triage_stages"] = {}  # clear stages on completion
   165→    meta.pop("stage_refresh_required", None)
   166→    meta.pop("stage_snapshot_hash", None)
   167→
   168→    resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
   169→
   170→    cluster_count = len([c for c in clusters.values() if c.get("issue_ids")])
   171→    print(colorize(f"  Triage complete: {organized}/{total} issues in {cluster_count} cluster(s).", "green"))
   172→    effective_strategy = strategy if strategy.strip().lower() != "same" else meta.get("strategy_summary", "")
   173→    if effective_strategy:
   174→        print(colorize(f"  Strategy: {effective_strategy}", "cyan"))
   175→    print(colorize("  Run `desloppify next` to start implementation.", "green"))
   176→
   177→def find_cluster_for(fid: str, clusters: dict) -> str | None:
   178→    """Return the cluster name containing *fid*, or None."""
   179→    for name, c in clusters.items():
   180→        if fid in c.get("issue_ids", []):
   181→            return name
   182→    return None
   183→
   184→def count_log_activity_since(plan: dict, since: str) -> dict[str, int]:
   185→    """Count execution log entries by action since *since* timestamp."""
   186→    counts: dict[str, int] = defaultdict(int)
   187→    for entry in plan.get("execution_log", []):
   188→        if entry.get("timestamp", "") >= since:
   189→            counts[entry.get("action", "unknown")] += 1
   190→    return dict(counts)
   191→
   192→__all__ = [
   193→    "apply_completion",
   194→    "cascade_clear_later_confirmations",
   195→    "count_log_activity_since",
   196→    "find_cluster_for",
   197→    "has_triage_in_queue",
   198→    "inject_triage_stages",
   199→    "manual_clusters_with_issues",
```

```
     3→from __future__ import annotations

     5→import json
     6→from collections.abc import Callable

    17→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    18→class BatchRunSummaryConfig:
    19→    """Inputs required to write the run_summary.json payload."""

    42→@dataclass
    43→class BatchProgressTracker:
    44→    """Tracks per-batch lifecycle state and emits progress/log events."""
    45→
    46→    selected_indexes: list[int]
    47→    prompt_files: dict[int, Path]
    48→    output_files: dict[int, Path]
    49→    log_files: dict[int, Path]
    50→    total_batches: int
    51→    colorize_fn: Callable[[str, str], str]
    52→    append_run_log_fn: Callable[[str], None]
    53→    stall_warning_seconds: int
    54→    batch_positions: dict[int, int] = field(init=False)
    55→    batch_status: dict[str, dict[str, object]] = field(init=False)
    56→    stall_warned_batches: set[int] = field(default_factory=set, init=False)
    57→
    58→    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
    59→        self.batch_positions = {
    60→            batch_idx: pos + 1 for pos, batch_idx in enumerate(self.selected_indexes)
    61→        }
    62→        self.batch_status = {
    63→            str(idx + 1): {
    64→                "position": self.batch_positions.get(idx, 0),
    65→                "status": "pending",
    66→                "prompt_path": str(self.prompt_files[idx]),
    67→                "result_path": str(self.output_files[idx]),
    68→                "log_path": str(self.log_files[idx]),
    69→            }
    70→            for idx in self.selected_indexes
    71→        }
    72→
    73→    def report(self, batch_index: int, event: str, code: int | None = None, **details) -> None:
    74→        if event == "heartbeat":
    75→            self._report_heartbeat(details)
    76→            return
    77→
    78→        position = self.batch_positions.get(batch_index, 0)
    79→        key = str(batch_index + 1)
    80→        state = self.batch_status.setdefault(
    81→            key,
    82→            {
    83→                "position": position,
    84→                "status": "pending",
    85→                "prompt_path": str(self.prompt_files.get(batch_index, "")),
    86→                "result_path": str(self.output_files.get(batch_index, "")),
    87→                "log_path": str(self.log_files.get(batch_index, "")),
    88→            },
    89→        )
    90→        if event == "queued":
    91→            state["status"] = "queued"
    92→            print(
    93→                self.colorize_fn(
    94→                    f"  Batch {position}/{self.total_batches} queued (#{batch_index + 1})",
    95→                    "dim",
    96→                )
    97→            )
    98→            self.append_run_log_fn(
    99→                f"batch-queued batch={batch_index + 1} position={position}/{self.total_batches}"
   100→            )
   101→            return
   102→        if event == "start":
   103→            state["status"] = "running"
   104→            state["started_at"] = datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(timespec="seconds")
   105→            print(
   106→                self.colorize_fn(
   107→                    f"  Batch {position}/{self.total_batches} started (#{batch_index + 1})",
   108→                    "dim",
   109→                )
   110→            )
   111→            self.append_run_log_fn(
   112→                f"batch-start batch={batch_index + 1} position={position}/{self.total_batches}"
   113→            )
   114→            return
   115→        if event != "done":
   116→            return
   117→        self._mark_done(batch_index, code=code, details=details)
   118→
   119→    def report_event(self, progress_event: BatchProgressEvent) -> None:
   120→        """Typed event entrypoint shared with runner_parallel callbacks."""
   121→        if not hasattr(progress_event, "batch_index") or not hasattr(
   122→            progress_event,
   123→            "event",
   124→        ):
   125→            return
   126→        details = getattr(progress_event, "details", {})
   127→        payload = details if isinstance(details, dict) else {}
   128→        self.report(
   129→            int(getattr(progress_event, "batch_index", -1)),
   130→            str(getattr(progress_event, "event", "")),
   131→            getattr(progress_event, "code", None),
   132→            **payload,
   133→        )
   134→
   135→    def record_execution_issue(self, batch_index: int, exc: Exception) -> None:
   136→        if batch_index < 0:
   137→            self.append_run_log_fn(f"execution-error heartbeat error={exc}")
   138→            return
   139→        self.append_run_log_fn(f"execution-error batch={batch_index + 1} error={exc}")
   140→
   141→    def mark_interrupted(self) -> None:
   142→        for idx in self.selected_indexes:
   143→            key = str(idx + 1)
   144→            state = self.batch_status.setdefault(
   145→                key,
   146→                {"position": self.batch_positions.get(idx, 0), "status": "pending"},
   147→            )
   148→            if state.get("status") in {"pending", "queued", "running"}:
   149→                state["status"] = "interrupted"
   150→
   151→    def mark_final_statuses(
   152→        self,
   153→        *,
   154→        selected_indexes: list[int],
   155→        failure_set: set[int],
   156→        execution_failure_set: set[int],
   157→    ) -> None:
   158→        for idx in selected_indexes:
   159→            key = str(idx + 1)
   160→            state = self.batch_status.setdefault(
   161→                key,
   162→                {"position": self.batch_positions.get(idx, 0), "status": "pending"},
   163→            )
   164→            if idx not in failure_set:
   165→                state["status"] = "succeeded"
   166→                continue
   167→            if idx in execution_failure_set:
   168→                state["status"] = "failed"
   169→                continue
   170→            if not self.output_files[idx].exists():
   171→                state["status"] = "missing_output"
   172→                continue
   173→            state["status"] = "parse_failed"
   174→
   175→    def _report_heartbeat(self, details: dict[str, object]) -> None:
   176→        active, queued, elapsed = _normalize_heartbeat_payload(details)
   177→        if not active and not queued:
   178→            return
   179→        segments = _heartbeat_segments(active, elapsed)
   180→        queued_segment = f", queued {len(queued)}" if queued else ""
   181→        print(
   182→            self.colorize_fn(
   183→                "  Batch heartbeat: "
   184→                f"{len(active)}/{self.total_batches} active{queued_segment} "
   185→                f"({', '.join(segments) if segments else 'running batches pending'})",
   186→                "dim",
   187→            )
   188→        )
   189→        self.append_run_log_fn(
   190→            "heartbeat "
   191→            f"active={[idx + 1 for idx in active]} queued={[idx + 1 for idx in queued]} "
   192→            f"elapsed={{{_heartbeat_elapsed_log(active, elapsed)}}}"
   193→        )
   194→        if self.stall_warning_seconds <= 0:
   195→            return
   196→        slow_active = _slow_active_batches(
   197→            active,
   198→            elapsed=elapsed,
   199→            threshold=self.stall_warning_seconds,
   200→        )
   201→        newly_warned = [idx for idx in slow_active if idx not in self.stall_warned_batches]
   202→        if not newly_warned:
   203→            return
   204→        self.stall_warned_batches.update(newly_warned)
   205→        warning_message = (
   206→            "  Stall warning: batches "
   207→            f"{[idx + 1 for idx in sorted(newly_warned)]} exceeded "
   208→            f"{self.stall_warning_seconds}s elapsed. "
   209→            "This may be normal for long runs; review run.log and batch logs."
   210→        )
   211→        print(self.colorize_fn(warning_message, "yellow"))
   212→        self.append_run_log_fn(
   213→            "stall-warning "
   214→            f"threshold={self.stall_warning_seconds}s "
   215→            f"batches={[idx + 1 for idx in sorted(newly_warned)]}"
   216→        )
   217→
   218→    def _mark_done(self, batch_index: int, *, code: int | None, details: dict[str, object]) -> None:
   219→        position = self.batch_positions.get(batch_index, 0)
   220→        key = str(batch_index + 1)
   221→        state = self.batch_status.setdefault(
   222→            key,
   223→            {
   224→                "position": position,
   225→                "status": "pending",
   226→                "prompt_path": str(self.prompt_files.get(batch_index, "")),
   227→                "result_path": str(self.output_files.get(batch_index, "")),
   228→                "log_path": str(self.log_files.get(batch_index, "")),
   229→            },
   230→        )
   231→        status = "done" if code == 0 else f"failed ({code})"
   232→        tone = "dim" if code == 0 else "yellow"
   233→        elapsed_seconds = details.get("elapsed_seconds")
   234→        elapsed_suffix = ""
   235→        if isinstance(elapsed_seconds, int | float):
   236→            elapsed_suffix = f" in {int(max(0, elapsed_seconds))}s"
   237→            state["elapsed_seconds"] = int(max(0, elapsed_seconds))
   238→        state["status"] = "succeeded" if code == 0 else "failed"
   239→        state["exit_code"] = int(code) if isinstance(code, int) else code
   240→        state["completed_at"] = datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(timespec="seconds")
   241→        self.stall_warned_batches.discard(batch_index)
   242→        print(
   243→            self.colorize_fn(
   244→                f"  Batch {position}/{self.total_batches} {status}{elapsed_suffix} (#{batch_index + 1})",
   245→                tone,
   246→            )
   247→        )
   248→        self.append_run_log_fn(
   249→            f"batch-done batch={batch_index + 1} position={position}/{self.total_batches} "
   250→            f"code={code} elapsed={state.get('elapsed_seconds', 0)}"
   251→        )
   252→
   253→
   254→def _normalize_heartbeat_payload(
   255→    details: dict[str, object],
   256→) -> tuple[list[int], list[int], dict[int, object]]:
   257→    active = details.get("active_batches")
   258→    queued = details.get("queued_batches", [])
   259→    elapsed = details.get("elapsed_seconds", {})
   260→    active_list = active if isinstance(active, list) else []
   261→    queued_list = queued if isinstance(queued, list) else []
   262→    elapsed_map = elapsed if isinstance(elapsed, dict) else {}
   263→    return active_list, queued_list, elapsed_map
   264→
   265→
   266→def _elapsed_seconds_for(elapsed: dict[int, object], index: int) -> int:
   267→    raw_secs = elapsed.get(index, 0)
   268→    return int(raw_secs) if isinstance(raw_secs, int | float) else 0
   269→
   270→
   271→def _heartbeat_segments(active: list[int], elapsed: dict[int, object]) -> list[str]:
   272→    segments: list[str] = []
   273→    for idx in active[:6]:
   274→        segments.append(f"#{idx + 1}:{_elapsed_seconds_for(elapsed, idx)}s")
   275→    if len(active) > 6:
   276→        segments.append(f"+{len(active) - 6} more")
   277→    return segments
   278→
   279→
   280→def _heartbeat_elapsed_log(active: list[int], elapsed: dict[int, object]) -> str:
   281→    return ", ".join(f"{idx + 1}:{elapsed.get(idx, 0)}" for idx in active)
   282→
   283→
   284→def _slow_active_batches(
   285→    active: list[int],
   286→    *,
   287→    elapsed: dict[int, object],
   288→    threshold: int,
   289→) -> list[int]:
   290→    return [
   291→        idx
   292→        for idx in active
   293→        if _elapsed_seconds_for(elapsed, idx) >= threshold
   294→    ]
   295→
   296→
   297→def resolve_run_log_path(
   298→    raw_run_log_file: object,
   299→    *,
   300→    project_root: Path,
   301→    run_dir: Path,
   302→) -> Path:
   303→    if isinstance(raw_run_log_file, str) and raw_run_log_file.strip():
   304→        candidate = Path(raw_run_log_file.strip()).expanduser()
   305→        run_log_path = candidate if candidate.is_absolute() else project_root / candidate
   306→    else:
   307→        run_log_path = run_dir / "run.log"
   308→    run_log_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   309→    return run_log_path
   310→
   311→
   312→def make_run_log_writer(run_log_path: Path) -> Callable[[str], None]:
   313→    def _append_run_log(message: str) -> None:
   314→        line = f"{datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(timespec='seconds')} {message}\n"
   315→        try:
   316→            with run_log_path.open("a", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
   317→                handle.write(line)
   318→        except OSError:
   319→            return
   320→
   321→    return _append_run_log
   322→
   323→
   324→def build_batch_tasks(
   325→    *,
   326→    selected_indexes: list[int],
   327→    prompt_files: dict[int, Path],
   328→    output_files: dict[int, Path],
   329→    log_files: dict[int, Path],
   330→    project_root: Path,
   331→    run_codex_batch_fn: Callable[..., int],
   332→) -> dict[int, Callable[[], int]]:
   333→    return {
   334→        idx: partial(
   335→            _run_batch_task,
   336→            batch_index=idx,
   337→            prompt_path=prompt_files[idx],
   338→            output_path=output_files[idx],
   339→            log_path=log_files[idx],
   340→            project_root=project_root,
   341→            run_codex_batch_fn=run_codex_batch_fn,
   342→        )
   343→        for idx in selected_indexes
   344→    }
   345→
   346→
   347→def write_run_summary(
   348→    *,
   349→    summary_path: Path,
   350→    summary_config: BatchRunSummaryConfig,
   351→    batch_status: dict[str, dict[str, object]],
   352→    successful_batches: list[int],
   353→    failed_batches: list[int],
   354→    safe_write_text_fn: Callable[[Path, str], None],
   355→    colorize_fn: Callable[[str, str], str],
   356→    append_run_log_fn: Callable[[str], None],
   357→    interrupted: bool = False,
   358→    interruption_reason: str | None = None,
   359→) -> None:
   360→    run_summary: dict[str, object] = {
   361→        "created_at": summary_config.created_at,
   362→        "run_stamp": summary_config.run_stamp,
   363→        "runner": summary_config.runner,
   364→        "parallel": summary_config.run_parallel,
   365→        "selected_batches": [idx + 1 for idx in summary_config.selected_indexes],
   366→        "successful_batches": successful_batches,
   367→        "failed_batches": failed_batches,
   368→        "allow_partial": summary_config.allow_partial,
   369→        "max_parallel_batches": (
   370→            summary_config.max_parallel_batches if summary_config.run_parallel else 1
   371→        ),
   372→        "batch_timeout_seconds": summary_config.batch_timeout_seconds,
   373→        "batch_max_retries": summary_config.batch_max_retries,
   374→        "batch_retry_backoff_seconds": summary_config.batch_retry_backoff_seconds,
   375→        "batch_heartbeat_seconds": (
   376→            summary_config.heartbeat_seconds if summary_config.run_parallel else None
   377→        ),
   378→        "batch_stall_warning_seconds": (
   379→            summary_config.stall_warning_seconds if summary_config.run_parallel else None
   380→        ),
   381→        "batch_stall_kill_seconds": summary_config.stall_kill_seconds,
   382→        "immutable_packet": str(summary_config.immutable_packet_path),
   383→        "blind_packet": str(summary_config.prompt_packet_path),
   384→        "run_dir": str(summary_config.run_dir),
   385→        "logs_dir": str(summary_config.logs_dir),
   386→        "run_log": str(summary_config.run_log_path),
   387→        "batches": batch_status,
   388→    }
   389→    if isinstance(summary_config.backlog_gate, dict):
   390→        run_summary["backlog_gate"] = summary_config.backlog_gate
   391→    if interrupted:
   392→        run_summary["interrupted"] = True
   393→        if interruption_reason:
   394→            run_summary["interruption_reason"] = interruption_reason
   395→    safe_write_text_fn(summary_path, json.dumps(run_summary, indent=2) + "\n")
   396→    print(colorize_fn(f"  Run summary: {summary_path}", "dim"))
   397→    append_run_log_fn(f"run-summary {summary_path}")
   398→
   399→
   400→def _run_batch_task(
   401→    *,
   402→    batch_index: int,
   403→    prompt_path: Path,
   404→    output_path: Path,
   405→    log_path: Path,
   406→    project_root: Path,
   407→    run_codex_batch_fn: Callable[..., int],
   408→) -> int:
   409→    try:
   410→        prompt = prompt_path.read_text()
   411→    except OSError as exc:
   412→        raise RuntimeError(
   413→            f"unable to read prompt for batch #{batch_index + 1}: {prompt_path}"
   414→        ) from exc
   415→    return run_codex_batch_fn(
   416→        prompt=prompt,
   417→        repo_root=project_root,
   418→        output_file=output_path,
   419→        log_file=log_path,
   420→    )
   421→
   422→
   423→__all__ = [
   424→    "BatchProgressTracker",
   425→    "BatchRunSummaryConfig",
   426→    "build_batch_tasks",
   427→    "make_run_log_writer",
   428→    "resolve_run_log_path",
   429→    "write_run_summary",
   430→]
   431→
```

```
1→"""Epic triage orchestrator and public API."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→import logging
     7→from dataclasses import dataclass
     8→from typing import Any
     9→
    10→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    11→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    12→
    13→from .epic_triage_apply import TriageMutationResult, apply_triage_to_plan
    14→from .epic_triage_parsing import (
    15→    ISSUE_ID_RE,
    16→    extract_issue_citations,
    17→    parse_triage_result,
    18→)
    19→from .epic_triage_prompt import (
    20→    _TRIAGE_SYSTEM_PROMPT,
    21→    ContradictionNote,
    22→    DismissedIssue,
    23→    TriageInput,
    24→    TriageResult,
    25→    build_triage_prompt,

    30→
    31→
    32→def last_real_review_timestamp(state: dict) -> str | None:
    33→    """ISO timestamp of most recent genuine review import (not manual override/scan reset)."""
    34→    REAL_MODES = {"holistic", "per_file", "trusted_internal", "attested_external"}
    35→    audit = state.get("assessment_import_audit", [])
    36→    if isinstance(audit, list):
    37→        for entry in reversed(audit):
    38→            if isinstance(entry, dict) and entry.get("mode") in REAL_MODES:
    39→                ts = entry.get("timestamp")
    40→                if ts:
    41→                    return str(ts)
    42→    holistic = (state.get("review_cache") or {}).get("holistic")
    43→    if isinstance(holistic, dict):
    44→        return holistic.get("reviewed_at")
    45→    return None
    46→
    47→def detect_recurring_patterns(
    48→    open_issues: dict[str, dict],
    49→    resolved_issues: dict[str, dict],
    50→) -> dict[str, dict]:
    51→    """Detect dimensions with both resolved AND current open issues.
    52→
    53→    Returns ``{dimension: {"open": [ids], "resolved": [ids]}}``.
    54→    A dimension with both resolved and open issues signals a potential
    55→    loop — similar issues recur after previous fixes.
    56→    """
    57→    def _dimension(f: dict) -> str:
    58→        detail = f.get("detail", {})
    59→        if isinstance(detail, dict):
    60→            return detail.get("dimension", "")
    61→        return ""
    62→
    63→    open_by_dim: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    64→    for fid, f in open_issues.items():
    65→        dim = _dimension(f)
    66→        if dim:
    67→            open_by_dim.setdefault(dim, []).append(fid)
    68→
    69→    resolved_by_dim: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    70→    for fid, f in resolved_issues.items():
    71→        dim = _dimension(f)
    72→        if dim:
    73→            resolved_by_dim.setdefault(dim, []).append(fid)
    74→
    75→    recurring: dict[str, dict] = {}
    76→    for dim in set(open_by_dim) & set(resolved_by_dim):
    77→        recurring[dim] = {
    78→            "open": open_by_dim[dim],
    79→            "resolved": resolved_by_dim[dim],
    80→        }
    81→    return recurring
    82→
    83→@dataclass
    84→class TriageDeps:
    85→    """Injectable dependencies for the triage engine."""
    86→
    87→    llm_call: Any = None  # Callable[[str, str], str] — (system, user) -> response
    88→
    89→def triage_epics(
    90→    plan: PlanModel,
    91→    state: StateModel,
    92→    *,
    93→    deps: TriageDeps | None = None,
    94→    dry_run: bool = False,
    95→    trigger: str = "manual",
    96→) -> TriageMutationResult:
    97→    """Run epic triage: collect input, call LLM, apply results.
    98→
    99→    If ``dry_run`` is True, collects input and builds prompt but does not
   100→    call the LLM or mutate the plan.
   101→
   102→    If ``deps.llm_call`` is None, returns a dry-run result with the prompt.
   103→    """
   104→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   105→    si = collect_triage_input(plan, state)
   106→
   107→    prompt = build_triage_prompt(si)
   108→    valid_ids = set(si.open_issues.keys())
   109→
   110→    if dry_run or deps is None or deps.llm_call is None:
   111→        result = TriageMutationResult(dry_run=True)
   112→        result.strategy_summary = f"[dry-run] Prompt built with {len(si.open_issues)} issues"
   113→        return result
   114→
   115→    # Call LLM
   116→    try:
   117→        raw_response = deps.llm_call(_TRIAGE_SYSTEM_PROMPT, prompt)
   118→        raw_json = json.loads(raw_response)
   119→    except (json.JSONDecodeError, TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
   120→        logger.error("Epic triage LLM response parse error: %s", exc)
   121→        result = TriageMutationResult()
   122→        result.strategy_summary = f"Triage failed: {exc}"
   123→        return result
   124→
   125→    triage = parse_triage_result(raw_json, valid_ids)
   126→    return apply_triage_to_plan(plan, state, triage, trigger=trigger)
   127→
   128→__all__ = [
   129→    "ContradictionNote",
   130→    "DismissedIssue",
   131→    "ISSUE_ID_RE",
   132→    "TriageDeps",
   133→    "TriageInput",
   134→    "TriageMutationResult",
   135→    "TriageResult",
   136→    "apply_triage_to_plan",

   141→    "last_real_review_timestamp",
   142→    "parse_triage_result",

   144→]
   145→
```

```
1→"""Prompt construction and data models for epic triage."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     6→from typing import Any
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
     9→    Cluster,
    10→    PlanModel,
    11→    ensure_plan_defaults,
    12→    triage_clusters,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    15→
    16→
    17→@dataclass
    18→class TriageInput:
    19→    """All data the LLM needs to produce/update epics."""
    20→
    21→    open_issues: dict[str, dict]       # id -> issue (review + concerns)
    22→    mechanical_issues: dict[str, dict]  # id -> issue (non-review, for context)
    23→    existing_epics: dict[str, Cluster]    # current triage-clusters to update
    24→    dimension_scores: dict[str, Any]      # for context
    25→    new_since_last: set[str]             # issue IDs new since last triage
    26→    resolved_since_last: set[str]        # issue IDs resolved since last
    27→    previously_dismissed: list[str]      # IDs dismissed in prior triage
    28→    triage_version: int                  # next version number
    29→    resolved_issues: dict[str, dict]   # full issue objects for resolved IDs
    30→    completed_clusters: list[dict]       # clusters completed since last triage
    31→
    32→@dataclass
    33→class DismissedIssue:
    34→    """A issue the LLM says doesn't make sense."""
    35→
    36→    issue_id: str
    37→    reason: str
    38→
    39→@dataclass
    40→class ContradictionNote:
    41→    """Record of a resolved contradiction."""
    42→
    43→    kept: str
    44→    dismissed: str
    45→    reason: str
    46→
    47→@dataclass
    48→class TriageResult:
    49→    """Parsed and validated LLM triage output."""
    50→
    51→    strategy_summary: str
    52→    epics: list[dict]
    53→    dismissed_issues: list[DismissedIssue] = field(default_factory=list)
    54→    contradiction_notes: list[ContradictionNote] = field(default_factory=list)
    55→    priority_rationale: str = ""
    56→
    57→def collect_triage_input(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> TriageInput:
    58→    """Gather all data needed for the triage LLM prompt."""
    59→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    60→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
    61→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    62→    epics = triage_clusters(plan)
    63→
    64→    open_review: dict[str, dict] = {}
    65→    open_mechanical: dict[str, dict] = {}
    66→    for fid, f in issues.items():
    67→        if f.get("status") != "open":
    68→            continue
    69→        if f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns"):
    70→            open_review[fid] = f
    71→        else:
    72→            open_mechanical[fid] = f
    73→
    74→    triaged_ids = set(meta.get("triaged_ids", []))
    75→    current_review_ids = set(open_review.keys())
    76→    new_since = current_review_ids - triaged_ids
    77→    resolved_since = triaged_ids - current_review_ids
    78→    previously_dismissed = list(meta.get("dismissed_ids", []))
    79→    version = int(meta.get("version", 0)) + 1
    80→
    81→    # Resolved issue objects (for REFLECT stage)
    82→    resolved_issue_objs = {
    83→        fid: issues[fid] for fid in resolved_since if fid in issues
    84→    }
    85→
    86→    # Completed clusters since last triage completion
    87→    last_completed = meta.get("last_completed_at", "")
    88→    all_completed: list[dict] = plan.get("completed_clusters", [])
    89→    if last_completed:
    90→        recent_completed = [
    91→            c for c in all_completed
    92→            if c.get("completed_at", "") > last_completed
    93→        ]
    94→    else:
    95→        recent_completed = list(all_completed)
    96→
    97→    return TriageInput(
    98→        open_issues=open_review,
    99→        mechanical_issues=open_mechanical,
   100→        existing_epics=dict(epics),
   101→        dimension_scores=state.get("dimension_scores", {}),
   102→        new_since_last=new_since,
   103→        resolved_since_last=resolved_since,
   104→        previously_dismissed=previously_dismissed,
   105→        triage_version=version,
   106→        resolved_issues=resolved_issue_objs,
   107→        completed_clusters=recent_completed,
   108→    )
   109→
   110→_TRIAGE_SYSTEM_PROMPT = """\
   111→You are maintaining the meta-plan for this codebase. Your goal is to produce
   112→a coherent, prioritized strategy to address ALL open review issues.
   113→
   114→Your plan should:
   115→- Cluster issues by ROOT CAUSE, not by dimension or detector
   116→- Give each cluster (epic) a clear thesis: one imperative sentence
   117→- Order epics by dependency: what must be done first for later work to make sense
   118→- Dismiss issues that don't make sense, are contradictory, or are false positives
   119→- Mark which epics are agent-safe (can be executed mechanically) vs need human judgment
   120→- Avoid creating work that contradicts other work in the plan
   121→- Be ambitious but realistic — aim to resolve all issues coherently
   122→
   123→Available directions for epics: delete, merge, flatten, enforce, simplify, decompose, extract, inline.
   124→
   125→Available plan tools (the agent executing your plan has access to these):
   126→- `desloppify plan queue` — view all items in priority order
   127→- `desloppify plan focus epic/<name>` — focus the queue on one epic
   128→- `desloppify plan skip <id> --permanent --note "why" --attest "..."` — permanently dismiss
   129→- `desloppify plan skip <id> --note "revisit later"` — temporarily defer
   130→- `desloppify plan resolve <id> --note "what I did" --attest "..."` — mark resolved
   131→- `desloppify plan reorder <id> top|bottom|before|after <target>` — reorder
   132→- `desloppify plan cluster show <name>` — inspect a cluster
   133→- `desloppify scan` — re-scan after making changes to verify progress
   134→- `desloppify show review --status open` — see all open review issues
   135→
   136→Your output defines the ENTIRE work plan. Issues not assigned to any epic
   137→will remain in the queue as individual items. Dismissed issues will be
   138→removed from the queue with your stated reason.
   139→
   140→Respond with a single JSON object matching this schema:
   141→{
   142→  "strategy_summary": "2-4 sentence narrative: what the meta-plan says, top priorities, current state",
   143→  "epics": [
   144→    {
   145→      "name": "slug-name",
   146→      "thesis": "imperative one-liner",
   147→      "direction": "delete|merge|flatten|enforce|simplify|decompose|extract|inline",
   148→      "root_cause": "why this cluster exists",
   149→      "issue_ids": ["id1", "id2"],
   150→      "dismissed": ["id3"],
   151→      "agent_safe": true,
   152→      "dependency_order": 1,
   153→      "action_steps": ["step 1", "step 2"],
   154→      "status": "pending"
   155→    }
   156→  ],
   157→  "dismissed_issues": [
   158→    {"issue_id": "id", "reason": "why this issue doesn't make sense"}
   159→  ],
   160→  "contradiction_notes": [
   161→    {"kept": "issue_id", "dismissed": "issue_id", "reason": "why"}
   162→  ],
   163→  "priority_rationale": "why the dependency_order is what it is"
   164→}
   165→"""
   166→
   167→def build_triage_prompt(si: TriageInput) -> str:
   168→    """Build the user-facing prompt content with all issue data."""
   169→    parts: list[str] = []
   170→
   171→    # Section: existing epics
   172→    if si.existing_epics:
   173→        parts.append("## Existing Epics (update these, don't recreate)")
   174→        for name, epic in sorted(si.existing_epics.items()):
   175→            status = epic.get("status", "pending")
   176→            thesis = epic.get("thesis", "")
   177→            direction = epic.get("direction", "")
   178→            fids = epic.get("issue_ids", [])
   179→            parts.append(
   180→                f"- {name} [{status}] ({direction}): {thesis}"
   181→                f"\n  Issues: {', '.join(fids[:10])}"
   182→                f"{'...' if len(fids) > 10 else ''}"
   183→            )
   184→        parts.append("")
   185→
   186→    # Section: what changed
   187→    if si.new_since_last:
   188→        parts.append(f"## New issues since last triage ({len(si.new_since_last)})")
   189→        for fid in sorted(si.new_since_last):
   190→            f = si.open_issues.get(fid, {})
   191→            parts.append(f"- {fid}: {f.get('summary', '(no summary)')}")
   192→        parts.append("")
   193→
   194→    if si.resolved_since_last:
   195→        parts.append(f"## Resolved since last triage ({len(si.resolved_since_last)})")
   196→        for fid in sorted(si.resolved_since_last):
   197→            parts.append(f"- {fid}")
   198→        parts.append("")
   199→
   200→    # Section: all open review issues
   201→    parts.append(f"## All open review issues ({len(si.open_issues)})")
   202→    for fid, f in sorted(si.open_issues.items()):
   203→        detail = f.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(f.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   204→        suggestion = detail.get("suggestion", "")
   205→        dimension = detail.get("dimension", "")
   206→        confidence = f.get("confidence", "medium")
   207→        file_path = f.get("file", "")
   208→        summary = f.get("summary", "")
   209→        parts.append(f"- [{confidence}] {fid}")
   210→        parts.append(f"  File: {file_path}")
   211→        if dimension:
   212→            parts.append(f"  Dimension: {dimension}")
   213→        parts.append(f"  Summary: {summary}")
   214→        if suggestion:
   215→            parts.append(f"  Suggestion: {suggestion}")
   216→    parts.append("")
   217→
   218→    # Section: dimension scores for context
   219→    if si.dimension_scores:
   220→        parts.append("## Dimension scores (context)")
   221→        for name, data in sorted(si.dimension_scores.items()):
   222→            if isinstance(data, dict):
   223→                score = data.get("score", "?")
   224→                strict = data.get("strict", score)
   225→                issues = data.get("failing", 0)
   226→                parts.append(f"- {name}: {score}% (strict: {strict}%, {issues} issues)")
   227→        parts.append("")
   228→
   229→    # Section: previously dismissed
   230→    if si.previously_dismissed:
   231→        parts.append(f"## Previously dismissed ({len(si.previously_dismissed)})")
   232→        parts.append("Maintain unless contradicted by new evidence.")
   233→        for fid in si.previously_dismissed:
   234→            parts.append(f"- {fid}")
   235→        parts.append("")
   236→
   237→    return "\n".join(parts)
   238→
   239→__all__ = [
   240→    "_TRIAGE_SYSTEM_PROMPT",
   241→    "ContradictionNote",
   242→    "DismissedIssue",
   243→    "TriageInput",
   244→    "TriageResult",
   245→    "build_triage_prompt",
   246→    "collect_triage_input",
   247→]
   248→
```

```
1→"""CLI parser group builder for plan command family."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→
     8→def _add_queue_subparser(plan_sub) -> None:
     9→    p_queue = plan_sub.add_parser("queue", help="Compact table of upcoming queue items")
    10→    p_queue.add_argument("--top", type=int, default=30, help="Max items (default: 30, 0=all)")
    11→    p_queue.add_argument("--cluster", type=str, default=None, metavar="NAME",
    12→                         help="Filter to a specific cluster")
    13→    p_queue.add_argument("--include-skipped", action="store_true",
    14→                         help="Include skipped items at end")
    15→    p_queue.add_argument("--sort", choices=["priority", "recent"], default="priority",
    16→                         help="Sort order (default: priority)")
    17→
    18→
    19→def _add_reorder_subparser(plan_sub) -> None:
    20→    p_move = plan_sub.add_parser(
    21→        "reorder",
    22→        help="Reposition issues in the queue",
    23→        epilog="""\
    24→patterns accept issue IDs, detector names, file paths, globs, or cluster names.
    25→cluster names expand to all member IDs automatically.
    26→
    27→examples:
    28→  desloppify plan reorder security top                         # all issues from detector
    29→  desloppify plan reorder "unused::src/foo.ts::*" top          # glob pattern
    30→  desloppify plan reorder smells bottom                        # deprioritize
    31→  desloppify plan reorder my-cluster top                       # cluster members
    32→  desloppify plan reorder my-cluster unused top                # mix clusters + issues
    33→  desloppify plan reorder unused before -t security            # before a issue/cluster
    34→  desloppify plan reorder smells after -t my-cluster           # after a cluster
    35→  desloppify plan reorder security up -t 3                     # shift up 3 positions""",
    36→        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,
    37→    )
    38→    p_move.add_argument(
    39→        "patterns", nargs="+", metavar="PATTERN",
    40→        help="Issue ID(s), detector, file path, glob, or cluster name",
    41→    )
    42→    p_move.add_argument(
    43→        "position", choices=["top", "bottom", "before", "after", "up", "down"],
    44→        help="Where to move",
    45→    )
    46→    p_move.add_argument(
    47→        "-t", "--target", default=None,
    48→        help="Required for before/after (issue ID or cluster name) and up/down (integer offset)",
    49→    )
    50→
    51→

    70→def _add_skip_subparsers(plan_sub) -> None:

    72→    p_skip = plan_sub.add_parser(

   383→def add_plan_parser(sub) -> None:
   384→    p_plan = sub.add_parser(
   385→        "plan",
   386→        help="Living plan: generate, show, resolve, skip, cluster, triage",
   387→        description="""\
   388→Manage the living plan — a persistent layer on top of the work queue.
   389→Track custom ordering, clusters, skips, and per-issue annotations.
   390→Run with no subcommand to generate a full prioritized markdown plan.""",
   391→        epilog="""\
   392→typical workflow:
   393→  desloppify scan                       # detect issues
   394→  desloppify plan                       # full prioritized markdown
   395→  desloppify plan queue                 # compact table of all items
   396→  desloppify plan cluster create ...    # group related issues
   397→  desloppify plan focus <cluster>       # narrow scope
   398→  desloppify next                       # work on the next item
   399→  desloppify plan resolve <id> --attest .. # mark as fixed
   400→
   401→patterns (used by reorder, skip, resolve, describe, note, etc.):
   402→  Patterns match issues by detector, file, ID prefix, glob, or name.
   403→  Cluster names also work as patterns — they expand to all member IDs.
   404→  Examples: "security", "src/foo.py", "unused::*React*", "my-cluster"
   405→
   406→subcommands:
   407→  show       Show plan metadata summary
   408→  queue      Compact table of upcoming queue items
   409→  reset      Reset plan to empty
   410→  reorder    Reposition issues or clusters in the queue
   411→  resolve    Mark issues as fixed (score movement + next-step)
   412→  describe   Set augmented description
   413→  note       Set note on issues
   414→  skip       Skip issues (temporary/permanent/false_positive)
   415→  unskip     Bring skipped issues back to queue
   416→  reopen     Reopen resolved issues
   417→  focus      Set or clear active cluster focus
   418→  cluster    Manage issue clusters
   419→  triage     Staged triage workflow (after review)
   420→  scan-gate  Check or skip scan requirement for workflow items""",
   421→        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,
   422→    )
   423→    p_plan.add_argument("--state", type=str, default=None, help="Path to state file")
   424→    p_plan.add_argument(
   425→        "--output", type=str, metavar="FILE",
   426→        help="Write to file instead of stdout (refuses to overwrite existing files)",
   427→    )
   428→
   429→    plan_sub = p_plan.add_subparsers(dest="plan_action")
```
