    38→def _record_execution_issue(append_run_log_fn, batch_index: int, exc: Exception) -> None:
    39→    """Record one execute_batches callback/task failure in run.log."""
    40→    if batch_index < 0:
    41→        append_run_log_fn(f"execution-error heartbeat error={exc}")
    42→        return
    43→    append_run_log_fn(f"execution-error batch={batch_index + 1} error={exc}")
    44→
    45→
    46→def _build_progress_reporter(
    47→    *,
    48→    batch_positions: dict[int, int],
    49→    batch_status: dict[str, dict[str, object]],
    50→    stall_warned_batches: set[int],
    51→    total_batches: int,
    52→    stall_warning_seconds: float,
    53→    prompt_files: dict,
    54→    output_files: dict,
    55→    log_files: dict,
    56→    append_run_log,
    57→    colorize_fn,
    58→):
    59→    """Build the _report_progress closure used during batch execution."""
    60→
    61→    def _report_progress(
    62→        progress_event: BatchProgressEvent,
    63→    ) -> None:
    64→        batch_index = progress_event.batch_index
    65→        event = progress_event.event
    66→        code = progress_event.code
    67→        details = progress_event.details
    68→        if event == "heartbeat":
    69→            _handle_heartbeat(
    70→                details=details,
    71→                total_batches=total_batches,
    72→                stall_warning_seconds=stall_warning_seconds,
    73→                stall_warned_batches=stall_warned_batches,
    74→                append_run_log=append_run_log,
    75→                colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
    76→            )
    77→            return
    78→
    79→        position = batch_positions.get(batch_index, 0)
    80→        key = str(batch_index + 1)
    81→        state = batch_status.setdefault(
    82→            key,
    83→            {
    84→                "position": position,
    85→                "status": "pending",
    86→                "prompt_path": str(prompt_files.get(batch_index, "")),
    87→                "result_path": str(output_files.get(batch_index, "")),
    88→                "log_path": str(log_files.get(batch_index, "")),
    89→            },
    90→        )
    91→        if event == "queued":
    92→            state["status"] = "queued"
    93→            print(
    94→                colorize_fn(
    95→                    f"  Batch {position}/{total_batches} queued (#{batch_index + 1})",
    96→                    "dim",
    97→                )
    98→            )
    99→            append_run_log(f"batch-queued batch={batch_index + 1} position={position}/{total_batches}")
   100→            return
   101→        if event == "start":
   102→            state["status"] = "running"
   103→            state["started_at"] = datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(timespec="seconds")
   104→            print(
   105→                colorize_fn(
   106→                    f"  Batch {position}/{total_batches} started (#{batch_index + 1})",
   107→                    "dim",
   108→                )
   109→            )
   110→            append_run_log(f"batch-start batch={batch_index + 1} position={position}/{total_batches}")
   111→            return
   112→        if event == "done":
   113→            status = "done" if code == 0 else f"failed ({code})"
   114→            tone = "dim" if code == 0 else "yellow"
   115→            elapsed_seconds = details.get("elapsed_seconds")
   116→            elapsed_suffix = ""
   117→            if isinstance(elapsed_seconds, int | float):
   118→                elapsed_suffix = f" in {int(max(0, elapsed_seconds))}s"
   119→                state["elapsed_seconds"] = int(max(0, elapsed_seconds))
   120→            state["status"] = "succeeded" if code == 0 else "failed"
   121→            state["exit_code"] = int(code) if isinstance(code, int) else code
   122→            state["completed_at"] = datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(timespec="seconds")
   123→            if batch_index in stall_warned_batches:
   124→                stall_warned_batches.discard(batch_index)
   125→            print(
   126→                colorize_fn(
   127→                    f"  Batch {position}/{total_batches} {status}{elapsed_suffix} (#{batch_index + 1})",
   128→                    tone,
   129→                )
   130→            )
   131→            append_run_log(
   132→                f"batch-done batch={batch_index + 1} position={position}/{total_batches} "
   133→                f"code={code} elapsed={state.get('elapsed_seconds', 0)}"
   134→            )
   135→
   136→    return _report_progress
   137→
   138→
   139→def _handle_heartbeat(
   140→    *,
   141→    details: dict,
   142→    total_batches: int,
   143→    stall_warning_seconds: float,
   144→    stall_warned_batches: set[int],
   145→    append_run_log,
   146→    colorize_fn,
   147→) -> None:
   148→    """Handle a heartbeat progress event — print status and stall warnings."""
   149→    active = details.get("active_batches")
   150→    queued = details.get("queued_batches", [])
   151→    elapsed = details.get("elapsed_seconds", {})
   152→    if not isinstance(active, list):
   153→        active = []
   154→    if not isinstance(queued, list):
   155→        queued = []
   156→    if not active and not queued:
   157→        return
   158→    segments: list[str] = []
   159→    for idx in active[:6]:
   160→        secs = 0
   161→        if isinstance(elapsed, dict):
   162→            raw_secs = elapsed.get(idx, 0)
   163→            secs = int(raw_secs) if isinstance(raw_secs, int | float) else 0
   164→        segments.append(f"#{idx + 1}:{secs}s")
   165→    if len(active) > 6:
   166→        segments.append(f"+{len(active) - 6} more")
   167→    queued_segment = ""
   168→    if queued:
   169→        queued_segment = f", queued {len(queued)}"
   170→    print(
   171→        colorize_fn(
   172→            "  Batch heartbeat: "
   173→            f"{len(active)}/{total_batches} active{queued_segment} "
   174→            f"({', '.join(segments) if segments else 'running batches pending'})",
   175→            "dim",
   176→        )
   177→    )
   178→    append_run_log(
   179→        "heartbeat "
   180→        f"active={[idx + 1 for idx in active]} queued={[idx + 1 for idx in queued]} "
   181→        f"elapsed={{{', '.join(f'{idx + 1}:{elapsed.get(idx, 0)}' for idx in active)}}}"
   182→    )
   183→    if stall_warning_seconds > 0 and isinstance(elapsed, dict):
   184→        slow_active = [
   185→            idx
   186→            for idx in active
   187→            if isinstance(elapsed.get(idx), int | float)
   188→            and int(elapsed.get(idx) or 0) >= stall_warning_seconds
   189→        ]
   190→        newly_warned = [idx for idx in slow_active if idx not in stall_warned_batches]
   191→        if newly_warned:
   192→            stall_warned_batches.update(newly_warned)
   193→            warning_message = (
   194→                "  Stall warning: batches "
   195→                f"{[idx + 1 for idx in sorted(newly_warned)]} exceeded "
   196→                f"{stall_warning_seconds}s elapsed. "
   197→                "This may be normal for long runs; review run.log and batch logs."
   198→            )
   199→            print(colorize_fn(warning_message, "yellow"))
   200→            append_run_log(
   201→                "stall-warning "
   202→                f"threshold={stall_warning_seconds}s batches={[idx + 1 for idx in sorted(newly_warned)]}"
   203→            )
   204→
   205→
   206→def _collect_and_reconcile_results(
   207→    *,
   208→    collect_batch_results_fn,
   209→    selected_indexes: list[int],
   210→    execution_failures: list[int],
   211→    output_files: dict,
   212→    packet: dict,
   213→    batch_positions: dict[int, int],
   214→    batch_status: dict[str, dict[str, object]],
   215→) -> tuple[list[dict], list[int], list[int], set[int]]:
   216→    """Collect batch results and reconcile per-batch status entries.
   217→
   218→    Returns (batch_results, successful_indexes, failures, failure_set).
   219→    """
   220→    allowed_dims = {
   221→        str(dim) for dim in packet.get("dimensions", []) if isinstance(dim, str)
   222→    }
   223→    batch_results, failures = collect_batch_results_fn(
   224→        selected_indexes=selected_indexes,
   225→        failures=execution_failures,
   226→        output_files=output_files,
   227→        allowed_dims=allowed_dims,
   228→    )
   229→
   230→    execution_failure_set = set(execution_failures)
   231→    failure_set = set(failures)
   232→    successful_indexes = sorted(idx for idx in selected_indexes if idx not in failure_set)
   233→    for idx in selected_indexes:
   234→        key = str(idx + 1)
   235→        state = batch_status.setdefault(
   236→            key,
   237→            {"position": batch_positions.get(idx, 0), "status": "pending"},
   238→        )
   239→        if idx not in failure_set:
   240→            state["status"] = "succeeded"
   241→            continue
   242→        if idx in execution_failure_set:
   243→            state["status"] = "failed"
   244→            continue
   245→        if not output_files[idx].exists():
   246→            state["status"] = "missing_output"
   247→            continue
   248→        state["status"] = "parse_failed"
   249→
   250→    return batch_results, successful_indexes, failures, failure_set
   251→
   252→
   253→def _merge_and_write_results(
   254→    *,
   255→    merge_batch_results_fn,
   256→    build_import_provenance_fn,
   257→    batch_results: list[dict],
   258→    batches: list,
   259→    successful_indexes: list[int],
   260→    packet: dict,
   261→    packet_dimensions: list[str],
   262→    scored_dimensions: list[str],
   263→    scan_path: str,
   264→    runner: str,
   265→    prompt_packet_path: Path,
   266→    stamp: str,
   267→    run_dir: Path,
   268→    safe_write_text_fn,
   269→    colorize_fn,
   270→) -> Path:
   271→    """Merge batch results, enrich with metadata, write to disk. Returns merged_path."""
   272→    merged = merge_batch_results_fn(batch_results)
   273→    reviewed_files = collect_reviewed_files_from_batches(
   274→        batches=batches,
   275→        selected_indexes=successful_indexes,
   276→    )
   277→    full_sweep_included = any(
   278→        str(batch.get("name", "")).strip().lower() == "full codebase sweep"
   279→        for idx in successful_indexes
   280→        if 0 <= idx < len(batches)
   281→        for batch in [batches[idx]]
   282→        if isinstance(batch, dict)
   283→    )
   284→    review_scope: dict[str, object] = {
   285→        "reviewed_files_count": len(reviewed_files),
   286→        "successful_batch_count": len(successful_indexes),
   287→        "full_sweep_included": full_sweep_included,
   288→    }
   289→    total_files = packet.get("total_files")
   290→    if isinstance(total_files, int) and not isinstance(total_files, bool) and total_files > 0:
   291→        review_scope["total_files"] = total_files
   292→    merged["review_scope"] = review_scope
   293→    if reviewed_files:
   294→        merged["reviewed_files"] = reviewed_files
   295→        print(
   296→            colorize_fn(
   297→                f"  Reviewed files captured for cache refresh: {len(reviewed_files)}",
   298→                "dim",
   299→            )
   300→        )
   301→    merged["provenance"] = build_import_provenance_fn(
   302→        runner=runner,
   303→        blind_packet_path=prompt_packet_path,
   304→        run_stamp=stamp,
   305→        batch_indexes=successful_indexes,
   306→    )
   307→    merged_assessment_dims = normalize_dimension_list(
   308→        list((merged.get("assessments") or {}).keys())
   309→    )
   310→    merged_issue_dims = normalize_dimension_list(
   311→        [
   312→            issue.get("dimension")
   313→            for issue in (merged.get("issues") or [])
   314→            if isinstance(issue, dict)
   315→        ]
   316→    )
   317→    merged_imported_dims = normalize_dimension_list(
   318→        merged_assessment_dims + merged_issue_dims
   319→    )
   320→    review_scope["imported_dimensions"] = merged_imported_dims
   321→    missing_after_import = print_import_dimension_coverage_notice(
   322→        assessed_dims=merged_assessment_dims,
   323→        scored_dims=scored_dimensions,
   324→        scan_path=scan_path,
   325→        colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
   326→    )
   327→    merged["assessment_coverage"] = {
   328→        "scored_dimensions": scored_dimensions,
   329→        "selected_dimensions": packet_dimensions,
   330→        "imported_dimensions": merged_assessment_dims,
   331→        "missing_dimensions": missing_after_import,
   332→    }
   333→    merged_path = run_dir / "holistic_issues_merged.json"
   334→    safe_write_text_fn(merged_path, json.dumps(merged, indent=2) + "\n")
   335→    print(colorize_fn(f"\n  Merged outputs: {merged_path}", "bold"))
   336→    print_review_quality(merged.get("review_quality", {}), colorize_fn=colorize_fn)
   337→    return merged_path
   338→
   339→
   340→def _import_and_finalize(
   341→    *,
   342→    do_import_fn,
   343→    run_followup_scan_fn,
   344→    merged_path: Path,
   345→    state,
   346→    lang,
   347→    state_file,
   348→    config: dict,
   349→    allow_partial: bool,
   350→    successful_indexes: list[int],
   351→    failure_set: set[int],
   352→    append_run_log,
   353→    args,
   354→) -> None:
   355→    """Import merged results into state and optionally run a followup scan."""
   356→    try:
   357→        do_import_fn(
   358→            str(merged_path),
   359→            state,
   360→            lang,
   361→            state_file,
   362→            config=config,
   363→            allow_partial=allow_partial,
   364→            trusted_assessment_source=True,
   365→            trusted_assessment_label="trusted internal run-batches import",
   366→        )
   367→    except SystemExit as exc:
   368→        append_run_log(f"run-finished import-failed code={exc.code}")
   369→        raise
   370→    except Exception as exc:
   371→        append_run_log(f"run-finished import-error error={exc}")
   372→        raise
   373→    append_run_log(
   374→        "run-finished "
   375→        f"successful={[idx + 1 for idx in successful_indexes]} "
   376→        f"failed={[idx + 1 for idx in sorted(failure_set)]} imported={str(merged_path)}"
   377→    )
   378→
   379→    if getattr(args, "scan_after_import", False):
   380→        followup_code = run_followup_scan_fn(
   381→            lang_name=lang.name,
   382→            scan_path=str(args.path),
   383→        )
   384→        if followup_code != 0:
   385→            raise CommandError(
   386→                f"Error: follow-up scan failed with exit code {followup_code}.",
   387→                exit_code=followup_code,
   388→            )
   389→
   390→
   391→def do_run_batches(
   392→    args,
   393→    state,
   394→    lang,
   395→    state_file,
   396→    *,
   397→    config: dict[str, Any] | None,
   398→    run_stamp_fn,
   399→    load_or_prepare_packet_fn,
   400→    selected_batch_indexes_fn,
   401→    prepare_run_artifacts_fn,
   402→    run_codex_batch_fn,
   403→    execute_batches_fn,
   404→    collect_batch_results_fn,
   405→    print_failures_fn,
   406→    print_failures_and_raise_fn,
   407→    merge_batch_results_fn,
   408→    build_import_provenance_fn,
   409→    do_import_fn,
   410→    run_followup_scan_fn,
   411→    safe_write_text_fn,
   412→    colorize_fn,
   413→    project_root: Path,
   414→    subagent_runs_dir: Path,
   415→) -> None:
   416→    """Run holistic investigation batches with a local subagent runner."""
   417→    config = config or {}
   418→    runner = getattr(args, "runner", "codex")
   419→    validate_runner(runner, colorize_fn=colorize_fn)
   420→    allow_partial = bool(getattr(args, "allow_partial", False))
   421→    policy = resolve_batch_run_policy(args)
   422→    run_parallel = policy.run_parallel
   423→    max_parallel_batches = policy.max_parallel_batches
   424→    heartbeat_seconds = policy.heartbeat_seconds
   425→    batch_timeout_seconds = policy.batch_timeout_seconds
   426→    batch_max_retries = policy.batch_max_retries
   427→    batch_retry_backoff_seconds = policy.batch_retry_backoff_seconds
   428→    stall_warning_seconds = policy.stall_warning_seconds
   429→    stall_kill_seconds = policy.stall_kill_seconds
   430→
   431→    stamp = run_stamp_fn()
   432→    packet, immutable_packet_path, prompt_packet_path = load_or_prepare_packet_fn(
   433→        args,
   434→        state=state,
   435→        lang=lang,
   436→        config=config,
   437→        stamp=stamp,
   438→    )
   439→
   440→    scan_path = str(getattr(args, "path", ".") or ".")
   441→    packet_dimensions = normalize_dimension_list(packet.get("dimensions", []))
   442→    scored_dimensions = scored_dimensions_for_lang(lang.name)
   443→    print_preflight_dimension_scope_notice(
   444→        selected_dims=packet_dimensions,
   445→        scored_dims=scored_dimensions,
   446→        explicit_selection=bool(getattr(args, "dimensions", None)),
   447→        scan_path=scan_path,
   448→        colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
   449→    )
   450→    suggested_prepare_cmd = f"desloppify review --prepare --path {scan_path}"
   451→    raw_dim_prompts = packet.get("dimension_prompts")
   452→    batches = explode_to_single_dimension(
   453→        require_batches(
   454→            packet,
   455→            colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
   456→            suggested_prepare_cmd=suggested_prepare_cmd,
   457→        ),
   458→        dimension_prompts=raw_dim_prompts if isinstance(raw_dim_prompts, dict) else None,
   459→    )
   460→    selected_indexes = selected_batch_indexes_fn(args, batch_count=len(batches))
   461→    total_batches = len(selected_indexes)
   462→    effective_workers = min(total_batches, max_parallel_batches) if run_parallel else 1
   463→    waves = max(1, math.ceil(total_batches / max(1, effective_workers)))
   464→    worst_case_seconds = waves * batch_timeout_seconds
   465→    worst_case_minutes = max(1, math.ceil(worst_case_seconds / 60))
   466→    print(
   467→        colorize_fn(
   468→            "  Runtime expectation: "
   469→            f"{total_batches} batch(es), workers={effective_workers}, "
   470→            f"timeout-per-batch={int(batch_timeout_seconds / 60)}m, "
   471→            f"worst-case upper bound ~{worst_case_minutes}m.",
   472→            "dim",
   473→        )
   474→    )
   475→    run_dir, logs_dir, prompt_files, output_files, log_files = prepare_run_artifacts_fn(
   476→        stamp=stamp,
   477→        selected_indexes=selected_indexes,
   478→        batches=batches,
   479→        packet_path=prompt_packet_path,
   480→        run_root=subagent_runs_dir,
   481→        repo_root=project_root,
   482→    )
   483→    run_log_path = resolve_run_log_path(
   484→        getattr(args, "run_log_file", None),
   485→        project_root=project_root,
   486→        run_dir=run_dir,
   487→    )
   488→    append_run_log = make_run_log_writer(run_log_path)
   489→
   490→    append_run_log(
   491→        "run-start "
   492→        f"runner={runner} parallel={run_parallel} max_parallel={max_parallel_batches} "
   493→        f"timeout={batch_timeout_seconds}s heartbeat={heartbeat_seconds:.1f}s "
   494→        f"stall_warning={stall_warning_seconds}s stall_kill={stall_kill_seconds}s "
   495→        f"retries={batch_max_retries} "
   496→        f"retry_backoff={batch_retry_backoff_seconds:.1f}s upper_bound={worst_case_minutes}m "
   497→        f"selected={[idx + 1 for idx in selected_indexes]}"
   498→    )
   499→    append_run_log(f"run-path {run_dir}")
   500→    append_run_log(f"packet {immutable_packet_path}")
   501→    append_run_log(f"blind-packet {prompt_packet_path}")
   502→    print(colorize_fn(f"  Live run log: {run_log_path}", "dim"))
   503→
   504→    if getattr(args, "dry_run", False):
   505→        # Write a stub run_summary.json so --import-run works after manual
   506→        # subagent execution (e.g. Claude subagents filling in results/).
   507→        dry_summary: dict[str, object] = {
   508→            "created_at": datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(timespec="seconds"),
   509→            "run_stamp": stamp,
   510→            "runner": "dry-run",
   511→            "parallel": False,
   512→            "selected_batches": [idx + 1 for idx in selected_indexes],
   513→            "successful_batches": [idx + 1 for idx in selected_indexes],
   514→            "failed_batches": [],
   515→            "immutable_packet": str(immutable_packet_path),
   516→            "blind_packet": str(prompt_packet_path),
   517→            "run_dir": str(run_dir),
   518→            "logs_dir": str(logs_dir),
   519→            "batches": {
   520→                str(idx + 1): {
   521→                    "status": "pending",
   522→                    "prompt_path": str(prompt_files[idx]),
   523→                    "result_path": str(output_files[idx]),
   524→                }
   525→                for idx in selected_indexes
   526→            },
   527→        }
   528→        dry_summary_path = run_dir / "run_summary.json"
   529→        safe_write_text_fn(dry_summary_path, json.dumps(dry_summary, indent=2) + "\n")
   530→
   531→        n = len(selected_indexes)
   532→        print(
   533→            colorize_fn(
   534→                f"  Dry run: {n} prompt(s) generated, runner execution skipped.", "yellow"
   535→            )
   536→        )
   537→        print(colorize_fn(f"  Run directory: {run_dir}", "dim"))
   538→        print(colorize_fn(f"  Immutable packet: {immutable_packet_path}", "dim"))
   539→        print(colorize_fn(f"  Blind packet: {prompt_packet_path}", "dim"))
   540→        print(colorize_fn(f"  Prompts: {run_dir / 'prompts'}", "dim"))
   541→        print(colorize_fn(f"  Results: {run_dir / 'results'}  (write subagent output here)", "dim"))
   542→        print()
   543→        print(
   544→            colorize_fn(
   545→                f"  Next: launch {n} subagent(s), one per prompt file. "
   546→                "Each writes JSON output to the matching results/ file.",
   547→                "bold",
   548→            )
   549→        )
   550→        print(
   551→            colorize_fn(
   552→                f"  Then: desloppify review --import-run {run_dir} --scan-after-import",
   553→                "bold",
   554→            )
   555→        )
   556→        append_run_log("run-finished dry-run")
   557→        return
   558→    tasks = build_batch_tasks(
   559→        selected_indexes=selected_indexes,
   560→        prompt_files=prompt_files,
   561→        output_files=output_files,
   562→        log_files=log_files,
   563→        project_root=project_root,
   564→        run_codex_batch_fn=run_codex_batch_fn,
   565→    )
   566→
   567→    batch_positions = {batch_idx: pos + 1 for pos, batch_idx in enumerate(selected_indexes)}
   568→    summary_created_at = datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(timespec="seconds")
   569→    stall_warned_batches: set[int] = set()
   570→    batch_status: dict[str, dict[str, object]] = {
   571→        str(idx + 1): {
   572→            "position": batch_positions.get(idx, 0),
   573→            "status": "pending",
   574→            "prompt_path": str(prompt_files[idx]),
   575→            "result_path": str(output_files[idx]),
   576→            "log_path": str(log_files[idx]),
   577→        }
   578→        for idx in selected_indexes
   579→    }
   580→
   581→    if run_parallel:
   582→        print(
   583→            colorize_fn(
   584→                "  Parallel runner config: "
   585→                f"max-workers={min(total_batches, max_parallel_batches)}, "
   586→                f"heartbeat={heartbeat_seconds:.1f}s",
   587→                "dim",
   588→            )
   589→        )
   590→
   591→    _report_progress = _build_progress_reporter(
   592→        batch_positions=batch_positions,
   593→        batch_status=batch_status,
   594→        stall_warned_batches=stall_warned_batches,
   595→        total_batches=total_batches,
   596→        stall_warning_seconds=stall_warning_seconds,
   597→        prompt_files=prompt_files,
   598→        output_files=output_files,
   599→        log_files=log_files,
   600→        append_run_log=append_run_log,
   601→        colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
   602→    )
   603→
   604→    record_execution_issue = partial(_record_execution_issue, append_run_log)
   605→    run_summary_path = run_dir / "run_summary.json"
   606→    summary_config = BatchRunSummaryConfig(
   607→        created_at=summary_created_at,
   608→        run_stamp=stamp,
   609→        runner=runner,
   610→        run_parallel=run_parallel,
   611→        selected_indexes=selected_indexes,
   612→        allow_partial=allow_partial,
   613→        max_parallel_batches=max_parallel_batches,
   614→        batch_timeout_seconds=batch_timeout_seconds,
   615→        batch_max_retries=batch_max_retries,
   616→        batch_retry_backoff_seconds=batch_retry_backoff_seconds,
   617→        heartbeat_seconds=heartbeat_seconds,
   618→        stall_warning_seconds=stall_warning_seconds,
   619→        stall_kill_seconds=stall_kill_seconds,
   620→        immutable_packet_path=immutable_packet_path,
   621→        prompt_packet_path=prompt_packet_path,
   622→        run_dir=run_dir,
   623→        logs_dir=logs_dir,
   624→        run_log_path=run_log_path,
   625→    )
   626→
   627→    def write_run_summary(*, successful_batches, failed_batches, interrupted=False, interruption_reason=None):
   628→        _write_run_summary_impl(
   629→            summary_path=run_summary_path,
   630→            summary_config=summary_config,
   631→            batch_status=batch_status,
   632→            successful_batches=successful_batches,
   633→            failed_batches=failed_batches,
   634→            safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text_fn,
   635→            colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
   636→            append_run_log_fn=append_run_log,
   637→            interrupted=interrupted,
   638→            interruption_reason=interruption_reason,
   639→        )
   640→
   641→    try:
   642→        execution_failures = execute_batches_fn(
   643→            tasks=tasks,
   644→            options=BatchExecutionOptions(
   645→                run_parallel=run_parallel,
   646→                max_parallel_workers=max_parallel_batches,
   647→                heartbeat_seconds=heartbeat_seconds,
   648→            ),
   649→            progress_fn=_report_progress,
   650→            error_log_fn=record_execution_issue,
   651→        )
   652→    except KeyboardInterrupt:
   653→        for idx in selected_indexes:
   654→            key = str(idx + 1)
   655→            state = batch_status.setdefault(
   656→                key,
   657→                {"position": batch_positions.get(idx, 0), "status": "pending"},
   658→            )
   659→            if state.get("status") in {"pending", "queued", "running"}:
   660→                state["status"] = "interrupted"
   661→        write_run_summary(
   662→            successful_batches=[],
   663→            failed_batches=[],
   664→            interrupted=True,
   665→            interruption_reason="keyboard_interrupt",
   666→        )
   667→        append_run_log("run-interrupted reason=keyboard_interrupt")
   668→        raise SystemExit(130) from None
   669→
   670→    batch_results, successful_indexes, failures, failure_set = _collect_and_reconcile_results(
   671→        collect_batch_results_fn=collect_batch_results_fn,
   672→        selected_indexes=selected_indexes,
   673→        execution_failures=execution_failures,
   674→        output_files=output_files,
   675→        packet=packet,
   676→        batch_positions=batch_positions,
   677→        batch_status=batch_status,
   678→    )
   679→
   680→    write_run_summary(
   681→        successful_batches=[idx + 1 for idx in successful_indexes],
   682→        failed_batches=[idx + 1 for idx in sorted(failure_set)],
   683→    )
   684→
   685→    if failures and (not allow_partial or not batch_results):
   686→        append_run_log(
   687→            f"run-finished failures={[idx + 1 for idx in sorted(failure_set)]} mode=exit"
   688→        )
   689→        print_failures_and_raise_fn(
   690→            failures=failures,
   691→            packet_path=immutable_packet_path,
   692→            logs_dir=logs_dir,
   693→            colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
   694→        )
   695→    elif failures:
   696→        print(
   697→            colorize_fn(
   698→                "  Partial completion enabled: importing successful batches and keeping failed batches open.",
   699→                "yellow",
   700→            )
   701→        )
   702→        print_failures_fn(
   703→            failures=failures,
   704→            packet_path=immutable_packet_path,
   705→            logs_dir=logs_dir,
   706→            colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
   707→        )
   708→        append_run_log(
   709→            "run-partial "
   710→            f"successful={[idx + 1 for idx in successful_indexes]} "
   711→            f"failed={[idx + 1 for idx in sorted(failure_set)]}"
   712→        )
   713→
   714→    merged_path = _merge_and_write_results(
   715→        merge_batch_results_fn=merge_batch_results_fn,
   716→        build_import_provenance_fn=build_import_provenance_fn,
   717→        batch_results=batch_results,
   718→        batches=batches,
   719→        successful_indexes=successful_indexes,
   720→        packet=packet,
   721→        packet_dimensions=packet_dimensions,
   722→        scored_dimensions=scored_dimensions,
   723→        scan_path=scan_path,
   724→        runner=runner,
   725→        prompt_packet_path=prompt_packet_path,
   726→        stamp=stamp,
   727→        run_dir=run_dir,
   728→        safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text_fn,
   729→        colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
   730→    )
   731→
   732→    _import_and_finalize(
   733→        do_import_fn=do_import_fn,
   734→        run_followup_scan_fn=run_followup_scan_fn,
   735→        merged_path=merged_path,
   736→        state=state,
   737→        lang=lang,
   738→        state_file=state_file,
   739→        config=config,
   740→        allow_partial=allow_partial,
   741→        successful_indexes=successful_indexes,
   742→        failure_set=failure_set,
   743→        append_run_log=append_run_log,
   744→        args=args,
   745→    )
   746→
   747→
   748→__all__ = ["do_run_batches"]
   749→

    54→        '      "impact_scope": "local|module|subsystem|codebase",\n'
    55→        '      "fix_scope": "single_edit|multi_file_refactor|architectural_change",\n'
    56→        '      "confidence": "high|medium|low",\n'
    57→        f'      "{DIMENSION_NOTE_ISSUES_KEY}": "required when score >{HIGH_SCORE_ISSUES_NOTE_THRESHOLD:.1f}",\n'
    58→        '      "sub_axes": {"abstraction_leverage": 0-100, "indirection_cost": 0-100, "interface_honesty": 0-100, "delegation_density": 0-100, "definition_directness": 0-100, "type_discipline": 0-100}  // required for abstraction_fitness when evidence supports it; all one decimal place\n'
    59→        "    }\n"
    60→        "  },\n"
    61→        '  "issues": [{\n'
    62→        '    "dimension": "<dimension>",\n'
    63→        '    "identifier": "short_id",\n'
    64→        '    "summary": "one-line defect summary",\n'
    65→        '    "related_files": ["relative/path.py"],\n'
    66→        '    "evidence": ["specific code observation"],\n'
    67→        '    "suggestion": "concrete fix recommendation",\n'
    68→        '    "confidence": "high|medium|low",\n'
    69→        '    "impact_scope": "local|module|subsystem|codebase",\n'
    70→        '    "fix_scope": "single_edit|multi_file_refactor|architectural_change",\n'
    71→        '    "root_cause_cluster": "optional_cluster_name_when_supported_by_history"\n'
    72→        "  }],\n"
    73→        '  "retrospective": {\n'
    74→        '    "root_causes": ["optional: concise root-cause hypotheses"],\n'
    75→        '    "likely_symptoms": ["optional: identifiers that look symptom-level"],\n'
    76→        '    "possible_false_positives": ["optional: prior concept keys likely mis-scoped"]\n'
    77→        "  }\n"
    78→        "}\n"
    79→    )
    80→
    81→
    82→def _extract_dimension_prompts(batch: dict[str, object]) -> dict[str, dict[str, object]]:
    83→    """Extract dimension prompts embedded by explode_to_single_dimension."""
    84→    prompt = batch.get("_dimension_prompt")
    85→    if not isinstance(prompt, dict):
    86→        return {}
    87→    dims = batch.get("dimensions", [])
    88→    if isinstance(dims, list) and len(dims) == 1:
    89→        return {str(dims[0]): prompt}
    90→    return {}
    91→
    92→
    93→def render_batch_prompt(
    94→    *,
    95→    repo_root: Path,
    96→    packet_path: Path,
    97→    batch_index: int,
    98→    batch: dict[str, object],
    99→) -> str:
   100→    """Render one subagent prompt for a holistic investigation batch."""
   101→    context = build_batch_context(batch, batch_index)
   102→    dim_prompts = _extract_dimension_prompts(batch)
   103→    return join_non_empty_sections(
   104→        _render_metadata_block(
   105→            repo_root=repo_root,
   106→            packet_path=packet_path,
   107→            batch_index=batch_index,
   108→            context=context,
   109→        ),
   110→        render_dimension_prompts_block(context.dimensions, dim_prompts),
   111→        render_scoring_frame(),
   112→        render_scan_evidence_note(),
   113→        render_seed_files_block(context),
   114→        render_historical_focus(batch),
   115→        render_mechanical_concern_signals(batch),
   116→        render_task_requirements(issues_cap=context.issues_cap, dim_set=context.dimension_set),
   117→        render_scope_enums(),
   118→        _render_output_schema(context, batch_index),
   119→    )
   120→
   121→
   122→__all__ = ["render_batch_prompt"]
   123→

    24→def _validate_attestation(
    25→    attestation: str,
    26→    stage: str,
    27→    *,
    28→    dimensions: list[str] | None = None,
    29→    cluster_names: list[str] | None = None,
    30→) -> str | None:
    31→    """Return error message if attestation doesn't reference required data, else None."""
    32→    text = attestation.lower()
    33→
    34→    if stage == "observe":
    35→        if dimensions:
    36→            found = [d for d in dimensions if d.lower().replace("_", " ") in text or d.lower() in text]
    37→            if not found:
    38→                dim_list = ", ".join(dimensions[:6])
    39→                return f"Attestation must reference at least one dimension from the summary. Mention one of: {dim_list}"
    40→
    41→    elif stage == "reflect":
    42→        refs: list[str] = []
    43→        if dimensions:
    44→            refs.extend(d for d in dimensions if d.lower().replace("_", " ") in text or d.lower() in text)
    45→        if cluster_names:
    46→            refs.extend(n for n in cluster_names if n.lower() in text)
    47→        if not refs and (dimensions or cluster_names):
    48→            return (
    49→                f"Attestation must reference at least one dimension or cluster name.\n"
    50→                f"  Valid dimensions: {', '.join((dimensions or [])[:6])}\n"
    51→                f"  Valid clusters: {', '.join((cluster_names or [])[:6]) if cluster_names else '(none yet)'}"
    52→            )
    53→
    54→    elif stage == "organize":
    55→        if cluster_names:
    56→            found = [n for n in cluster_names if n.lower() in text]
    57→            if not found:
    58→                names = ", ".join(cluster_names[:6])
    59→                return f"Attestation must reference at least one cluster from the plan. Mention one of: {names}"
    60→
    61→    elif stage == "enrich":
    62→        if cluster_names:
    63→            found = [n for n in cluster_names if n.lower() in text]
    64→            if not found:
    65→                names = ", ".join(cluster_names[:6])
    66→                return f"Attestation must reference at least one cluster you enriched. Mention one of: {names}"
    67→
    68→    return None
    69→

   100→    print(f"  Your analysis covered {issue_count} issues across {len(by_dim)} dimensions:")

   107→

   109→        if attestation:
   110→            print(colorize(

   112→                "red",
   113→            ))
   114→        print(colorize("\n  If satisfied, confirm:", "dim"))
   115→        print(colorize('    desloppify plan triage --confirm observe --attestation "I have thoroughly reviewed..."', "dim"))

   118→
   119→    # Validate attestation references actual data
   120→    validation_err = _validate_attestation(attestation.strip(), "observe", dimensions=dim_names)
   121→    if validation_err:
   122→        print(colorize(f"\n  {validation_err}", "red"))

   125→    # Record confirmation

   134→        detail={"attestation": attestation.strip()},
   135→    )
   136→    resolved_services.save_plan(plan)

   138→    print_user_message(

   427→def _cmd_confirm_stage(

   431→) -> None:
   432→    """Router for ``--confirm observe/reflect/organize/enrich``."""
   433→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
   434→    confirm_stage = getattr(args, "confirm", None)
   435→    attestation = getattr(args, "attestation", None)
   436→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   437→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   438→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   439→
   440→    if confirm_stage == "observe":
   441→        _confirm_observe(args, plan, stages, attestation, services=resolved_services)
   442→    elif confirm_stage == "reflect":
   443→        _confirm_reflect(args, plan, stages, attestation, services=resolved_services)
   444→    elif confirm_stage == "organize":
   445→        _confirm_organize(args, plan, stages, attestation, services=resolved_services)
   446→    elif confirm_stage == "enrich":
   447→        _confirm_enrich(args, plan, stages, attestation, services=resolved_services)
   448→
   449→
   450→MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN = _MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN
   451→validate_attestation = _validate_attestation
   452→
   453→
   454→def cmd_confirm_stage(
   455→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   456→    *,
   457→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   458→) -> None:
   459→    """Public triage confirmation entrypoint."""
   460→    _cmd_confirm_stage(args, services=services)
   461→
   462→__all__ = [
   463→    "MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN",
   464→    "cmd_confirm_stage",
   465→    "validate_attestation",
   466→    "_MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN",
   467→    "_cmd_confirm_stage",

   469→    "_confirm_observe",
   470→    "_confirm_organize",
   471→    "_confirm_reflect",
   472→    "_validate_attestation",
   473→]
   474→

1→"""Shared runtime path resolution for review command flows.
     2→
     3→All override hooks live here so tests patch a single module instead of
     4→per-command boilerplate.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→from __future__ import annotations
     8→
     9→from pathlib import Path
    10→
    11→from desloppify.base.discovery.paths import get_project_root
    12→
    13→# ── Test override hooks (monkeypatch these in tests) ──────────────
    14→PROJECT_ROOT: Path | None = None
    15→REVIEW_PACKET_DIR: Path | None = None
    16→SUBAGENT_RUNS_DIR: Path | None = None
    17→EXTERNAL_SESSION_ROOT: Path | None = None
    18→
    19→
    20→def runtime_project_root(*, project_root_override: Path | None = None) -> Path:
    21→    """Resolve project root with optional test override hook."""
    22→    override = project_root_override if project_root_override is not None else PROJECT_ROOT
    23→    if isinstance(override, Path):
    24→        return override
    25→    return get_project_root()
    26→
    27→
    28→def review_packet_dir(
    29→    *,
    30→    project_root_override: Path | None = None,
    31→    review_packet_dir_override: Path | None = None,
    32→) -> Path:
    33→    """Resolve `.desloppify/review_packets` with optional override."""
    34→    override = review_packet_dir_override if review_packet_dir_override is not None else REVIEW_PACKET_DIR
    35→    if isinstance(override, Path):
    36→        return override
    37→    return runtime_project_root(project_root_override=project_root_override) / ".desloppify" / "review_packets"
    38→
    39→
    40→def blind_packet_path(
    41→    *,
    42→    project_root_override: Path | None = None,
    43→    stamp: str | None = None,
    44→) -> Path:
    45→    """Resolve blind packet path under `.desloppify`.
    46→
    47→    When ``stamp`` is provided, return a run-scoped blind packet path to avoid
    48→    cross-run write races.
    49→    """
    50→    if isinstance(stamp, str) and stamp.strip():
    51→        packet_dir = review_packet_dir(project_root_override=project_root_override)
    52→        return packet_dir / f"review_packet_blind_{stamp.strip()}.json"
    53→    return runtime_project_root(project_root_override=project_root_override) / ".desloppify" / "review_packet_blind.json"
    54→
    55→
    56→def subagent_runs_dir(
    57→    *,
    58→    project_root_override: Path | None = None,
    59→    subagent_runs_dir_override: Path | None = None,
    60→) -> Path:
    61→    """Resolve subagent run artifact directory with optional override."""
    62→    override = subagent_runs_dir_override if subagent_runs_dir_override is not None else SUBAGENT_RUNS_DIR
    63→    if isinstance(override, Path):
    64→        return override
    65→    return runtime_project_root(project_root_override=project_root_override) / ".desloppify" / "subagents" / "runs"
    66→
    67→
    68→def external_session_root(
    69→    *,
    70→    project_root_override: Path | None = None,
    71→    external_session_root_override: Path | None = None,
    72→) -> Path:
    73→    """Resolve external review session root with optional override."""
    74→    override = external_session_root_override if external_session_root_override is not None else EXTERNAL_SESSION_ROOT
    75→    if isinstance(override, Path):
    76→        return override
    77→    return runtime_project_root(project_root_override=project_root_override) / ".desloppify" / "external_review_sessions"
    78→
    79→
    80→__all__ = [
    81→    "blind_packet_path",
    82→    "external_session_root",
    83→    "review_packet_dir",
    84→    "runtime_project_root",
    85→    "subagent_runs_dir",
    86→]
    87→

    37→def print_stage_progress(stages: dict, plan: dict | None = None) -> None:
    38→    """Print the 4-stage progress indicator."""

    44→            else:
    45→                print(colorize(f"    \u2713 {label} (needs confirm)", "yellow"))
    46→        elif TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES[stage_name].issubset(stages):
    47→            print(colorize(f"    \u2192 {label} (current)", "yellow"))
    48→        else:
    49→            print(colorize(f"    \u25cb {label}", "dim"))
    50→
    51→    # Show enrichment gaps when in the organize stage
    52→    if plan and "reflect" in stages and "organize" not in stages:
    53→        gaps = _unenriched_clusters(plan)
    54→        manual = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
    55→        if not manual:
    56→            print(colorize("\n    No manual clusters yet. Create clusters and enrich them.", "yellow"))
    57→        elif gaps:
    58→            print(colorize(f"\n    {len(gaps)} cluster(s) need enrichment:", "yellow"))
    59→            for name, missing in gaps:

    61→            print(colorize(

    65→        else:

    68→def print_progress(plan: dict, open_issues: dict) -> None:

    75→    }
    76→    if active_clusters:

    78→        for name, cluster in active_clusters.items():

    80→            desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
    81→            steps = cluster.get("action_steps", [])

   204→def print_reflect_dashboard(

   235→            summary = f.get("summary", "")

   244→    if recurring:

   246→        for dim, info in sorted(recurring.items()):

   260→
   261→def _print_dashboard_header(si: object, stages: dict, meta: dict, plan: dict) -> None:
   262→    """Print the header section: title, open issues count, stage progress, overall status."""
   263→    print(colorize("  Epic triage \u2014 manual", "bold"))
   264→    print(colorize("  " + "\u2500" * 60, "dim"))
   265→    print(f"  Open review issues: {len(si.open_issues)}")
   266→    print(colorize("  Goal: identify contradictions, resolve them, then group the coherent", "cyan"))
   267→    print(colorize("  remainder into clusters by root cause with action steps and priorities.", "cyan"))
   268→    if si.existing_epics:
   269→        print(f"  Existing epics: {len(si.existing_epics)}")
   270→    if si.new_since_last:
   271→        print(colorize(f"  New since last triage: {len(si.new_since_last)}", "yellow"))
   272→        for fid in sorted(si.new_since_last):
   273→            f = si.open_issues.get(fid, {})
   274→            dim = ""
   275→            detail = f.get("detail")
   276→            if isinstance(detail, dict):
   277→                dim = detail.get("dimension", "")
   278→            dim_tag = f" ({dim})" if dim else ""
   279→            print(colorize(f"    * [{short_issue_id(fid)}] {f.get('summary', '')}{dim_tag}", "yellow"))
   280→    if si.resolved_since_last:
   281→        print(f"  Resolved since last triage: {len(si.resolved_since_last)}")
   282→
   283→    # Stage progress (with enrichment gaps)
   284→    print()
   285→    print_stage_progress(stages, plan)
   286→    if meta.get("stage_refresh_required"):
   287→        print(
   288→            colorize(
   289→                "  Note: review issues changed since stage progress started; "
   290→                "refresh stage reports before completion.",
   291→                "yellow",
   292→            )
   293→        )
   294→
   295→
   296→def _print_action_guidance(stages: dict, meta: dict, si: object, plan: dict) -> None:
   297→    """Print the 'What to do' action guidance section based on current stage."""
   298→    print()
   299→    has_only_additions = bool(si.new_since_last) and not si.resolved_since_last
   300→    if "observe" not in stages and has_only_additions and meta.get("strategy_summary"):
   301→        # Show both paths: accept or re-plan
   302→        print(colorize("  Two paths available:", "yellow"))
   303→        print()
   304→        print(colorize("  To accept current queue (new items at end):", "cyan"))
   305→        print(
   306→            '    desloppify plan triage --confirm-existing '
   307→            '--note "..." --strategy "same" --confirmed "I have reviewed..."'
   308→        )
   309→        print()
   310→        print(colorize("  To re-prioritize and restructure:", "cyan"))
   311→        print(f"    {TRIAGE_CMD_OBSERVE}")
   312→    elif "observe" not in stages:
   313→        print(colorize("  Next step:", "yellow"))
   314→        print(f"    {TRIAGE_CMD_OBSERVE}")
   315→        print(colorize("    (themes, root causes, contradictions between issues — NOT a list of IDs)", "dim"))
   316→    elif "reflect" not in stages:
   317→        print(colorize("  Next step: use the completed work and patterns below to write your reflect report.", "yellow"))
   318→        print(f"    {TRIAGE_CMD_REFLECT}")
   319→        print(colorize("    (Contradictions, recurring patterns, which direction to take, what to defer)", "dim"))
   320→    elif "organize" not in stages:
   321→        gaps = _unenriched_clusters(plan)
   322→        manual = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
   323→
   324→        if not manual:
   325→            print(colorize("  Next steps:", "yellow"))
   326→            print("    0. Defer contradictory issues: `desloppify plan skip <hash>`")
   327→            print(f"    1. Create clusters:  {TRIAGE_CMD_CLUSTER_CREATE}")
   328→            print(f"    2. Add issues:     {TRIAGE_CMD_CLUSTER_ADD}")
   329→            print(f"    3. Enrich clusters:  {TRIAGE_CMD_CLUSTER_STEPS}")
   330→            print(f"    4. Record stage:     {TRIAGE_CMD_ORGANIZE}")
   331→        elif gaps:
   332→            print(colorize("  Enrich these clusters before recording organize:", "yellow"))
   333→            for name, missing in gaps:
   334→                print(colorize(f"    {name}: missing {', '.join(missing)}", "yellow"))
   335→            print(colorize(
   336→                f"    Fix: {TRIAGE_CMD_CLUSTER_ENRICH_COMPACT}",
   337→                "dim",
   338→            ))
   339→            print(colorize(f"    Then: {TRIAGE_CMD_ORGANIZE}", "dim"))
   340→        else:
   341→            print(colorize("  All clusters enriched! Record the organize stage:", "green"))
   342→            print(f"    {TRIAGE_CMD_ORGANIZE}")
   343→
   344→        if meta.get("strategy_summary"):
   345→            print()
   346→            print(colorize("  Or fast-track (if existing plan is still valid):", "dim"))
   347→            print(f"    {TRIAGE_CMD_CONFIRM_EXISTING}")
   348→    elif "enrich" not in stages:
   349→        shallow = _unenriched_clusters(plan)
   350→        if shallow:
   351→            print(colorize("  Next step: enrich steps with detail and issue_refs.", "yellow"))
   352→            print(colorize('    desloppify plan cluster update <name> --update-step N --detail "sub-details"', "dim"))
   353→        else:
   354→            print(colorize("  Steps look enriched. Record the enrich stage:", "green"))
   355→        print(f"    {TRIAGE_CMD_ENRICH}")
   356→        print(colorize("  You can still reorganize: add/remove clusters, reorder items.", "dim"))
   357→    else:
   358→        print(colorize("  Ready to complete:", "green"))
   359→        print(f"    {TRIAGE_CMD_COMPLETE_VERBOSE}")
   360→        print(colorize('    (use --strategy "same" to keep existing strategy)', "dim"))
   361→
   362→
   363→def _print_prior_stage_reports(stages: dict) -> None:
   364→    """Print prior stage reports (observe/reflect) as context for current action."""
   365→    if "observe" in stages:
   366→        obs_report = stages["observe"].get("report", "")
   367→        if obs_report:
   368→            print(colorize("\n  Your observe analysis:", "dim"))
   369→            for line in obs_report.strip().splitlines()[:8]:
   370→                print(colorize(f"    {line}", "dim"))
   371→            if len(obs_report.strip().splitlines()) > 8:
   372→                print(colorize("    ...", "dim"))
   373→    if "reflect" in stages:
   374→        ref_report = stages["reflect"].get("report", "")
   375→        if ref_report:
   376→            print(colorize("\n  Your reflect strategy:", "dim"))
   377→            for line in ref_report.strip().splitlines()[:8]:
   378→                print(colorize(f"    {line}", "dim"))
   379→            if len(ref_report.strip().splitlines()) > 8:
   380→                print(colorize("    ...", "dim"))
   381→
   382→
   383→def _print_issues_by_dimension(open_issues: dict) -> None:
   384→    """Print issues grouped by dimension with suggestions to surface contradictions."""
   385→    by_dim: dict[str, list[tuple[str, dict]]] = defaultdict(list)
   386→    for fid, f in open_issues.items():
   387→        detail = f.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(f.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   388→        dim = detail.get("dimension", "unknown")
   389→        by_dim[dim].append((fid, f))
   390→
   391→    print(colorize("\n  Review issues by dimension:", "cyan"))
   392→    print(colorize("  (Look for contradictions: issues in the same dimension that", "dim"))
   393→    print(colorize("  recommend opposite changes. These must be resolved before clustering.)", "dim"))
   394→    max_per_dim = 5
   395→    for dim in sorted(by_dim, key=lambda d: (-len(by_dim[d]), d)):
   396→        items = by_dim[dim]
   397→        print(colorize(f"\n    {dim} ({len(items)}):", "bold"))
   398→        for fid, f in items[:max_per_dim]:
   399→            summary = f.get("summary", "")
   400→            short = short_issue_id(fid)
   401→            detail = f.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(f.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   402→            suggestion = (detail.get("suggestion") or "")[:120]
   403→            print(f"      [{short}] {summary}")
   404→            if suggestion:
   405→                print(colorize(f"        \u2192 {suggestion}", "dim"))
   406→        if len(items) > max_per_dim:
   407→            print(colorize(f"      ... and {len(items) - max_per_dim} more", "dim"))
   408→    print(colorize("\n  Use hash in commands: desloppify plan skip <hash>  |  desloppify show <hash>", "dim"))
   409→
   410→
   411→def cmd_triage_dashboard(
   412→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   413→    *,
   414→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   415→) -> None:
   416→    """Default view: show issues, stage progress, and next command."""
   417→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
   418→    runtime = resolved_services.command_runtime(args)
   419→    state = runtime.state
   420→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   421→    si = resolved_services.collect_triage_input(plan, state)
   422→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   423→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   424→
   425→    _print_dashboard_header(si, stages, meta, plan)
   426→    _print_action_guidance(stages, meta, si, plan)
   427→    _print_prior_stage_reports(stages)
   428→    _print_issues_by_dimension(si.open_issues)
   429→
   430→    # Show reflect dashboard when observe done, reflect not done
   431→    if "observe" in stages and "reflect" not in stages:
   432→        print_reflect_dashboard(si, plan, services=resolved_services)
   433→
   434→    # Show current cluster progress
   435→    print_progress(plan, si.open_issues)
   436→
   437→def show_plan_summary(plan: dict, state: dict) -> None:
   438→    """Print a compact plan rendering: clusters + queue order + coverage."""
   439→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   440→    active = {
   441→        n: c for n, c in clusters.items()
   442→        if c.get("issue_ids") and not c.get("auto")
   443→    }
   444→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
   445→
   446→    if active:
   447→        print(colorize(f"\n  Clusters ({len(active)}):", "bold"))
   448→        for name, cluster in active.items():
   449→            count = len(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   450→            steps = len(cluster.get("action_steps", []))
   451→            desc = (cluster.get("description") or "")[:60]
   452→            print(f"    {name}: {count} items, {steps} steps — {desc}")
   453→
   454→    queue_order = [
   455→        fid for fid in plan.get("queue_order", [])
   456→        if not fid.startswith("triage::") and not fid.startswith("workflow::")
   457→    ]
   458→    if queue_order:
   459→        show = min(15, len(queue_order))
   460→        print(colorize(f"\n  Queue order (first {show} of {len(queue_order)}):", "bold"))
   461→        for i, fid in enumerate(queue_order[:show]):
   462→            f = issues.get(fid, {})
   463→            summary = (f.get("summary") or fid)[:60]
   464→            detector = f.get("detector", "?")
   465→            cn = find_cluster_for(fid, active)
   466→            print(f"    {i+1}. [{detector}] {summary}{f' ({cn})' if cn else ''}")
   467→
   468→    organized, total, _ = triage_coverage(
   469→        plan, open_review_ids=open_review_ids_from_state(state),
   470→    )
   471→    pct = int(organized / total * 100) if total else 0
   472→    print(colorize(f"\n  Coverage: {organized}/{total} in clusters ({pct}%)", "bold"))
   473→
   474→__all__ = [
   475→    "cmd_triage_dashboard",
   476→    "print_organize_result",
   477→    "print_progress",
   478→    "print_reflect_dashboard",
   479→    "print_reflect_result",
   480→    "print_stage_progress",
   481→    "show_plan_summary",
   482→]

I now have a comprehensive understanding of the architecture. Let me produce the detailed implementation plan.

---

## Implementation Plan: Subagent-Delegated Triage Execution

### Architecture Overview

The plan introduces a `triage/runner/` module under `app/commands/plan/triage/` that mirrors the structure of `app/commands/review/` runner components. Triage stages are fundamentally different from review batches: review subagents return JSON that gets merged; triage subagents **execute CLI commands** that mutate plan state directly.

### Key Design Decisions

**1. CLI Surface**

New flags on `desloppify plan triage`:

```
--run-stages           # Codex subprocess mode: run all stages sequentially via subagents
--runner codex         # Runner backend (only codex initially)
--dry-run              # Generate prompts but don't execute
--stage-timeout        # Per-stage timeout (default: 30 min)
```

No `--parallel` flag for across-stage parallelism (stages are sequential). Within-stage parallelism is not used initially because:
- Observe/reflect are analysis-only (single subagent each)
- Organize/enrich mutate shared plan state (concurrent CLI commands would race on plan.json)

**2. Stage Packets (Subagent Prompts)**

Each stage's subagent prompt contains:
- **TriageInput data** (all open issues, resolved issues, existing clusters, dimension scores, deltas)
- **Prior stage outputs** (observe report feeds into reflect prompt, etc.)
- **CLI command reference** specific to that stage
- **Validation requirements** (what the subagent needs to achieve for the stage to pass)
- **Codebase access instructions** (repo root, how to read source files)

**3. Stage Subagent Counts**

| Stage | Subagents | Rationale |
|-------|-----------|-----------|
| Observe | 1 | Analysis only, needs holistic view of all issues |
| Reflect | 1 | Strategy formation needs full context including observe output |
| Organize | 1 | Mutates shared plan state; multiple subagents would race |
| Enrich | 1 | Mutates shared plan state; could split per-cluster later |
| Commit | 0 | Automated: validate + apply after enrich completes |

**4. Claude External for Triage**

Deferred. Organize/enrich require running CLI commands which Claude external cannot do directly. The review external model works because it returns JSON. For triage, the subagent needs to actually modify plan.json via CLI calls. Two future options:
- Option A: Claude returns a list of CLI commands as structured JSON, and an importer executes them
- Option B: Claude generates a shell script that gets reviewed and executed

For now: Codex subprocess only (same as how review started).

**5. Pipeline Orchestration**

The pipeline runs all 5 stages sequentially in a single `--run-stages` invocation:

```
observe -> auto-confirm observe -> reflect -> auto-confirm reflect
-> organize -> auto-confirm organize -> enrich -> auto-confirm enrich
-> commit (auto)
```

Each stage: (a) builds prompt, (b) spawns Codex subprocess, (c) waits for completion, (d) validates stage was recorded via CLI, (e) auto-confirms with the subagent's report as attestation, (f) proceeds to next stage.

**6. Validation After Each Stage**

After each subagent returns, the orchestrator:
- Reloads plan.json from disk
- Checks the stage was properly recorded (report exists, meets length requirements)
- For organize: checks clusters exist, all issues covered, clusters enriched
- For enrich: checks all steps have detail/issue_refs
- Auto-confirms the stage with a generated attestation based on the actual data

### File Structure

New files under `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/runner/`:

```
triage/runner/
  __init__.py
  orchestrator.py          # Main pipeline: run all stages sequentially
  stage_prompts.py         # Build per-stage subagent prompts
  stage_validation.py      # Post-subagent validation per stage
  codex_runner.py          # Codex subprocess execution (reuses runner_process.py)
```

### Detailed File Specifications

#### `triage/runner/__init__.py`

Exports `do_run_triage_stages`.

#### `triage/runner/orchestrator.py`

Main orchestration function. Signature:

```python
def do_run_triage_stages(
    args: argparse.Namespace,
    *,
    services: TriageServices,
    config: dict | None = None,
) -> None:
```

Flow:
1. Load plan, collect TriageInput
2. Create run directory under `.desloppify/triage_runs/<stamp>/`
3. For each stage in `["observe", "reflect", "organize", "enrich"]`:
   a. Build stage prompt via `stage_prompts.build_stage_prompt(stage, triage_input, prior_reports)`
   b. Write prompt to `<run_dir>/prompts/<stage>.md`
   c. Execute via `codex_runner.run_triage_stage(prompt, repo_root, output_file, log_file, ...)`
   d. Reload plan from disk (subagent has mutated it via CLI commands)
   e. Validate stage was recorded: `stage_validation.validate_stage(stage, plan)`
   f. If validation fails: report error and abort
   g. Auto-confirm stage: build attestation from actual plan data, call confirmation
   h. Collect stage report for next stage's prompt context
4. After all 4 stages pass: auto-complete triage with strategy derived from stages

Key details:
- Uses `run_codex_batch` from `review/runner_process.py` directly (same Codex subprocess pattern)
- `codex_batch_command` already handles `-C <repo_root> -s workspace-write` which gives subagents both file read access and the ability to run desloppify CLI commands
- Each stage prompt explicitly lists available desloppify CLI commands and tells the subagent to run them
- The subagent's output file is not used for JSON parsing -- instead we validate plan.json was modified correctly
- Run log, per-stage logs, and prompt snapshots persist under the run directory

#### `triage/runner/stage_prompts.py`

```python
def build_stage_prompt(
    stage: str,
    triage_input: TriageInput,
    prior_reports: dict[str, str],  # stage_name -> report text
    *,
    repo_root: Path,
) -> str:
```

Each prompt includes:
1. **Role preamble**: "You are a codebase triage subagent. You have access to the full codebase and the desloppify CLI."
2. **Repo root**: path for file reading
3. **Issue data**: formatted from TriageInput (reuses `build_triage_prompt` from engine)
4. **Prior stage context**: reports from earlier stages
5. **Stage-specific instructions**: what to analyze, what CLI commands to run
6. **Validation requirements**: what must be true when you're done

Stage-specific prompt sections:

**OBSERVE prompt:**
- "Read the source files referenced by issues. Identify themes, root causes, contradictions."
- "When done, run: `desloppify plan triage --stage observe --report \"<your analysis>\"`"
- Must produce 100+ char report with actual observations

**REFLECT prompt:**
- Includes observe report as context
- "Compare current issues vs resolved history. Identify recurring patterns."
- "Run: `desloppify plan triage --stage reflect --report \"<your strategy>\"` --attestation \"<80+ chars mentioning dimensions>\""
- Must mention recurring dimension names if any exist

**ORGANIZE prompt:**
- Includes observe + reflect reports
- "Create clusters, add issues, set descriptions and steps. Then skip contradictory issues."
- Lists all CLI commands: `plan cluster create`, `plan cluster add`, `plan cluster update`, `plan skip`
- "Run: `desloppify plan triage --stage organize --report \"<summary>\"` --attestation \"<80+ chars mentioning cluster names>\""
- All review issues must be in a cluster or skipped

**ENRICH prompt:**
- Includes all prior reports
- "Add detail and issue_refs to each cluster step."
- "Run: `desloppify plan cluster update <name> --update-step N --detail \"...\"`"
- "Run: `desloppify plan triage --stage enrich --report \"<summary>\"` --attestation \"<80+ chars mentioning cluster names>\""
- All steps must have detail or issue_refs

#### `triage/runner/stage_validation.py`

```python
def validate_stage(stage: str, plan: dict, state: dict) -> tuple[bool, str]:
    """Returns (success, error_message)."""
```

Reuses the existing validation logic from `_stage_validation.py`:
- observe: check stages dict has "observe" with report >= min chars
- reflect: check "reflect" exists with report >= min chars
- organize: check clusters exist, all enriched, all issues covered (reuses `_manual_clusters_or_error`, `_clusters_enriched_or_error`, `_unclustered_review_issues_or_error`)
- enrich: check `_shallow_steps` returns empty

Also:

```python
def build_auto_attestation(stage: str, plan: dict, triage_input: TriageInput) -> str:
    """Generate a valid attestation from actual plan data for auto-confirmation."""
```

This generates attestation text that references real dimensions/cluster names to pass the `_validate_attestation` check. For example:
- observe: "I have thoroughly analyzed {n} issues across dimensions {dim1}, {dim2}, {dim3} and identified root causes and contradictions in the codebase"
- organize: "This plan correctly organizes issues into clusters {c1}, {c2}, {c3} with enriched steps and handles contradictions via skip/dismiss"

#### `triage/runner/codex_runner.py`

Thin wrapper around the existing `runner_process.py`:

```python
def run_triage_stage(
    *,
    prompt: str,
    repo_root: Path,
    output_file: Path,
    log_file: Path,
    timeout_seconds: int,
) -> int:
    """Execute one triage stage via codex and return exit code."""
```

Uses `codex_batch_command` and `run_codex_batch` from `review/runner_process.py`. The output file isn't parsed for JSON -- it's just the Codex execution output for debugging. The real "output" is the mutations to plan.json via CLI commands.

### Implementation Order

1. **`triage/runner/__init__.py`** - Package setup
2. **`triage/runner/stage_prompts.py`** - Build prompts first (can test independently)
3. **`triage/runner/stage_validation.py`** - Post-stage validation (can test with mock plans)
4. **`triage/runner/codex_runner.py`** - Thin wrapper (minimal code)
5. **`triage/runner/orchestrator.py`** - Main pipeline (depends on 2-4)
6. **`parser_groups_plan_impl.py`** - Add CLI flags
7. **`triage_handlers.py`** - Add routing

### Potential Challenges

1. **Plan file locking**: The subagent and the orchestrator both read plan.json. The orchestrator only reads after the subagent completes, so no concurrent access. But if the subagent crashes mid-write, plan.json could be corrupted. Mitigation: existing `save_plan` uses atomic write patterns.

2. **Subagent prompt quality**: The subagent needs to run multiple CLI commands correctly. The prompts must be explicit about exact command syntax. Include example commands with actual issue IDs from the data.

3. **Attestation generation**: Auto-generated attestations must pass the validation checks in `_validate_attestation`. The generator must reference actual dimension names and cluster names from the plan data.

4. **Stage confirmation flow**: The orchestrator needs to simulate what `_cmd_confirm_stage` does but programmatically. Rather than calling the CLI for confirmation, it can directly call the confirmation functions with synthesized args, or call the CLI as a subprocess (simpler, same pattern as the subagent).

5. **Error recovery**: If a stage fails validation, the orchestrator should leave the plan in whatever state the subagent left it. The user can then fix manually and resume, or re-run `--run-stages` which will detect existing stage progress and continue from where it left off.

6. **Stage reuse**: If observe is already recorded and confirmed, `--run-stages` should skip it and proceed to reflect. Check `stages` dict for existing confirmed stages.
